    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
    5: import { useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
    6: 
    7: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    8: import { getCurriculumCoverage } from "@/services/curriculumCoverageService";
    9: import type { Topic } from "@/types/curriculum";
   10: 
   11: function difficultyLabel(
   12:   difficulty: Topic["difficulty"],
   13: ): string {
   14:   if (difficulty === "â­â­â­") {
   15:     return "Advanced";
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   if (difficulty === "â­â­â˜†") {
   19:     return "Intermediate";
   20:   }
   21: 
   22:   return "Foundation";
   23: }
   24: 
   25: function qualificationLabel(
   26:   qualification: "GCSE" | "A_LEVEL",
   27: ): string {
   28:   return qualification === "A_LEVEL"
   29:     ? "A-level"
   30:     : "GCSE";
   31: }
   32: 
   33: export default function LearnPage() {
   34:   const router = useRouter();
   35: 
   36:   const {
   37:     user,
   38:     profile,
   39:     loading,
   40:     profileReady,
   41:     profileError,
   42:   } = useAuth();
   43: 
   44:   useEffect(() => {
   45:     if (!loading && !user) {
   46:       router.replace("/login");
   47:       return;
   48:     }
   49: 
   50:     if (
   51:       !loading &&
   52:       profileReady &&
   53:       profile?.role === "student" &&
   54:       (!profile.onboardingComplete ||
   55:         !profile.qualification ||
   56:         !profile.examBoard)
   57:     ) {
   58:       router.replace("/onboarding");
   59:     }
   60:   }, [
   61:     loading,
   62:     user,
   63:     profileReady,
   64:     profile,
   65:     router,
   66:   ]);
   67: 
   68:   const coverage = useMemo(() => {
   69:     if (
   70:       !profile?.qualification ||
   71:       !profile.examBoard
   72:     ) {
   73:       return null;
   74:     }
   75: 
   76:     return getCurriculumCoverage(
   77:       profile.qualification,
   78:       profile.examBoard,
   79:     );
   80:   }, [
   81:     profile?.qualification,
   82:     profile?.examBoard,
   83:   ]);
   84: 
   85:   if (
   86:     loading ||
   87:     (user &&
   88:       !profileReady &&
   89:       !profileError)
   90:   ) {
   91:     return (
   92:       <div className="flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center">
   93:         <div className="text-center">
   94:           <div className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-blue-100 border-t-blue-600" />
   95: 
   96:           <p className="mt-4 font-bold text-slate-700">

  391: function Metric({
  392:   label,
  393:   value,
  394:   className,
  395: }: {
  396:   label: string;
  397:   value: number;
  398:   className: string;

  407: 
  408:       <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">
  409:         {value}
  410:       </p>
  411:     </div>
  412:   );

   28: type FirestoreTeacherResource = Omit<
   29:   SavedTeacherResource,
   30:   "id" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"
   31: > & {
   32:   createdAt?: {
   33:     toDate?: () => Date;
   34:   };
   35:   updatedAt?: {
   36:     toDate?: () => Date;
   37:   };
   38: };
   39: 
   40: function convertTimestamp(
   41:   value:
   42:     | FirestoreTeacherResource["createdAt"]
   43:     | FirestoreTeacherResource["updatedAt"],
   44: ): Date | null {
   45:   if (value && typeof value.toDate === "function") {
   46:     return value.toDate();
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   return null;
   50: }
   51: 
   52: function formatResourceType(value: string): string {
   53:   return value
   54:     .replace(/-/g, " ")
   55:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   56: }

   58: function BulletList({

  134: export default function StudentResourcePage() {
  135:   const params = useParams<{
  136:     resourceId: string;
  137:   }>();
  138: 
  139:   const { user } = useAuth();
  140: 
  141:   const resourceId = params.resourceId;
  142: 
  143:   const [resource, setResource] = useState<SavedTeacherResource | null>(null);
  144: 
  145:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  146: 
  147:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  148: 
  149:   const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState(0);
  150: 
  151:   const [showAnswers, setShowAnswers] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  152: 
  153:   const loadResource = useCallback(async () => {
  154:     if (!user?.uid || !resourceId) {
  155:       setResource(null);
  156:       setLoading(false);
  157:       return;
  158:     }
  159: 
  160:     try {
  161:       setLoading(true);
  162:       setError("");
  163: 
  164:       const resourceReference = doc(db, "teacherResources", resourceId);
  165: 
  166:       const snapshot = await getDoc(resourceReference);
  167: 
  168:       if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  169:         setResource(null);
  170:         setError("This teaching resource could not be found.");
  171:         return;
  172:       }
  173: 
  174:       const data = snapshot.data() as FirestoreTeacherResource;
  175: 
  176:       if (data.status !== "published") {
  177:         setResource(null);
  178:         setError("This resource is no longer available to students.");
  179:         return;
  180:       }
  181: 
  182:       setResource({
  183:         id: snapshot.id,
  184:         teacherId: data.teacherId,
  185:         sourceResourceId: data.sourceResourceId,
  186:         title: data.title,
  187:         topic: data.topic,
  188:         resourceType: data.resourceType,
  189:         yearGroup: data.yearGroup,
  190:         examBoard: data.examBoard,
  191:         duration: data.duration,
  192:         difficulty: data.difficulty,
  193:         content: data.content,
  194:         status: data.status,
  195:         createdAt: convertTimestamp(data.createdAt),
  196:         updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),
  197:       });
  198:     } catch (caughtError) {
  199:       console.error("Unable to load student resource:", caughtError);
  200: 
  201:       setResource(null);
  202: 
  203:       setError(
  204:         caughtError instanceof Error
  205:           ? caughtError.message
  206:           : "The teaching resource could not be loaded.",
  207:       );
  208:     } finally {
  209:       setLoading(false);
  210:     }
  211:   }, [resourceId, user?.uid]);
  212: 
  213:   useEffect(() => {
  214:     void loadResource();
  215:   }, [loadResource]);
  216: 
  217:   const sectionCount = resource?.content.sections.length ?? 0;
  218: 
  219:   const currentSection = useMemo(() => {
  220:     if (!resource || sectionCount === 0) {
  221:       return null;
  222:     }
  223: 
  224:     return resource.content.sections[activeSection];
  225:   }, [activeSection, resource, sectionCount]);
  226: 
  227:   function goToPreviousSection() {
  228:     setActiveSection((current) => Math.max(current - 1, 0));
  229: 
  230:     window.scrollTo({
  231:       top: 0,
  232:       behavior: "smooth",
  233:     });
  234:   }
  235: 
  236:   function goToNextSection() {
  237:     setActiveSection((current) => Math.min(current + 1, sectionCount - 1));
  238: 
  239:     window.scrollTo({
  240:       top: 0,
  241:       behavior: "smooth",
  242:     });
  243:   }
  244: 
  245:   function toggleAnswer(index: number) {
  246:     setShowAnswers((current) => ({
  247:       ...current,
  248:       [index]: !current[index],
  249:     }));
  250:   }
  251: 
  252:   if (loading) {
  253:     return <ResourceSkeleton />;
  254:   }
  255: 
  256:   if (!resource || error) {
  257:     return (
  258:       <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  259:         <Link
  260:           href="/assignments"
  261:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
  262:         >
  263:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  264:           Back to assignments
  265:         </Link>
  266: 
  267:         <section className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  268:           <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-red-100 text-red-600">
  269:             <AlertCircle className="h-7 w-7" />
  270:           </div>
  271: 
  272:           <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  273:             Resource unavailable
  274:           </h1>
  275: 
  276:           <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  277:             {error || "This teaching resource could not be found."}
  278:           </p>
  279: 
  280:           <button
  281:             type="button"
  282:             onClick={() => {
  283:               void loadResource();
  284:             }}
  285:             className="mt-6 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-red-700"
  286:           >
  287:             Try again
  288:           </button>
  289:         </section>
  290:       </main>
  291:     );
  292:   }
  293:

  295: 
  296:   return (
  297:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-7 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  298:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  299:         <Link
  300:           href="/assignments"
  301:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
  302:         >
  303:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  304:           Back to assignment

  310:           </p>

  312:       </div>

  315:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 xl:flex-row xl:items-start xl:justify-between">
  316:           <div className="max-w-4xl">

  318:               <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur">
  319:                 {formatResourceType(resource.resourceType)}
  320:               </span>

  323:                 {resource.yearGroup}
  324:               </span>

FILE: app\revision-plan\page.tsx
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: import Link from "next/link";
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
    5: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
    6: import RevisionPlanCard from "@/components/revision-plan/RevisionPlanCard";
    7: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    8: import { getStudentInterventions } from "@/services/interventionService";
    9: import type { Intervention } from "@/types/intervention";
   10: export default function RevisionPlanPage() {
   11:   const { user } = useAuth();
   12:   const [items, setItems] = useState<Intervention[]>([]);
   13:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   14:   async function load() {
   15:     if (!user?.uid) return;
   16:     setLoading(true);
   17:     try {
   18:       setItems(await getStudentInterventions(user.uid));
   19:     } finally {
   20:       setLoading(false);
   21:     }
   22:   }
   23:   useEffect(() => {
   24:     void load();
   25:   }, [user?.uid]);
   26:   if (loading)
   27:     return (
   28:       <div className="space-y-6">
   29:         <Skeleton className="h-48" />
   30:         <Skeleton className="h-96" />
   31:       </div>
   32:     );
   33:   const active = items.filter((i) => i.status === "active");
   34:   const completed = items.filter((i) => i.status === "completed");
   35:   return (
   36:     <div className="space-y-8">
   37:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-900 to-teal-800 text-white">
   38:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row md:justify-between">
   39:           <div>
   40:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase text-teal-100">
   41:               Personalised learning
   42:             </p>
   43:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">Revision Plan</h1>
   44:             <p className="mt-3 text-teal-100">
   45:               Complete your targeted lesson, quiz and exam-practice steps.
   46:             </p>
   47:           </div>
   48:           <div className="flex gap-3">
   49:             <Link
   50:               href="/assignments"
   51:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 font-bold"
   52:             >
   53:               Assignments
   54:             </Link>
   55:             <Link
   56:               href="/dashboard"
   57:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 font-bold"
   58:             >
   59:               Dashboard
   60:             </Link>
   61:           </div>
   62:         </div>
   63:       </Card>
   64:       {active.length ? (
   65:         <div className="space-y-6">
   66:           {active.map((i) => (
   67:             <RevisionPlanCard
   68:               key={i.id}
   69:               intervention={i}
   70:               studentId={user?.uid || ""}
   71:               onUpdated={() => void load()}
   72:             />
   73:           ))}
   74:         </div>
   75:       ) : (
   76:         <Card>
   77:           <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">No active revision plan</h2>
   78:           <p className="mt-3 text-slate-600">
   79:             Teacher-created interventions will appear here.
   80:           </p>
   81:         </Card>
   82:       )}
   83:       {completed.length > 0 && (
   84:         <Card>
   85:           <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Completed plans</h2>
   86:           <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
   87:             {completed.map((i) => (
   88:               <div key={i.id} className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 p-5">
   89:                 <p className="font-black text-emerald-950">{i.title}</p>
   90:                 <p className="text-sm text-emerald-800">
   91:                   Impact {i.impact >= 0 ? "+" : ""}
   92:                   {i.impact}%
   93:                 </p>
   94:               </div>
   95:             ))}
   96:           </div>
   97:         </Card>
   98:       )}
   99:     </div>
  100:   );
  101: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";

    5:   useEffect,
    6:   useMemo,
    7:   useState,
    8:   type ReactNode,
    9: } from "react";
   10: import {
   11:   AlertTriangle,
   12:   ArrowRight,
   13:   BarChart3,
   14:   BookOpenCheck,
   15:   CheckCircle2,
   16:   ClipboardList,
   17:   Gauge,
   18:   Search,
   19:   Target,
   20:   TrendingDown,
   21:   TrendingUp,
   22:   Users,
   23: } from "lucide-react";
   24: 
   25: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   26: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   27: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   28: 
   29: import {
   30:   getTeacherAnalyticsActionCentre,
   31:   type TeacherActionClass,
   32:   type TeacherActionPriority,
   33:   type TeacherActionStudent,
   34:   type TeacherAnalyticsActionCentre,
   35: } from "@/services/analytics/teacherActionCentreService";
   36: 
   37: const emptyActionCentre: TeacherAnalyticsActionCentre = {
   38:   teacherId: "",
   39:   classCount: 0,
   40:   studentCount: 0,
   41:   studentsWithEvidence: 0,
   42:   studentsWithoutEvidence: 0,
   43:   highPriorityCount: 0,
   44:   decliningCount: 0,
   45:   lowEvidenceCount: 0,
   46:   studentsWithTargets: 0,
   47:   studentsBelowTarget: 0,
   48:   studentsOnOrAboveTarget: 0,
   49:   studentsWithoutTargets: 0,
   50:   averageCompletionRate: 0,
   51:   activeInterventions: 0,
   52:   completedInterventions: 0,
   53:   priorityStudents: [],
   54:   classes: [],
   55:   priorityTopics: [],
   56:   strongestTopics: [],
   57: };
   58: 
   59: function priorityTone(priority: TeacherActionPriority): string {
   60:   if (priority === "high") {
   61:     return "bg-red-100 text-red-800";
   62:   }
   63: 
   64:   if (priority === "medium") {
   65:     return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800";
   66:   }
   67: 
   68:   if (priority === "monitor") {
   69:     return "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800";
   70:   }
   71: 
   72:   return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";
   73: }
   74: 
   75: function priorityLabel(priority: TeacherActionPriority): string {
   76:   if (priority === "high") return "High";
   77:   if (priority === "medium") return "Medium";
   78:   if (priority === "monitor") return "Monitor";
   79:   return "No flag";
   80: }
   81: 
   82: function gradeGapLabel(value: number | null): string {
   83:   if (value === null) {
   84:     return "Target not set";
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   if (value > 0) {
   88:     return `+${value} above target`;
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   if (value === 0) {
   92:     return "On target";
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   return `${Math.abs(value)} below target`;
   96: }
   97: 
   98: function confidenceLabel(value: string): string {
   99:   if (value === "insufficient") return "Insufficient";
  100:   if (value === "low") return "Low";
  101:   if (value === "medium") return "Medium";
  102:   if (value === "high") return "High";
  103:   return value || "Unknown";
  104: }
  105: 
  106: export default function TeacherAnalyticsPage() {
  107:   const {
  108:     user,
  109:     profile,
  110:     loading: authLoading,
  111:     profileReady,
  112:   } = useAuth();
  113: 
  114:   const [centre, setCentre] =
  115:     useState<TeacherAnalyticsActionCentre>(emptyActionCentre);
  116: 
  117:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  118:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  119:   const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  120:   const [classFilter, setClassFilter] = useState("all");
  121:   const [priorityFilter, setPriorityFilter] = useState("all");
  122: 
  123:   useEffect(() => {
  124:     if (authLoading || !profileReady) {
  125:       return;
  126:     }
  127: 
  128:     if (!user?.uid) {
  129:       setCentre(emptyActionCentre);
  130:       setLoading(false);
  131:       return;
  132:     }
  133: 
  134:     const teacherId: string = user.uid;
  135: 
  136:     let cancelled = false;
  137: 
  138:     async function load() {
  139:       try {
  140:         setLoading(true);
  141:         setError("");
  142: 
  143:         const loaded =
  144:           await getTeacherAnalyticsActionCentre(
  145:             teacherId,
  146:           );
  147: 
  148:         if (!cancelled) {
  149:           setCentre(loaded);
  150:         }
  151:       } catch (caughtError) {
  152:         console.error(
  153:           "Unable to load teacher analytics action centre:",
  154:           caughtError,
  155:         );
  156: 
  157:         if (!cancelled) {
  158:           setCentre(emptyActionCentre);
  159:           setError(
  160:             caughtError instanceof Error
  161:               ? caughtError.message
  162:               : "Teacher analytics could not be loaded.",
  163:           );
  164:         }
  165:       } finally {
  166:         if (!cancelled) {
  167:           setLoading(false);
  168:         }
  169:       }
  170:     }
  171: 
  172:     void load();
  173: 
  174:     return () => {
  175:       cancelled = true;
  176:     };
  177:   }, [
  178:     authLoading,
  179:     profileReady,
  180:     user?.uid,
  181:   ]);
  182: 
  183:   const filteredStudents = useMemo(() => {
  184:     const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();
  185: 
  186:     return centre.priorityStudents.filter((student) => {
  187:       const matchesSearch =
  188:         !term ||
  189:         student.studentName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
  190:         student.studentEmail.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
  191:         student.className.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
  192:         student.weakestTopic.toLowerCase().includes(term);
  193: 
  194:       const matchesClass =
  195:         classFilter === "all" || student.classId === classFilter;
  196: 
  197:       const matchesPriority =
  198:         priorityFilter === "all" || student.priority === priorityFilter;
  199: 
  200:       return matchesSearch && matchesClass && matchesPriority;
  201:     });
  202:   }, [centre.priorityStudents, search, classFilter, priorityFilter]);
  203: 
  204:   if (authLoading || !profileReady || loading) {
  205:     return (
  206:       <div className="space-y-8">
  207:         <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  208:         <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  209:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  210:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  211:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  212:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  213:         </div>
  214:         <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  215:       </div>
  216:     );
  217:   }
  218: 
  219:   if (profile?.role !== "teacher" && profile?.role !== "admin") {
  220:     return (
  221:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-7">
  222:         <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  223:           Teacher access required
  224:         </h1>
  225:       </Card>
  226:     );
  227:   }
  228: 
  229:   return (
  230:     <div className="space-y-8">
  231:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-950 via-indigo-950 to-violet-900 p-7 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-9">
  232:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
  233:           <div>
  234:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-violet-200">
  235:               T1D Â· Teacher intelligence
  236:             </p>
  237: 
  238:             <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
  239:               Analytics & Action Centre
  240:             </h1>
  241: 
  242:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-7 text-violet-100">
  243:               Turn class attainment, completion, topic mastery and evidence quality
  244:               into a prioritised teacher action list.
  245:             </p>
  246:           </div>
  247: 
  248:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  249:             <Link
  250:               href="/teacher/interventions"
  251:               className="rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-black text-violet-950 transition hover:bg-violet-50"
  252:             >
  253:               Intervention Centre
  254:             </Link>
  255: 
  256:             <Link
  257:               href="/teacher/reports"
  258:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/25 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-white/20"
  259:             >
  260:               Reports
  261:             </Link>
  262:           </div>
  263:         </div>
  264:       </section>
  265: 
  266:       {error && (
  267:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-6">
  268:           <p className="font-bold text-red-900">{error}</p>
  269:         </Card>
  270:       )}
  271: 
  272:       <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  273:         <SummaryCard
  274:           label="Students"
  275:           value={centre.studentCount}
  276:           description={`${centre.classCount} active class${centre.classCount === 1 ? "" : "es"}`}
  277:           icon={<Users className="h-5 w-5" />}
  278:         />
  279: 
  280:         <SummaryCard
  281:           label="Completion"
  282:           value={`${centre.averageCompletionRate}%`}
  283:           description="Average assignment completion"
  284:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
  285:         />
  286: 
  287:         <SummaryCard
  288:           label="High priority"
  289:           value={centre.highPriorityCount}
  290:           description={`${centre.decliningCount} declining learner${centre.decliningCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}`}
  291:           icon={<AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5" />}
  292:         />
  293: 
  294:         <SummaryCard
  295:           label="Interventions"
  296:           value={centre.activeInterventions}
  297:           description={`${centre.completedInterventions} completed`}
  298:           icon={<ClipboardList className="h-5 w-5" />}
  299:         />
  300:       </div>
  301: 
  302:       <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[1.35fr_.65fr]">
  303:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 sm:p-7">
  304:           <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
  305:             <div>
  306:               <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-red-600">
  307:                 Action queue
  308:               </p>
  309:               <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  310:                 Students needing attention
  311:               </h2>
  312:               <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
  313:                 Priority combines target gap, trend, completion and evidence confidence.
  314:               </p>
  315:             </div>
  316: 
  317:             <Link
  318:               href="/teacher/interventions"
  319:               className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-violet-700"
  320:             >
  321:               Plan interventions
  322:               <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  323:             </Link>
  324:           </div>
  325: 
  326:           <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 lg:grid-cols-[1.4fr_.8fr_.8fr]">
  327:             <label className="relative block">
  328:               <span className="sr-only">Search students</span>
  329:               <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  330:               <input
  331:                 type="search"
  332:                 value={search}
  333:                 onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
  334:                 placeholder="Search student, class or topic..."
  335:                 className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 py-3 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm outline-none focus:border-violet-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-violet-100"
  336:               />
  337:             </label>
  338: 
  339:             <select
  340:               value={classFilter}
  341:               onChange={(event) => setClassFilter(event.target.value)}
  342:               className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
  343:             >
  344:               <option value="all">All classes</option>
  345:               {centre.classes.map((classItem) => (
  346:                 <option key={classItem.classId} value={classItem.classId}>
  347:                   {classItem.className}
  348:                 </option>
  349:               ))}
  350:             </select>
  351: 
  352:             <select
  353:               value={priorityFilter}
  354:               onChange={(event) => setPriorityFilter(event.target.value)}
  355:               className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
  356:             >
  357:               <option value="all">All priorities</option>
  358:               <option value="high">High</option>
  359:               <option value="medium">Medium</option>
  360:               <option value="monitor">Monitor</option>
  361:               <option value="none">No flag</option>
  362:             </select>
  363:           </div>
  364: 
  365:           {filteredStudents.length === 0 ? (
  366:             <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 p-10 text-center">
  367:               <CheckCircle2 className="mx-auto h-9 w-9 text-emerald-500" />
  368:               <p className="mt-3 font-black text-slate-900">
  369:                 No students match this action filter
  370:               </p>
  371:               <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  372:                 Adjust the filters or review the wider class evidence below.
  373:               </p>
  374:             </div>
  375:           ) : (
  376:             <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
  377:               {filteredStudents.slice(0, 12).map((student) => (
  378:                 <PriorityStudentCard key={student.key} student={student} />
  379:               ))}
  380:             </div>
  381:           )}
  382:         </Card>
  383: 
  384:         <div className="space-y-6">
  385:           <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-6">
  386:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-amber-700">
  387:               Evidence quality
  388:             </p>
  389: 
  390:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-amber-950">
  391:               {centre.studentsWithEvidence}/{centre.studentCount} learners with graded evidence
  392:             </h2>
  393: 
  394:             <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
  395:               <EvidenceRow
  396:                 label="Without graded evidence"
  397:                 value={centre.studentsWithoutEvidence}
  398:               />
  399:               <EvidenceRow
  400:                 label="Low / insufficient confidence"
  401:                 value={centre.lowEvidenceCount}
  402:               />
  403:               <EvidenceRow
  404:                 label="Targets not set"
  405:                 value={centre.studentsWithoutTargets}
  406:               />
  407:             </div>
  408: 
  409:             <p className="mt-5 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-800">
  410:               Treat low-confidence attainment cautiously until more assessed evidence is available.
  411:             </p>
  412:           </Card>
  413: 
  414:           <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6">
  415:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-700">
  416:               Target position
  417:             </p>
  418: 
  419:             <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
  420:               <MiniMetric
  421:                 label="On / above"
  422:                 value={centre.studentsOnOrAboveTarget}
  423:               />
  424:               <MiniMetric
  425:                 label="Below target"
  426:                 value={centre.studentsBelowTarget}
  427:               />
  428:             </div>
  429: 
  430:             <Link
  431:               href="/teacher/students"
  432:               className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-blue-700"
  433:             >
  434:               Open student directory
  435:               <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  436:             </Link>
  437:           </Card>
  438:         </div>
  439:       </section>
  440: 
  441:       <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  442:         <TopicPanel
  443:           title="Priority curriculum areas"
  444:           subtitle="Lowest current weighted mastery across your active classes."
  445:           topics={centre.priorityTopics}
  446:           positive={false}
  447:         />
  448: 
  449:         <TopicPanel
  450:           title="Strongest curriculum areas"
  451:           subtitle="Topics currently showing the strongest assessed mastery."
  452:           topics={centre.strongestTopics}
  453:           positive
  454:         />
  455:       </section>
  456: 
  457:       <section>
  458:         <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
  459:           <div>
  460:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-teal-600">
  461:               Class intelligence
  462:             </p>
  463:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  464:               Performance by teaching group
  465:             </h2>
  466:           </div>
  467: 
  468:           <Link
  469:             href="/teacher/knowledge-map"
  470:             className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-teal-700"
  471:           >
  472:             Class Knowledge Map
  473:             <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  474:           </Link>
  475:         </div>
  476: 
  477:         {centre.classes.length === 0 ? (
  478:           <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 p-10 text-center">
  479:             <p className="font-black text-slate-900">No active classes yet.</p>
  480:           </Card>
  481:         ) : (
  482:           <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  483:             {centre.classes.map((classItem) => (
  484:               <ClassIntelligenceCard
  485:                 key={classItem.classId}
  486:                 classItem={classItem}
  487:               />
  488:             ))}
  489:           </div>
  490:         )}
  491:       </section>
  492: 
  493:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 p-6">
  494:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  495:           <div>
  496:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-violet-700">
  497:               Next teacher action
  498:             </p>
  499:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-violet-950">
  500:               Move from diagnosis to targeted support
  501:             </h2>
  502:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-violet-800">
  503:               Use the Intervention Centre to convert flagged students and weak topics into measurable support plans.
  504:             </p>
  505:           </div>
  506: 
  507:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  508:             <Link
  509:               href="/teacher/interventions"
  510:               className="rounded-xl bg-violet-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-black text-white"
  511:             >
  512:               Open Intervention Centre
  513:             </Link>
  514: 
  515:             <Link
  516:               href="/teacher/reports"
  517:               className="rounded-xl border border-violet-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-black text-violet-800"
  518:             >
  519:               Generate reports
  520:             </Link>
  521:           </div>
  522:         </div>
  523:       </Card>
  524:     </div>
  525:   );
  526: }
  527: 
  528: function SummaryCard({
  529:   label,
  530:   value,
  531:   description,
  532:   icon,
  533: }: {
  534:   label: string;
  535:   value: number | string;
  536:   description: string;
  537:   icon: ReactNode;
  538: }) {
  539:   return (
  540:     <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  541:       <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  542:         <div>
  543:           <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">{label}</p>
  544:           <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  545:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{description}</p>
  546:         </div>
  547:         <div className="rounded-2xl bg-violet-50 p-3 text-violet-700">
  548:           {icon}
  549:         </div>
  550:       </div>
  551:     </Card>
  552:   );
  553: }
  554: 
  555: function PriorityStudentCard({
  556:   student,
  557: }: {
  558:   student: TeacherActionStudent;
  559: }) {
  560:   return (
  561:     <article className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
  562:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
  563:         <div className="min-w-0">
  564:           <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  565:             <h3 className="font-black text-slate-950">{student.studentName}</h3>
  566:             <span className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black ${priorityTone(student.priority)}`}>
  567:               {priorityLabel(student.priority)} priority
  568:             </span>
  569:             <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">
  570:               {student.className}
  571:             </span>
  572:           </div>
  573: 
  574:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{student.studentEmail}</p>
  575: 
  576:           <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  577:             <StudentMetric label="Working" value={student.workingGrade} />
  578:             <StudentMetric label="Target" value={student.targetGrade} />
  579:             <StudentMetric label="Completion" value={`${student.completionRate}%`} />
  580:             <StudentMetric label="Confidence" value={confidenceLabel(student.confidence)} />
  581:           </div>
  582: 
  583:           <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs font-bold">
  584:             <span className={student.gradeGap !== null && student.gradeGap < 0 ? "text-red-700" : "text-emerald-700"}>
  585:               {gradeGapLabel(student.gradeGap)}
  586:             </span>
  587: 
  588:             {student.trend === "declining" && (
  589:               <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-red-700">
  590:                 <TrendingDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  591:                 Declining trend
  592:               </span>
  593:             )}
  594: 
  595:             <span className="text-slate-600">
  596:               Weakest: {student.weakestTopic}
  597:               {student.weakestTopicPercentage === null
  598:                 ? ""
  599:                 : ` (${student.weakestTopicPercentage}%)`}
  600:             </span>
  601:           </div>
  602: 
  603:           {student.reasons.length > 0 && (
  604:             <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  605:               {student.reasons.slice(0, 2).join(" Â· ")}
  606:             </p>
  607:           )}
  608:         </div>
  609: 
  610:         <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap gap-2">
  611:           <Link
  612:             href={`/teacher/analytics/${student.studentId}`}
  613:             className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-black text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
  614:           >
  615:             Analytics
  616:           </Link>
  617: 
  618:           <Link
  619:             href={`/teacher/interventions?studentId=${encodeURIComponent(
  620:               student.studentId,
  621:             )}&studentName=${encodeURIComponent(
  622:               student.studentName,
  623:             )}&className=${encodeURIComponent(
  624:               student.className,
  625:             )}&topic=${encodeURIComponent(
  626:               student.weakestTopic,
  627:             )}`}
  628:             className="rounded-xl bg-violet-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-black text-white"
  629:           >
  630:             Intervention
  631:           </Link>
  632:         </div>
  633:       </div>
  634:     </article>
  635:   );
  636: }
  637: 
  638: function StudentMetric({
  639:   label,
  640:   value,
  641: }: {
  642:   label: string;
  643:   value: string;
  644: }) {
  645:   return (
  646:     <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-3 py-3">
  647:       <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  648:       <p className="mt-1 font-black text-slate-900">{value}</p>
  649:     </div>
  650:   );
  651: }
  652: 
  653: function EvidenceRow({
  654:   label,
  655:   value,
  656: }: {
  657:   label: string;
  658:   value: number;
  659: }) {
  660:   return (
  661:     <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-white/70 px-4 py-3">
  662:       <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-900">{label}</span>
  663:       <span className="font-black text-amber-950">{value}</span>
  664:     </div>
  665:   );
  666: }
  667: 
  668: function MiniMetric({
  669:   label,
  670:   value,
  671: }: {
  672:   label: string;
  673:   value: number;
  674: }) {
  675:   return (
  676:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/80 p-4">
  677:       <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-500">{label}</p>
  678:       <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-blue-950">{value}</p>
  679:     </div>
  680:   );
  681: }
  682: 
  683: function TopicPanel({
  684:   title,
  685:   subtitle,
  686:   topics,
  687:   positive,
  688: }: {
  689:   title: string;
  690:   subtitle: string;
  691:   topics: TeacherAnalyticsActionCentre["priorityTopics"];
  692:   positive: boolean;
  693: }) {
  694:   return (
  695:     <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  696:       <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
  697:         <div className={`rounded-2xl p-3 ${positive ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700" : "bg-red-50 text-red-700"}`}>
  698:           {positive ? <TrendingUp className="h-5 w-5" /> : <Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
  699:         </div>
  700: 
  701:         <div>
  702:           <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{title}</h2>
  703:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p>
  704:         </div>
  705:       </div>
  706: 
  707:       {topics.length === 0 ? (
  708:         <p className="mt-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-5 text-sm text-slate-500">
  709:           More assessed evidence is needed before topic patterns can be shown.
  710:         </p>
  711:       ) : (
  712:         <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
  713:           {topics.map((topic) => (
  714:             <div key={topic.topic} className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  715:               <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  716:                 <div>
  717:                   <p className="font-black text-slate-900">{topic.topic}</p>
  718:                   <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
  719:                     {topic.evidenceCount} evidence item{topic.evidenceCount === 1 ? "" : "s"} Â· {topic.studentCount} learner{topic.studentCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}
  720:                   </p>
  721:                 </div>
  722:                 <p className={`text-xl font-black ${positive ? "text-emerald-700" : "text-red-700"}`}>
  723:                   {topic.weightedPercentage}%
  724:                 </p>
  725:               </div>
  726: 
  727:               <div className="mt-3 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  728:                 <div
  729:                   className="h-full rounded-full bg-current"
  730:                   style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, topic.weightedPercentage))}%` }}
  731:                 />
  732:               </div>
  733:             </div>
  734:           ))}
  735:         </div>
  736:       )}
  737:     </Card>
  738:   );
  739: }
  740: 
  741: function ClassIntelligenceCard({
  742:   classItem,
  743: }: {
  744:   classItem: TeacherActionClass;
  745: }) {
  746:   const coverage =
  747:     classItem.studentCount > 0
  748:       ? Math.round((classItem.studentsWithEvidence / classItem.studentCount) * 100)
  749:       : 0;
  750: 
  751:   return (
  752:     <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  753:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
  754:         <div>
  755:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-teal-600">
  756:             {classItem.yearGroup || "Teaching group"}
  757:           </p>
  758:           <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{classItem.className}</h3>
  759:         </div>
  760: 
  761:         <Link
  762:           href={`/teacher/classes/${classItem.classId}`}
  763:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black text-blue-700"
  764:         >
  765:           Open class
  766:           <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
  767:         </Link>
  768:       </div>
  769: 
  770:       <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
  771:         <ClassMetric label="Working" value={classItem.averageWorkingGrade} icon={<Gauge className="h-4 w-4" />} />
  772:         <ClassMetric label="Target" value={classItem.averageTargetGrade} icon={<Target className="h-4 w-4" />} />
  773:         <ClassMetric label="Completion" value={`${classItem.averageCompletionRate}%`} icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />} />
  774:         <ClassMetric label="On / above target" value={`${classItem.onOrAboveTargetPercentage}%`} icon={<BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4" />} />
  775:       </div>
  776: 
  777:       <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
  778:         <RiskMetric label="High priority" value={classItem.highPriorityCount} tone="red" />
  779:         <RiskMetric label="Declining" value={classItem.decliningCount} tone="amber" />
  780:         <RiskMetric label="Low evidence" value={classItem.lowEvidenceCount} tone="blue" />
  781:       </div>
  782: 
  783:       <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  784:         <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
  785:           <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">Evidence coverage</p>
  786:           <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-950">{coverage}%</p>
  787:         </div>
  788:         <div className="mt-2 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  789:           <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-teal-600" style={{ width: `${coverage}%` }} />
  790:         </div>
  791:       </div>
  792: 
  793:       {classItem.priorityTopics.length > 0 && (
  794:         <div className="mt-5">
  795:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  796:             Priority topics
  797:           </p>
  798:           <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  799:             {classItem.priorityTopics.slice(0, 3).map((topic) => (
  800:               <span key={topic.topic} className="rounded-full bg-red-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-red-700">
  801:                 {topic.topic} Â· {topic.weightedPercentage}%
  802:               </span>
  803:             ))}
  804:           </div>
  805:         </div>
  806:       )}
  807:     </Card>
  808:   );
  809: }
  810: 
  811: function ClassMetric({
  812:   label,
  813:   value,
  814:   icon,
  815: }: {
  816:   label: string;
  817:   value: string;
  818:   icon: ReactNode;
  819: }) {
  820:   return (
  821:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  822:       <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  823:         {icon}
  824:         {label}
  825:       </p>
  826:       <p className="mt-2 font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  827:     </div>
  828:   );
  829: }
  830: 
  831: function RiskMetric({
  832:   label,
  833:   value,
  834:   tone,
  835: }: {
  836:   label: string;
  837:   value: number;
  838:   tone: "red" | "amber" | "blue";
  839: }) {
  840:   const style =
  841:     tone === "red"
  842:       ? "bg-red-50 text-red-800"
  843:       : tone === "amber"
  844:         ? "bg-amber-50 text-amber-800"
  845:         : "bg-blue-50 text-blue-800";
  846: 
  847:   return (
  848:     <div className={`rounded-2xl p-4 ${style}`}>
  849:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</p>
  850:       <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{value}</p>
  851:     </div>
  852:   );
  853: }

   54: function formatDate(value: Date | null, includeTime = false): string {
   55:   if (!value) {
   56:     return "â€”";
   57:   }
   58: 
   59:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   60:     "en-GB",
   61:     includeTime
   62:       ? {
   63:           day: "2-digit",
   64:           month: "short",
   65:           year: "numeric",
   66:           hour: "2-digit",
   67:           minute: "2-digit",
   68:         }
   69:       : {
   70:           day: "2-digit",
   71:           month: "long",
   72:           year: "numeric",
   73:         },
   74:   ).format(value);
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function formatResourceType(value: string): string {
   78:   return value
   79:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   80:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   81: }
   82: 
   83: function formatStatus(status: StudentAssignmentStatus): string {
   84:   if (status === "in_progress") {
   85:     return "In Progress";
   86:   }
   87: 
   88:   if (status === "completed") {
   89:     return "Completed";
   90:   }
   91: 
   92:   return "Not Started";
   93: }
   94: 
   95: function escapeCsvValue(value: string): string {
   96:   return `"${value.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
   97: }
   98: 
   99: function getDueDateInformation(dueDate: Date | null): {
  100:   label: string;
  101:   overdue: boolean;
  102: } {
  103:   if (!dueDate) {
  104:     return {
  105:       label: "No due date",
  106:       overdue: false,
  107:     };
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   const today = new Date();
  111:   const due = new Date(dueDate);
  112: 
  113:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  114:   due.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  115: 
  116:   const differenceInDays = Math.ceil(
  117:     (due.getTime() - today.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24),
  118:   );
  119: 
  120:   if (differenceInDays < 0) {
  121:     const overdueDays = Math.abs(differenceInDays);
  122: 
  123:     return {
  124:       label: `${overdueDays} ${overdueDays === 1 ? "day" : "days"} overdue`,
  125:       overdue: true,
  126:     };
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   if (differenceInDays === 0) {
  130:     return {
  131:       label: "Due today",
  132:       overdue: false,
  133:     };
  134:   }
  135: 
  136:   if (differenceInDays === 1) {
  137:     return {
  138:       label: "Due tomorrow",
  139:       overdue: false,
  140:     };
  141:   }
  142: 
  143:   return {
  144:     label: `${differenceInDays} days remaining`,
  145:     overdue: false,
  146:   };
  147: }

  149: export default function TeacherResourceAssignmentPage() {

  166:

  168: 
  169:   const loadAssignment = useCallback(async () => {
  170:     if (!assignmentId || !user?.uid) {

  172:     }
  173: 
  174:     try {
  175:       setLoading(true);
  176:       setError("");
  177: 
  178:       const loadedAssignment = await getAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  179: 
  180:       if (!loadedAssignment) {

  183: 
  184:         setError("This resource assignment could not be found.");
  185:

  190:         setAssignment(null);
  191:         setRows([]);

  193:         setError("You do not have permission to view this assignment.");
  194:

  201:             getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId)),
  202:             getStudentAssignmentProgress(loadedAssignment.id, studentId),

  205:           const profileData = profileSnapshot.exists()

  210:             student: {
  211:               id: studentId,
  212:               name: profileData?.name || "Student",
  213:               email: profileData?.email || "No email available",
  214:             },
  215:             progress,
  216:           } satisfies StudentProgressRow;
  217:         }),
  218:       );
  219: 
  220:       loadedRows.sort((first, second) =>
  221:         first.student.name.localeCompare(second.student.name),
  222:       );
  223: 
  224:       setAssignment(loadedAssignment);
  225:       setRows(loadedRows);
  226:     } catch (caughtError) {
  227:       console.error("Failed to load assignment progress:", caughtError);
  228: 
  229:       setAssignment(null);
  230:       setRows([]);
  231: 
  232:       setError(
  233:         caughtError instanceof Error
  234:           ? caughtError.message
  235:           : "The assignment dashboard could not be loaded.",
  236:       );
  237:     } finally {
  238:       setLoading(false);
  239:     }
  240:   }, [assignmentId, user?.uid]);
  241: 
  242:   useEffect(() => {
  243:     void loadAssignment();
  244:   }, [loadAssignment]);
  245:

  482:             </div>
  483: 
  484:             <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-emerald-100">
  485:               {completedRows.length} of {rows.length} students completed

  499:                 type="button"
  500:                 onClick={exportCsv}

  506:                 ) : (

  508:                 )}
  509:                 Export CSV
  510:               </button>
  511:             </div>
  512:           </div>
  513:         </div>

  515: 
  516:       <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  517:         <SummaryCard
  518:           label="Total Students"
  519:           value={rows.length}
  520:           description="Assigned students"
  521:           icon={<Users className="h-6 w-6" />}
  522:           iconClassName="bg-blue-50 text-blue-600"
  523:         />

  526:           label="Completed"
  527:           value={completedRows.length}

  529:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6" />}

  531:         />

  534:           label="In Progress"

  536:           description="Currently working"

  538:           iconClassName="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600"
  539:         />
  540:

  542:           label="Not Started"
  543:           value={notStartedRows.length}
  544:           description="May need support"
  545:           icon={<XCircle className="h-6 w-6" />}

  547:         />
  548:       </div>

  551:         <div className="border-b border-slate-200 p-6 sm:p-7">
  552:           <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 xl:flex-row xl:items-end xl:justify-between">

  555:                 Student progress

  577:                 value={statusFilter}
  578:                 onChange={(event) =>

  580:                 }

  582:               >
  583:                 <option value="all">All statuses</option>
  584:                 <option value="completed">Completed</option>
  585:                 <option value="in_progress">In progress</option>
  586:                 <option value="not_started">Not started</option>
  587:               </select>
  588:             </div>
  589:           </div>
  590:         </div>
  591: 
  592:         {rows.length === 0 ? (
  593:           <EmptyState
  594:             title="No students assigned"
  595:             description="No students are currently assigned to this resource."
  596:           />
  597:         ) : filteredRows.length === 0 ? (
  598:           <EmptyState
  599:             title="No matching students"
  600:             description="Try changing your search or status filter."
  601:           />
  602:         ) : (

  604:             <table className="min-w-full">
  605:               <thead className="bg-slate-50">
  606:                 <tr className="text-left text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  607:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Student</th>
  608:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Status</th>
  609:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Started</th>
  610:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Completed</th>
  611:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Last activity</th>
  612:                 </tr>
  613:               </thead>
  614: 
  615:               <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
  616:                 {filteredRows.map(({ student, progress }) => (

  618:                     <td className="px-6 py-5">
  619:                       <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
  620:                         <StudentAvatar name={student.name} />
  621: 
  622:                         <div>
  623:                           <p className="font-bold text-slate-950">
  624:                             {student.name}
  625:                           </p>
  626: 
  627:                           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  628:                             {student.email}
  629:                           </p>
  630:                         </div>
  631:                       </div>
  632:                     </td>

  635:                       <StatusBadge status={progress.status} />
  636:                     </td>

  639:                       {formatDate(progress.startedAt, true)}
  640:                     </td>

  644:                     </td>

  648:                     </td>
  649:                   </tr>

  651:               </tbody>
  652:             </table>
  653:           </div>

  655:

  659:           </div>
  660:         )}
  661:       </Card>
  662:     </div>
  663:   );
  664: }

  666: function SummaryCard({
  667:   label,
  668:   value,
  669:   description,
  670:   icon,
  671:   iconClassName,
  672: }: {
  673:   label: string;

  688:         </div>
  689: 
  690:         <div
  691:           className={`flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl ${iconClassName}`}
  692:         >
  693:           {icon}
  694:         </div>
  695:       </div>
  696:     </Card>
  697:   );
  698: }
  699: 
  700: function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: StudentAssignmentStatus }) {
  701:   if (status === "completed") {
  702:     return (
  703:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
  704:         <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  705:         Completed
  706:       </span>
  707:     );
  708:   }
  709: 
  710:   if (status === "in_progress") {
  711:     return (
  712:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-amber-700">
  713:         <Clock3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  714:         In Progress
  715:       </span>
  716:     );
  717:   }
  718: 
  719:   return (
  720:     <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">
  721:       <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  722:       Not Started
  723:     </span>
  724:   );
  725: }
  726: 
  727: function StudentAvatar({ name }: { name: string }) {
  728:   const initials = name
  729:     .split(" ")
  730:     .filter(Boolean)
  731:     .slice(0, 2)
  732:     .map((part) => part[0])
  733:     .join("")
  734:     .toUpperCase();
  735: 
  736:   return (
  737:     <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-teal-100 text-sm font-black text-teal-700">
  738:       {initials || "ST"}
  739:     </div>
  740:   );
  741: }
  742: 
  743: function EmptyState({
  744:   title,
  745:   description,
  746: }: {
  747:   title: string;
  748:   description: string;
  749: }) {
  750:   return (
  751:     <div className="p-10 text-center">
  752:       <Users className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-slate-400" />
  753: 
  754:       <h3 className="mt-4 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{title}</h3>
  755: 
  756:       <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-lg text-sm text-slate-500">
  757:         {description}
  758:       </p>
  759:     </div>
  760:   );
  761: }

   70: function formatDate(
   71:   value: Date | null,
   72: ): string {
   73:   if (!value) {
   74:     return "No due date";
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   78:     "en-GB",
   79:     {
   80:       day: "numeric",
   81:       month: "short",
   82:       year: "numeric",
   83:     },
   84:   ).format(value);
   85: }
   86: 
   87: function dueLabel(
   88:   assignment:
   89:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
   90: ): string {
   91:   if (!assignment.dueDate) {
   92:     return assignment.status ===
   93:       "active"
   94:       ? "No deadline"
   95:       : "No deadline was set";
   96:   }
   97: 
   98:   if (
   99:     assignment.status ===
  100:     "closed"
  101:   ) {
  102:     return `Closed Â· due date was ${formatDate(
  103:       assignment.dueDate,
  104:     )}`;
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   if (
  108:     assignment.status ===
  109:     "cancelled"
  110:   ) {
  111:     return `Cancelled Â· due date was ${formatDate(
  112:       assignment.dueDate,
  113:     )}`;
  114:   }
  115: 
  116:   if (
  117:     isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
  118:       assignment,
  119:     )
  120:   ) {
  121:     return "Overdue";
  122:   }
  123: 
  124:   const today = new Date();
  125:   const due =
  126:     new Date(
  127:       assignment.dueDate,
  128:     );
  129: 
  130:   today.setHours(
  131:     0,
  132:     0,
  133:     0,
  134:     0,
  135:   );
  136: 
  137:   due.setHours(
  138:     0,
  139:     0,
  140:     0,
  141:     0,
  142:   );
  143: 
  144:   const difference =
  145:     Math.ceil(
  146:       (due.getTime() -
  147:         today.getTime()) /
  148:         (1000 *
  149:           60 *
  150:           60 *
  151:           24),
  152:     );
  153: 
  154:   if (difference === 0) {
  155:     return "Due today";
  156:   }
  157: 
  158:   if (difference === 1) {
  159:     return "Due tomorrow";
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   return `${difference} days remaining`;
  163: }
  164:

  230: function statusLabel(
  231:   status:
  232:     UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  233: ): string {
  234:   if (status === "closed") {
  235:     return "Closed";
  236:   }
  237: 
  238:   if (
  239:     status === "cancelled"
  240:   ) {
  241:     return "Cancelled";
  242:   }
  243: 
  244:   return "Active";
  245: }
  246: 
  247: function statusTone(
  248:   status:
  249:     UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  250: ): string {
  251:   if (status === "closed") {
  252:     return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";
  253:   }
  254: 
  255:   if (
  256:     status === "cancelled"
  257:   ) {
  258:     return "bg-red-50 text-red-700";
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   return "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700";
  262: }
  263: 
  264: function sortAssignments(
  265:   assignments:
  266:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment[],
  267:   sort:
  268:     SortOption,
  269: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment[] {
  270:   const copied =
  271:     [...assignments];
  272: 
  273:   if (
  274:     sort ===
  275:     "completion-high"
  276:   ) {
  277:     return copied.sort(
  278:       (
  279:         first,
  280:         second,
  281:       ) =>
  282:         second.completionPercentage -
  283:         first.completionPercentage,
  284:     );
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   if (
  288:     sort ===
  289:     "completion-low"
  290:   ) {
  291:     return copied.sort(
  292:       (
  293:         first,
  294:         second,
  295:       ) =>
  296:         first.completionPercentage -
  297:         second.completionPercentage,
  298:     );
  299:   }
  300: 
  301:   if (
  302:     sort ===
  303:     "due-soon"
  304:   ) {
  305:     return copied.sort(
  306:       (
  307:         first,
  308:         second,
  309:       ) => {
  310:         if (
  311:           !first.dueDate &&
  312:           !second.dueDate
  313:         ) {
  314:           return 0;
  315:         }
  316: 
  317:         if (!first.dueDate) {
  318:           return 1;
  319:         }
  320: 
  321:         if (!second.dueDate) {
  322:           return -1;
  323:         }
  324: 
  325:         return (
  326:           first.dueDate.getTime() -
  327:           second.dueDate.getTime()
  328:         );
  329:       },
  330:     );
  331:   }
  332: 
  333:   return copied.sort(
  334:     (
  335:       first,
  336:       second,
  337:     ) =>
  338:       (second.createdAt?.getTime() ??
  339:         0) -
  340:       (first.createdAt?.getTime() ??
  341:         0),
  342:   );
  343: }

  429:         ) {
  430:           console.error(
  431:             "Unable to load unified teacher assignments:",
  432:             caughtError,
  433:           );
  434: 
  435:           setSummary(
  436:             emptySummary,
  437:           );
  438: 
  439:           setError(
  440:             caughtError instanceof
  441:               Error
  442:               ? caughtError.message
  443:               : "Your assignments could not be loaded.",
  444:           );
  445:         } finally {
  446:           setLoading(false);
  447:         }
  448:       },
  449:       [user?.uid],
  450:     );
  451: 
  452:   useEffect(() => {
  453:     if (
  454:       authLoading ||
  455:       !profileReady
  456:     ) {
  457:       return;
  458:     }
  459: 
  460:     void loadAssignments();
  461:   }, [
  462:     authLoading,
  463:     profileReady,
  464:     loadAssignments,
  465:   ]);
  466: 
  467:   const classes =
  468:     useMemo(() => {
  469:       const classMap =
  470:         new Map<
  471:           string,
  472:           string
  473:         >();
  474: 
  475:       summary.assignments.forEach(
  476:         (
  477:           assignment,
  478:         ) => {
  479:           if (
  480:             assignment.classId
  481:           ) {
  482:             classMap.set(
  483:               assignment.classId,
  484:               assignment.className ||
  485:                 "Untitled class",
  486:             );
  487:           }
  488:         },
  489:       );
  490: 
  491:       return Array.from(
  492:         classMap.entries(),
  493:       )
  494:         .map(
  495:           ([
  496:             id,
  497:             name,
  498:           ]) => ({
  499:             id,
  500:             name,
  501:           }),
  502:         )
  503:         .sort(
  504:           (
  505:             first,
  506:             second,
  507:           ) =>
  508:             first.name.localeCompare(
  509:               second.name,
  510:               "en-GB",
  511:             ),
  512:         );
  513:     }, [
  514:       summary.assignments,
  515:     ]);
  516: 
  517:   const filteredAssignments =
  518:     useMemo(() => {
  519:       const search =
  520:         searchTerm
  521:           .trim()
  522:           .toLowerCase();
  523: 
  524:       const filtered =
  525:         summary.assignments.filter(
  526:           (
  527:             assignment,
  528:           ) => {
  529:             const matchesType =
  530:               typeFilter ===
  531:                 "all" ||
  532:               assignment.kind ===
  533:                 typeFilter;
  534: 
  535:             const matchesStatus =
  536:               statusFilter ===
  537:                 "all" ||
  538:               (statusFilter ===
  539:               "overdue"
  540:                 ? isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
  541:                     assignment,
  542:                   )
  543:                 : assignment.status ===
  544:                   statusFilter);
  545: 
  546:             const matchesClass =
  547:               classFilter ===
  548:                 "all" ||
  549:               assignment.classId ===
  550:                 classFilter;
  551: 
  552:             const matchesSearch =
  553:               !search ||
  554:               [
  555:                 assignment.title,
  556:                 assignment.topic,
  557:                 assignment.className,
  558:                 assignment.description,
  559:                 kindLabel(
  560:                   assignment.kind,
  561:                 ),
  562:               ].some(
  563:                 (value) =>
  564:                   value
  565:                     .toLowerCase()
  566:                     .includes(
  567:                       search,
  568:                     ),
  569:               );
  570: 
  571:             return (
  572:               matchesType &&
  573:               matchesStatus &&
  574:               matchesClass &&
  575:               matchesSearch
  576:             );
  577:           },
  578:         );
  579: 
  580:       return sortAssignments(
  581:         filtered,
  582:         sortOption,
  583:       );
  584:     }, [
  585:       summary.assignments,
  586:       searchTerm,
  587:       typeFilter,
  588:       statusFilter,
  589:       classFilter,
  590:       sortOption,
  591:     ]);
  592: 
  593:   const completionPercentage =
  594:     summary.totalStudentCount >
  595:     0
  596:       ? Math.round(
  597:           (summary.completedStudentCount /
  598:             summary.totalStudentCount) *
  599:             100,
  600:         )
  601:       : 0;
  602: 
  603:   const filtersActive =
  604:     Boolean(
  605:       searchTerm.trim(),
  606:     ) ||
  607:     typeFilter !== "all" ||
  608:     statusFilter !== "all" ||
  609:     classFilter !== "all" ||
  610:     sortOption !== "newest";
  611: 
  612:   function clearFilters() {
  613:     setSearchTerm("");
  614:     setTypeFilter("all");
  615:     setStatusFilter("all");
  616:     setClassFilter("all");
  617:     setSortOption(
  618:       "newest",
  619:     );
  620:   }
  621: 
  622:   async function changeStatus(
  623:     assignment:
  624:       UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
  625:     status:
  626:       UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  627:   ) {
  628:     try {
  629:       setUpdatingKey(
  630:         assignment.key,
  631:       );
  632: 
  633:       await updateUnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus(
  634:         assignment,
  635:         status,
  636:       );
  637: 
  638:       toast.success(
  639:         status === "active"
  640:           ? "Assignment reopened."
  641:           : status ===
  642:               "closed"
  643:             ? "Assignment closed."
  644:             : "Assignment cancelled.",
  645:       );
  646: 
  647:       await loadAssignments();
  648:     } catch (
  649:       caughtError
  650:     ) {
  651:       console.error(
  652:         "Unable to update assignment status:",
  653:         caughtError,
  654:       );
  655: 
  656:       toast.error(
  657:         caughtError instanceof
  658:           Error
  659:           ? caughtError.message
  660:           : "The assignment status could not be changed.",
  661:       );
  662:     } finally {
  663:       setUpdatingKey("");
  664:     }
  665:   }
  666: 
  667:   if (
  668:     authLoading ||
  669:     !profileReady ||
  670:     loading
  671:   ) {
  672:     return (
  673:       <div className="space-y-8">
  674:         <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  675: 
  676:         <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  677:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  678:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  679:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  680:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  681:         </div>
  682: 
  683:         <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  684:       </div>
  685:     );
  686:   }
  687: 
  688:   if (
  689:     profile?.role !==
  690:       "teacher" &&
  691:     profile?.role !==
  692:       "admin"
  693:   ) {
  694:     return (
  695:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-7">
  696:         <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  697:           Teacher access required
  698:         </h1>
  699:       </Card>
  700:     );
  701:   }
  702: 
  703:   return (
  704:     <div className="space-y-8">
  705:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-700 via-teal-700 to-cyan-700 p-7 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-9">
  706:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
  707:           <div>
  708:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-100">
  709:               T1C Â· Assignment management
  710:             </p>
  711: 
  712:             <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
  713:               Assignment Centre
  714:             </h1>
  715: 
  716:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-emerald-100">
  717:               Manage lessons, programming challenges, quizzes and written exams
  718:               from one teacher workspace.
  719:             </p>
  720:           </div>
  721: 
  722:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  723:             <Link
  724:               href="/teacher/classes"
  725:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/25 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-white/20"
  726:             >
  727:               Classes
  728:             </Link>
  729: 
  730:             <Link
  731:               href="/teacher/assignment-wizard"
  732:               className="rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-black text-teal-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-50"
  733:             >
  734:               + Create assignment
  735:             </Link>
  736:           </div>
  737:         </div>
  738:       </section>
  739: 
  740:       <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">

  742:           label="Assignments"

  744:             summary.totalAssignments
  745:           }

  749:           }
  750:         />
  751: 
  752:         <SummaryCard
  753:           label="Active"

  755:             summary.activeAssignments
  756:           }
  757:           description={`${summary.overdueAssignments} overdue`}

  760:           }
  761:         />
  762:

  764:           label="Completion"

  928:                   .value,

  930:             }
  931:             className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
  932:           >
  933:             <option value="all">
  934:               All classes

  942:                   }

  959:

  969:                     .value as
  970:                     SortOption,
  971:                 )
  972:               }
  973:               className="w-full appearance-none rounded-xl border border-slate-300 py-3 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm"
  974:             >
  975:               <option value="newest">
  976:                 Newest first
  977:               </option>
  978:

  990:             </select>
  991:           </label>
  992:         </div>
  993: 
  994:         <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  995:           <QuickFilter
  996:             active={
  997:               statusFilter ===
  998:               "all"
  999:             }

 1001:               setStatusFilter(
 1002:                 "all",

 1004:             }
 1005:           >
 1006:             All
 1007:           </QuickFilter>

 1010:             active={
 1011:               statusFilter ===
 1012:               "active"
 1013:             }

 1015:               setStatusFilter(
 1016:                 "active",
 1017:               )
 1018:             }
 1019:           >
 1020:             Active
 1021:           </QuickFilter>

 1023:           <QuickFilter

 1027:             }

 1029:               setStatusFilter(
 1030:                 "overdue",
 1031:               )
 1032:             }

 1034:             Overdue (

 1070: 
 1071:       {error ? (

 1123:       ) : (

 1125:           {filteredAssignments.map(

 1130:                 isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(

 1150:                         )}`}

   20: function formatDate(date: Date | null): string {
   21:   if (!date) {
   22:     return "No date";
   23:   }
   24: 
   25:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   26:     day: "numeric",
   27:     month: "short",
   28:     year: "numeric",
   29:   }).format(date);
   30: }
   31: 
   32: 
   33: function qualificationLabel(value: string): string {
   34:   if (value === "A_LEVEL") {
   35:     return "A Level";
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   return value || "Not set";
   39: }
   40: 
   41: function getDueDateStatus(dueDate: Date | null): {
   42:   label: string;
   43:   className: string;
   44: } {
   45:   if (!dueDate) {
   46:     return {
   47:       label: "No due date",
   48:       className: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700",
   49:     };
   50:   }
   51: 
   52:   const today = new Date();
   53:   const due = new Date(dueDate);
   54: 
   55:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   56:   due.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   57: 
   58:   const differenceInMilliseconds = due.getTime() - today.getTime();
   59: 
   60:   const differenceInDays = Math.ceil(
   61:     differenceInMilliseconds / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24),
   62:   );
   63: 
   64:   if (differenceInDays < 0) {
   65:     return {
   66:       label: "Overdue",
   67:       className: "bg-red-100 text-red-700",
   68:     };
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   if (differenceInDays === 0) {
   72:     return {
   73:       label: "Due today",
   74:       className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700",
   75:     };
   76:   }
   77: 
   78:   if (differenceInDays === 1) {
   79:     return {
   80:       label: "Due tomorrow",
   81:       className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700",
   82:     };
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return {
   86:     label: `Due in ${differenceInDays} days`,
   87:     className: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700",
   88:   };
   89: }
   90: 
   91: function calculateCompletionPercentage(
   92:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
   93: ): number {
   94:   return assignment.completionPercentage;
   95: }

  106: 
  107:   const [teacherClass, setTeacherClass] = useState<TeacherClass | null>(null);
  108: 
  109:   const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<UnifiedTeacherAssignment[]>([]);

  114:

  116: 
  117:   useEffect(() => {
  118:     async function loadClassDetails() {
  119:       if (!user?.uid || !classId) {
  120:         return;
  121:       }
  122: 
  123:       try {
  124:         setIsLoading(true);
  125:         setError("");
  126: 
  127:         const classRecord = await getTeacherClassById(classId);
  128: 
  129:         if (!classRecord) {
  130:           setError("This class could not be found.");
  131:           return;
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         if (classRecord.teacherId !== user.uid) {
  135:           setError("You do not have permission to view this class.");
  136:           return;
  137:         }
  138:

  140:           await getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(user.uid);
  141:

  143:           assignmentSummary.assignments.filter(
  144:             (assignment) => assignment.classId === classId,
  145:           );
  146: 
  147:         setTeacherClass(classRecord);
  148:         setAssignments(matchingAssignments);
  149:       } catch (loadError: unknown) {
  150:         const firebaseCode =
  151:           typeof loadError === "object" &&
  152:           loadError !== null &&
  153:           "code" in loadError &&
  154:           typeof (loadError as { code?: unknown }).code === "string"
  155:             ? (loadError as { code: string }).code
  156:             : "";
  157: 
  158:         if (
  159:           firebaseCode === "permission-denied" ||
  160:           firebaseCode === "firestore/permission-denied"
  161:         ) {
  162:           setError("You do not have permission to view this class.");
  163:           return;
  164:         }
  165: 
  166:         setError(
  167:           loadError instanceof Error

  169:             : "Unable to load this class.",
  170:         );
  171:       } finally {
  172:         setIsLoading(false);
  173:       }
  174:     }
  175: 
  176:     void loadClassDetails();
  177:   }, [classId, user?.uid]);

  193:
