  384:                     key={tab.id}
  385:                     type="button"
  386:                     onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
  387:                     className={`border-b-2 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
  388:                       isActive
  389:                         ? "border-blue-600 text-blue-600"
  390:                         : "border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800"
  391:                     }`}
  392:                   >
  393:                     {tab.label}
  394:                   </button>
  395:                 );
  396:               })}
  397:             </div>
  398:           </div>
  399: 
  400:           <div className="pt-6">
  401:             {activeTab === "overview" && (
  402:               <OverviewTab
  403:                 teacherClass={teacherClass}
  404:                 assignments={assignments}
  405:                 students={students}
  406:                 onOpenStudents={() => setActiveTab("students")}
  407:                 onOpenAssignments={() => setActiveTab("assignments")}
  408:               />
  409:             )}
  410: 
  411:             {activeTab === "students" && (
  412:               <ClassStudentsManager
  413:                 classId={teacherClass.id}
  414:                 students={students}
  415:                 onStudentsChanged={(updatedStudents) => {
  416:                   setTeacherClass((currentClass) => {
  417:                     if (!currentClass) {
  418:                       return currentClass;
  419:                     }
  420: 
  421:                     return {
  422:                       ...currentClass,
  423:                       students: updatedStudents,
  424:                       studentIds: updatedStudents.map(
  425:                         (student) => student.studentId,
  426:                       ),
  427:                     };
  428:                   });
  429:                 }}
  430:               />
  431:             )}
  432: 
  433:             {activeTab === "assignments" && (
  434:               <AssignmentsTab
  435:                 assignments={assignments}
  436:                 onAssignResource={() => router.push("/teacher/resources")}
  437:               />
  438:             )}
  439: 
  440:             {activeTab === "analytics" && (
  441:               <AnalyticsTab
  442:                 assignments={assignments}
  443:                 overallCompletionPercentage={overallCompletionPercentage}
  444:                 totalCompletions={totalCompletions}
  445:                 totalPossibleCompletions={totalPossibleCompletions}
  446:               />
  447:             )}
  448: 
  449: 
  450:             {activeTab === "settings" && (
  451:               <ClassSettingsPanel
  452:                 teacherClass={teacherClass}
  453:                 onUpdated={(updatedClass) => {
  454:                   setTeacherClass(updatedClass);
  455:                 }}
  456:                 onDeleted={() => {
  457:                   router.push("/teacher/classes");
  458:                 }}
  459:               />
  460:             )}
  461:           </div>
  462:         </section>
  463:       </div>
  464:     </main>
  465:   );
  466: }
  467: 
  468: function StatCard({
  469:   label,
  470:   value,
  471:   description,
  472: }: {
  473:   label: string;
  474:   value: string | number;
  475:   description: string;
  476: }) {
  477:   return (
  478:     <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
  479:       <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">{label}</p>
  480: 
  481:       <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{value}</p>
  482: 
  483:       <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{description}</p>
  484:     </div>
  485:   );
  486: }
  487: 
  488: function OverviewTab({
  489:   teacherClass,
  490:   assignments,
  491:   students,
  492:   onOpenStudents,
  493:   onOpenAssignments,
  494: }: {
  495:   teacherClass: TeacherClass;
  496:   assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[];
  497:   students: ClassStudent[];
  498:   onOpenStudents: () => void;
  499:   onOpenAssignments: () => void;
  500: }) {
  501:   const recentAssignments = assignments.slice(0, 3);
  502: 
  503:   return (
  504:     <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[1.3fr_0.7fr]">
  505:       <div className="space-y-6">
  506:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  507:           <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  508:             <div>
  509:               <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
  510:                 Recent assignments
  511:               </h2>
  512: 
  513:               <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  514:                 The latest work assigned to this class.
  515:               </p>
  516:             </div>
  517: 
  518:             <button
  519:               type="button"
  520:               onClick={onOpenAssignments}
  521:               className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700"
  522:             >
  523:               View all
  524:             </button>
  525:           </div>
  526: 
  527:           {recentAssignments.length === 0 ? (
  528:             <EmptyState
  529:               title="No assignments yet"
  530:               description="Recent resources, programming work, quizzes and exams assigned to this class will appear here."
  531:             />
  532:           ) : (
  533:             <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
  534:               {recentAssignments.map((assignment) => (
  535:                 <AssignmentSummaryCard
  536:                   key={assignment.id}
  537:                   assignment={assignment}
  538:                 />
  539:               ))}
  540:             </div>
  541:           )}
  542:         </section>
  543: 
  544:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  545:           <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  546:             <div>
  547:               <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">Students</h2>
  548: 
  549:               <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  550:                 Learners currently linked to this class.
  551:               </p>
  552:             </div>
  553: 
  554:             <button
  555:               type="button"
  556:               onClick={onOpenStudents}
  557:               className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700"
  558:             >
  559:               Manage
  560:             </button>
  561:           </div>
  562: 
  563:           {students.length === 0 ? (
  564:             <EmptyState
  565:               title="No students enrolled"
  566:               description="Add students to begin assigning resources and tracking progress."
  567:             />
  568:           ) : (
  569:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
  570:               {students.slice(0, 6).map((student) => (
  571:                 <StudentCard key={student.studentId} student={student} />
  572:               ))}
  573:             </div>
  574:           )}
  575:         </section>
  576:       </div>
  577: 
  578:       <aside className="space-y-6">
  579:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  580:           <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
  581:             Class information
  582:           </h2>
  583: 
  584:           <dl className="mt-5 space-y-4">
  585:             <InformationRow label="Class name" value={teacherClass.name} />
  586: 
  587:             <InformationRow
  588:               label="Subject"
  589:               value={teacherClass.subject || "Computer Science"}
  590:             />
  591: 
  592:             <InformationRow
  593:               label="Year group"
  594:               value={teacherClass.yearGroup || "Not specified"}
  595:             />
  596: 
  597:             <InformationRow
  598:               label="Academic year"
  599:               value={teacherClass.academicYear || "Not specified"}
  600:             />
  601: 
  602:             <InformationRow
  603:               label="Qualification"
  604:               value={qualificationLabel(teacherClass.qualification)}
  605:             />
  606: 
  607:             <InformationRow
  608:               label="Exam board"
  609:               value={teacherClass.examBoard || "Not specified"}
  610:             />
  611: 
  612:             <InformationRow
  613:               label="Created"
  614:               value={formatDate(teacherClass.createdAt)}
  615:             />
  616: 
  617:             <InformationRow label="Status" value={teacherClass.status} />
  618:           </dl>
  619:         </section>
  620: 
  621:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6">
  622:           <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-blue-950">Next step</h2>
  623: 
  624:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-blue-800">
  625:             Add students, publish a teaching resource and assign it to this
  626:             class.
  627:           </p>
  628:         </section>
  629:       </aside>
  630:     </div>
  631:   );
  632: }

  634: function StudentsTab({ students }: { students: ClassStudent[] }) {

  690:                 </span>
  691:               </div>
  692:             ))}
  693:           </div>
  694:         </div>

  748: function AnalyticsTab({
  749:   assignments,
  750:   overallCompletionPercentage,
  751:   totalCompletions,
  752:   totalPossibleCompletions,
  753: }: {
  754:   assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[];
  755:   overallCompletionPercentage: number;
  756:   totalCompletions: number;
  757:   totalPossibleCompletions: number;
  758: }) {
  759:   return (
  760:     <div className="space-y-6">
  761:       <section className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  762:         <StatCard
  763:           label="Overall completion"
  764:           value={`${overallCompletionPercentage}%`}
  765:           description="Across every assignment"
  766:         />
  767: 
  768:         <StatCard
  769:           label="Student completions"
  770:           value={totalCompletions}
  771:           description={`Out of ${totalPossibleCompletions} expected`}
  772:         />
  773: 
  774:         <StatCard
  775:           label="Resources assigned"
  776:           value={assignments.length}
  777:           description="Total class assignments"
  778:         />
  779:       </section>
  780: 
  781:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
  782:         <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
  783:           Assignment performance
  784:         </h2>
  785: 
  786:         <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  787:           Completion rates across lessons/resources, programming, quizzes and exams assigned to this class.
  788:         </p>
  789: 
  790:         {assignments.length === 0 ? (
  791:           <EmptyState
  792:             title="No analytics available"
  793:             description="Analytics will appear after assignments have been created for this class."
  794:           />
  795:         ) : (
  796:           <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
  797:             {assignments.map((assignment) => {
  798:               const percentage = calculateCompletionPercentage(assignment);
  799: 
  800:               return (
  801:                 <div key={assignment.id}>
  802:                   <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  803:                     <div>
  804:                       <p className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
  805:                         {assignment.title}
  806:                       </p>
  807: 
  808:                       <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
  809:                         {assignment.completedCount} of {assignment.studentCount}{" "}
  810:                         completed
  811:                       </p>
  812:                     </div>
  813: 
  814:                     <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
  815:                       {percentage}%
  816:                     </span>
  817:                   </div>
  818: 
  819:                   <div className="h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  820:                     <div
  821:                       className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-400 via-blue-500 to-indigo-500 transition-all"
  822:                       style={{
  823:                         width: `${percentage}%`,
  824:                       }}
  825:                     />
  826:                   </div>
  827:                 </div>
  828:               );
  829:             })}
  830:           </div>
  831:         )}
  832:       </section>
  833:     </div>
  834:   );
  835: }
  836: 
  837: function AssignmentSummaryCard({
  838:   assignment,
  839: }: {
  840:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment;
  841: }) {
  842:   const dueDateStatus = getDueDateStatus(assignment.dueDate);
  843: 
  844:   const completionPercentage = calculateCompletionPercentage(assignment);
  845: 
  846:   return (
  847:     <article className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
  848:       <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">
  849:         <div>
  850:           <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
  851:             {assignment.title}
  852:           </h3>
  853: 
  854:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  855:             {assignment.topic ||
  856:               assignment.kind ||
  857:               "Teaching resource"}
  858:           </p>
  859:         </div>
  860: 
  861:         <span
  862:           className={`w-fit rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${dueDateStatus.className}`}
  863:         >
  864:           {dueDateStatus.label}
  865:         </span>
  866:       </div>
  867: 
  868:       <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
  869:         <span className="text-slate-500">
  870:           {assignment.completedCount} of {assignment.studentCount} completed
  871:         </span>
  872: 
  873:         <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
  874:           {completionPercentage}%
  875:         </span>
  876:       </div>
  877:     </article>
  878:   );
  879: }
  880: 
  881: function AssignmentDetailedCard({
  882:   assignment,
  883: }: {
  884:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment;
  885: }) {
  886:   const dueDateStatus = getDueDateStatus(assignment.dueDate);
  887: 
  888:   const completionPercentage = calculateCompletionPercentage(assignment);
  889: 
  890:   return (
  891:     <article className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
  892:       <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-start">
  893:         <div>
  894:           <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  895:             <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
  896:               {assignment.title}
  897:             </h3>
  898: 
  899:             <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold capitalize text-slate-700">
  900:               {assignment.status}
  901:             </span>
  902:           </div>
  903: 
  904:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  905:             {assignment.topic ||
  906:               assignment.kind ||
  907:               "Teaching resource"}
  908:           </p>
  909: 
  910:           {assignment.description && (
  911:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  912:               {assignment.description}
  913:             </p>
  914:           )}
  915:         </div>
  916: 
  917:         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  918:           <span
  919:             className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${dueDateStatus.className}`}
  920:           >
  921:             {dueDateStatus.label}
  922:           </span>
  923: 
  924:           <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
  925:             {formatDate(assignment.dueDate)}
  926:           </span>
  927:         </div>
  928:       </div>
  929: 
  930:       <div className="mt-5">
  931:         <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
  932:           <span className="text-slate-500">
  933:             {assignment.completedCount} of {assignment.studentCount} students
  934:             completed
  935:           </span>
  936: 
  937:           <span className="font-bold text-slate-800">
  938:             {completionPercentage}%
  939:           </span>
  940:         </div>
  941: 
  942:         <div className="h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  943:           <div
  944:             className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-sky-400 via-blue-500 to-indigo-500"
  945:             style={{
  946:               width: `${completionPercentage}%`,
  947:             }}
  948:           />
  949:         </div>
  950:       </div>
  951:     </article>
  952:   );
  953: }
  954: 
  955: function StudentCard({ student }: { student: ClassStudent }) {
  956:   return (
  957:     <article className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
  958:       <StudentAvatar name={student.displayName} />
  959: 
  960:       <div className="min-w-0">
  961:         <p className="truncate font-semibold text-slate-900">
  962:           {student.displayName || "Student"}
  963:         </p>
  964: 
  965:         <p className="truncate text-sm text-slate-500">
  966:           {student.email || "No email"}
  967:         </p>
  968:       </div>
  969:     </article>
  970:   );
  971: }
  972: 
  973: function StudentAvatar({ name }: { name: string }) {
  974:   const initials =
  975:     name
  976:       ?.split(" ")
  977:       .filter(Boolean)
  978:       .slice(0, 2)
  979:       .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
  980:       .join("") || "S";
  981: 
  982:   return (
  983:     <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-100 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700">
  984:       {initials}
  985:     </div>
  986:   );
  987: }
  988: 
  989: function InformationRow({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  990:   return (
  991:     <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
  992:       <dt className="text-sm text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
  993: 
  994:       <dd className="text-right text-sm font-semibold capitalize text-slate-900">
  995:         {value}
  996:       </dd>
  997:     </div>
  998:   );
  999: }
 1000: 
 1001: function EmptyState({
 1002:   title,
 1003:   description,
 1004: }: {
 1005:   title: string;
 1006:   description: string;
 1007: }) {
 1008:   return (
 1009:     <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-10 text-center">
 1010:       <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
 1011: 
 1012:       <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
 1013:         {description}
 1014:       </p>
 1015:     </div>
 1016:   );
 1017: }

   10: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
   11: 
   12: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   13: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   14: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   15: 
   16: import {
   17:   createTeacherClass,
   18:   getTeacherClasses,
   19:   type TeacherClass,
   20: } from "@/services/classService";
   21: 
   22: import type {
   23:   ExamBoard,
   24:   Qualification,
   25: } from "@/types/user";
   26: 
   27: import {
   28:   getUnifiedTeacherAssignments,
   29:   type UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
   30: } from "@/services/unifiedTeacherAssignmentService";
   31: 
   32: type StatusFilter =
   33:   | "active"
   34:   | "archived"
   35:   | "all";
   36: 
   37: function defaultAcademicYear(): string {
   38:   const now = new Date();
   39: 
   40:   const year =
   41:     now.getFullYear();
   42: 
   43:   /*
   44:    * Treat August onward as preparation for the new UK academic year.
   45:    */
   46:   const startYear =
   47:     now.getMonth() >= 7
   48:       ? year
   49:       : year - 1;
   50: 
   51:   return `${startYear}/${startYear + 1}`;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function qualificationLabel(
   55:   qualification:
   56:     Qualification | "",
   57: ): string {
   58:   if (
   59:     qualification ===
   60:     "A_LEVEL"
   61:   ) {
   62:     return "A Level";
   63:   }
   64: 
   65:   return qualification || "Not set";
   66: }
   67: 
   68: export default function TeacherClassesPage() {
   69:   const {
   70:     user,
   71:     profile,
   72:     loading: authLoading,
   73:     profileReady,
   74:   } = useAuth();
   75: 
   76:   const [classes, setClasses] =
   77:     useState<
   78:       TeacherClass[]
   79:     >([]);
   80: 
   81:   const [
   82:     unifiedAssignments,
   83:     setUnifiedAssignments,
   84:   ] = useState<
   85:     UnifiedTeacherAssignment[]
   86:   >([]);
   87: 
   88:   const [
   89:     loadingClasses,
   90:     setLoadingClasses,
   91:   ] = useState(true);
   92: 
   93:   const [
   94:     showForm,
   95:     setShowForm,
   96:   ] = useState(false);
   97: 
   98:   const [
   99:     className,
  100:     setClassName,
  101:   ] = useState("");
  102: 
  103:   const [
  104:     yearGroup,
  105:     setYearGroup,
  106:   ] = useState("");
  107: 
  108:   const [subject, setSubject] =
  109:     useState(
  110:       "Computer Science",
  111:     );
  112: 
  113:   const [
  114:     academicYear,
  115:     setAcademicYear,
  116:   ] = useState(
  117:     defaultAcademicYear(),
  118:   );
  119: 
  120:   const [
  121:     qualification,
  122:     setQualification,
  123:   ] =
  124:     useState<
  125:       Qualification | ""
  126:     >("GCSE");
  127: 
  128:   const [
  129:     examBoard,
  130:     setExamBoard,
  131:   ] =
  132:     useState<
  133:       ExamBoard | ""
  134:     >("OCR");
  135: 
  136:   const [
  137:     submitting,
  138:     setSubmitting,
  139:   ] = useState(false);
  140: 
  141:   const [
  142:     searchTerm,
  143:     setSearchTerm,
  144:   ] = useState("");
  145: 
  146:   const [
  147:     statusFilter,
  148:     setStatusFilter,
  149:   ] =
  150:     useState<StatusFilter>(
  151:       "active",
  152:     );
  153: 
  154:   async function loadClasses() {
  155:     if (!user?.uid) {
  156:       setClasses([]);
  157:       setLoadingClasses(
  158:         false,
  159:       );
  160: 
  161:       return;
  162:     }
  163: 
  164:     try {
  165:       setLoadingClasses(
  166:         true,
  167:       );
  168: 
  169:       const [
  170:         loaded,
  171:         assignmentSummary,
  172:       ] = await Promise.all([
  173:         getTeacherClasses(
  174:           user.uid,
  175:         ),
  176:         getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(
  177:           user.uid,
  178:         ),
  179:       ]);
  180: 
  181:       setClasses(
  182:         loaded,
  183:       );
  184: 
  185:       setUnifiedAssignments(
  186:         assignmentSummary.assignments,
  187:       );
  188:     } catch (error) {
  189:       console.error(
  190:         "Failed to load classes:",
  191:         error,
  192:       );
  193: 
  194:       toast.error(
  195:         "Could not load your classes.",
  196:       );
  197:     } finally {
  198:       setLoadingClasses(
  199:         false,
  200:       );
  201:     }
  202:   }
  203: 
  204:   useEffect(() => {
  205:     if (
  206:       authLoading ||
  207:       !profileReady
  208:     ) {
  209:       return;
  210:     }
  211: 
  212:     void loadClasses();
  213:   }, [
  214:     authLoading,
  215:     profileReady,
  216:     user?.uid,
  217:   ]);
  218: 
  219:   const visibleClasses =
  220:     useMemo(() => {
  221:       const search =
  222:         searchTerm
  223:           .trim()
  224:           .toLowerCase();
  225: 
  226:       return classes.filter(
  227:         (item) => {
  228:           const matchesStatus =
  229:             statusFilter ===
  230:               "all" ||
  231:             item.status ===
  232:               statusFilter;
  233: 
  234:           if (
  235:             !matchesStatus
  236:           ) {
  237:             return false;
  238:           }
  239: 
  240:           if (!search) {
  241:             return true;
  242:           }
  243: 
  244:           return [
  245:             item.name,
  246:             item.subject,
  247:             item.yearGroup,
  248:             item.academicYear,
  249:             item.qualification,
  250:             item.examBoard,
  251:           ].some(
  252:             (value) =>
  253:               value
  254:                 .toLowerCase()
  255:                 .includes(
  256:                   search,
  257:                 ),
  258:           );
  259:         },
  260:       );
  261:     }, [
  262:       classes,
  263:       searchTerm,
  264:       statusFilter,
  265:     ]);
  266: 
  267:   const activeClasses =
  268:     classes.filter(
  269:       (item) =>
  270:         item.status ===
  271:         "active",
  272:     );
  273: 
  274:   const totalStudents =
  275:     activeClasses.reduce(
  276:       (
  277:         total,
  278:         item,
  279:       ) =>
  280:         total +
  281:         item.studentIds.length,
  282:       0,
  283:     );
  284: 
  285:   const activeClassIds =
  286:     new Set(
  287:       activeClasses.map(
  288:         (item) => item.id,
  289:       ),
  290:     );
  291: 
  292:   const totalAssignments =
  293:     unifiedAssignments.filter(
  294:       (assignment) =>
  295:         activeClassIds.has(
  296:           assignment.classId,
  297:         ),
  298:     ).length;
  299: 
  300:   const assignmentCountByClass =
  301:     useMemo(() => {
  302:       const counts =
  303:         new Map<string, number>();
  304: 
  305:       unifiedAssignments.forEach(
  306:         (assignment) => {
  307:           counts.set(
  308:             assignment.classId,
  309:             (counts.get(
  310:               assignment.classId,
  311:             ) || 0) + 1,
  312:           );
  313:         },
  314:       );
  315: 
  316:       return counts;
  317:     }, [
  318:       unifiedAssignments,
  319:     ]);
  320: 
  321:   async function handleCreateClass(
  322:     event:
  323:       FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>,
  324:   ) {
  325:     event.preventDefault();
  326: 
  327:     if (!user?.uid) {
  328:       toast.error(
  329:         "You must be logged in as a teacher.",
  330:       );
  331:       return;
  332:     }
  333: 
  334:     if (
  335:       !profile?.schoolId
  336:     ) {
  337:       toast.error(
  338:         "Create or join your school organisation before creating classes.",
  339:       );
  340:       return;
  341:     }
  342: 
  343:     if (
  344:       !className.trim() ||
  345:       !yearGroup.trim() ||
  346:       !academicYear.trim() ||
  347:       !qualification ||
  348:       !examBoard
  349:     ) {
  350:       toast.error(
  351:         "Complete every required class field.",
  352:       );
  353:       return;
  354:     }
  355: 
  356:     try {
  357:       setSubmitting(true);
  358: 
  359:       await createTeacherClass({
  360:         name:
  361:           className,
  362: 
  363:         subject,
  364: 
  365:         yearGroup,
  366: 
  367:         academicYear,
  368: 
  369:         qualification,
  370: 
  371:         examBoard,
  372: 
  373:         teacherId:
  374:           user.uid,
  375: 
  376:         teacherName:
  377:           profile?.name ||
  378:           user.displayName ||
  379:           user.email ||
  380:           "Teacher",
  381: 
  382:         schoolId:
  383:           profile.schoolId,
  384:       });
  385: 
  386:       toast.success(
  387:         "Class created successfully.",
  388:       );
  389: 
  390:       setClassName("");
  391:       setYearGroup("");
  392:       setSubject(
  393:         "Computer Science",
  394:       );
  395:       setAcademicYear(
  396:         defaultAcademicYear(),
  397:       );
  398:       setQualification(
  399:         "GCSE",
  400:       );
  401:       setExamBoard(
  402:         "OCR",
  403:       );
  404:       setShowForm(false);
  405: 
  406:       await loadClasses();
  407:     } catch (error) {
  408:       console.error(
  409:         "Create class error:",
  410:         error,
  411:       );
  412: 
  413:       toast.error(
  414:         error instanceof Error
  415:           ? error.message
  416:           : "Could not create the class.",
  417:       );
  418:     } finally {
  419:       setSubmitting(false);
  420:     }
  421:   }
  422: 
  423:   if (
  424:     authLoading ||
  425:     !profileReady ||
  426:     loadingClasses
  427:   ) {
  428:     return (
  429:       <div className="space-y-8">
  430:         <Skeleton className="h-52 w-full" />
  431: 
  432:         <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
  433:           <Skeleton className="h-28" />
  434:           <Skeleton className="h-28" />
  435:           <Skeleton className="h-28" />
  436:         </div>
  437: 
  438:         <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full" />
  439:       </div>
  440:     );
  441:   }
  442: 
  443:   return (
  444:     <div className="space-y-8">
  445:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-700 via-teal-700 to-cyan-700 text-white">
  446:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
  447:           <div>
  448:             <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-100">
  449:               Teacher Portal
  450:             </p>
  451: 
  452:             <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-extrabold">
  453:               Manage Classes
  454:             </h1>
  455: 
  456:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-emerald-100">
  457:               Create school-scoped teaching groups, manage curriculum settings,
  458:               organise students and monitor assignments.
  459:             </p>
  460:           </div>
  461: 
  462:           <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  463:             <Link
  464:               href="/teacher/school"
  465:               className="rounded-xl border border-white/30 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-white/20"
  466:             >
  467:               School
  468:             </Link>
  469: 
  470:             <button
  471:               type="button"
  472:               onClick={() =>
  473:                 setShowForm(
  474:                   (current) =>
  475:                     !current,
  476:                 )
  477:               }
  478:               className="rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-teal-800 shadow-sm transition hover:bg-emerald-50"
  479:             >
  480:               {showForm
  481:                 ? "Close form"
  482:                 : "+ Create class"}
  483:             </button>
  484:           </div>
  485:         </div>
  486:       </Card>
  487: 
  488:       {!profile?.schoolId && (
  489:         <Card className="border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50">
  490:           <h2 className="font-black text-amber-950">
  491:             School setup required
  492:           </h2>
  493: 
  494:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-800">
  495:             New classes must belong to a school organisation so students remain
  496:             isolated from other schools.
  497:           </p>
  498:         </Card>
  499:       )}
  500: 
  501:       <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  502:         <Metric
  503:           label="Active classes"
  504:           value={
  505:             activeClasses.length
  506:           }
  507:         />
  508: 
  509:         <Metric
  510:           label="Students"
  511:           value={
  512:             totalStudents
  513:           }
  514:         />
  515: 
  516:         <Metric
  517:           label="Assignments"
  518:           value={
  519:             totalAssignments
  520:           }
  521:         />
  522:       </div>
  523: 
  524:       {showForm && (
  525:         <Card className="border border-teal-200">
  526:           <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-teal-600">
  527:             New teaching group
  528:           </p>
  529: 
  530:           <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  531:             Create a class
  532:           </h2>
  533: 
  534:           <form
  535:             onSubmit={
  536:               handleCreateClass
  537:             }
  538:             className="mt-6 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2"
  539:           >
  540:             <Field
  541:               label="Class name"
  542:               value={
  543:                 className
  544:               }
  545:               onChange={
  546:                 setClassName
  547:               }
  548:               placeholder="e.g. Year 11 CS Group"
  549:             />
  550: 
  551:             <Field
  552:               label="Year group"
  553:               value={
  554:                 yearGroup
  555:               }
  556:               onChange={
  557:                 setYearGroup
  558:               }
  559:               placeholder="e.g. Year 11"
  560:             />
  561: 
  562:             <Field
  563:               label="Subject"
  564:               value={
  565:                 subject
  566:               }
  567:               onChange={
  568:                 setSubject
  569:               }
  570:               placeholder="Computer Science"
  571:             />
  572: 
  573:             <Field
  574:               label="Academic year"
  575:               value={
  576:                 academicYear
  577:               }
  578:               onChange={
  579:                 setAcademicYear
  580:               }
  581:               placeholder="2026/2027"
  582:             />
  583: 
  584:             <label>
  585:               <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
  586:                 Qualification
  587:               </span>
  588: 
  589:               <select
  590:                 value={
  591:                   qualification
  592:                 }
  593:                 onChange={(
  594:                   event,
  595:                 ) =>
  596:                   setQualification(
  597:                     event.target
  598:                       .value as
  599:                       | Qualification
  600:                       | "",
  601:                   )
  602:                 }
  603:                 className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  604:               >
  605:                 <option value="GCSE">
  606:                   GCSE
  607:                 </option>
  608: 
  609:                 <option value="A_LEVEL">
  610:                   A Level
  611:                 </option>
  612:               </select>
  613:             </label>
  614: 
  615:             <label>
  616:               <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
  617:                 Exam board
  618:               </span>
  619: 
  620:               <select
  621:                 value={
  622:                   examBoard
  623:                 }
  624:                 onChange={(
  625:                   event,
  626:                 ) =>
  627:                   setExamBoard(
  628:                     event.target
  629:                       .value as
  630:                       | ExamBoard
  631:                       | "",
  632:                   )
  633:                 }
  634:                 className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  635:               >
  636:                 <option value="AQA">
  637:                   AQA
  638:                 </option>
  639: 
  640:                 <option value="OCR">
  641:                   OCR
  642:                 </option>
  643: 
  644:                 <option value="EDEXCEL">
  645:                   Pearson Edexcel
  646:                 </option>
  647:               </select>
  648:             </label>
  649: 
  650:             <div className="md:col-span-2">
  651:               <button
  652:                 type="submit"
  653:                 disabled={
  654:                   submitting ||
  655:                   !profile?.schoolId
  656:                 }
  657:                 className="rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-6 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-teal-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
  658:               >
  659:                 {submitting
  660:                   ? "Creating..."
  661:                   : "Create class"}
  662:               </button>
  663:             </div>
  664:           </form>
  665:         </Card>
  666:       )}
  667: 
  668:       <Card>
  669:         <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 xl:flex-row xl:items-end">
  670:           <div>
  671:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  672:               Class directory
  673:             </p>
  674: 
  675:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  676:               Your classes
  677:             </h2>
  678:           </div>
  679: 
  680:           <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
  681:             <input
  682:               type="search"
  683:               value={
  684:                 searchTerm
  685:               }
  686:               onChange={(
  687:                 event,
  688:               ) =>
  689:                 setSearchTerm(
  690:                   event.target
  691:                     .value,
  692:                 )
  693:               }
  694:               placeholder="Search classes..."
  695:               className="min-w-[240px] rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  696:             />
  697: 
  698:             <select
  699:               value={
  700:                 statusFilter
  701:               }
  702:               onChange={(
  703:                 event,
  704:               ) =>
  705:                 setStatusFilter(
  706:                   event.target
  707:                     .value as
  708:                     StatusFilter,
  709:                 )
  710:               }
  711:               className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  712:             >
  713:               <option value="active">
  714:                 Active
  715:               </option>
  716: 
  717:               <option value="archived">
  718:                 Archived
  719:               </option>
  720: 
  721:               <option value="all">
  722:                 All classes
  723:               </option>
  724:             </select>
  725:           </div>
  726:         </div>
  727: 
  728:         {visibleClasses.length ===
  729:         0 ? (
  730:           <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 p-10 text-center">
  731:             <h3 className="font-black text-slate-900">
  732:               No classes found
  733:             </h3>
  734: 
  735:             <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  736:               Create a class or adjust the current search/filter.
  737:             </p>
  738:           </div>
  739:         ) : (
  740:           <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
  741:             {visibleClasses.map(
  742:               (
  743:                 classItem,
  744:               ) => (
  745:                 <article
  746:                   key={
  747:                     classItem.id
  748:                   }
  749:                   className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
  750:                 >
  751:                   <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">
  752:                     <div>
  753:                       <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  754:                         <span className="rounded-full bg-teal-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black uppercase text-teal-700">
  755:                           {classItem.yearGroup ||
  756:                             "Year group"}
  757:                         </span>
  758: 
  759:                         <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-blue-700">
  760:                           {classItem.examBoard ||
  761:                             "Board"}{" "}
  762:                           {qualificationLabel(
  763:                             classItem.qualification,
  764:                           )}
  765:                         </span>
  766: 
  767:                         {classItem.status ===
  768:                           "archived" && (
  769:                           <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-amber-700">
  770:                             Archived
  771:                           </span>
  772:                         )}
  773:                       </div>
  774: 
  775:                       <h3 className="mt-3 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  776:                         {classItem.name}
  777:                       </h3>
  778: 
  779:                       <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  780:                         {classItem.subject ||
  781:                           "Computer Science"}{" "}
  782:                         Â·{" "}
  783:                         {classItem.academicYear ||
  784:                           "Academic year not set"}
  785:                       </p>
  786:                     </div>
  787: 
  788:                     <Link
  789:                       href={`/teacher/classes/${classItem.id}`}
  790:                       className="shrink-0 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700"
  791:                     >
  792:                       Open class
  793:                     </Link>
  794:                   </div>
  795: 
  796:                   <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
  797:                     <MiniMetric
  798:                       label="Students"
  799:                       value={
  800:                         classItem
  801:                           .studentIds
  802:                           .length
  803:                       }
  804:                     />
  805: 
  806:                     <MiniMetric
  807:                       label="Assignments"
  808:                       value={
  809:                         assignmentCountByClass.get(
  810:                           classItem.id,
  811:                         ) || 0
  812:                       }
  813:                     />
  814:                   </div>
  815:                 </article>
  816:               ),
  817:             )}
  818:           </div>
  819:         )}
  820:       </Card>
  821:     </div>
  822:   );

  826:   label,
  827:   value,
  828: }: {

  831: }) {
  832:   return (

  867:   value,
  868:   onChange,
  869:   placeholder,
  870: }: {

  875:   ) => void;

  881:         {label}
  882:       </span>
  883:

  885:         type="text"
  886:         value={value}

  888:           event,
  889:         ) =>
  890:           onChange(
  891:             event.target.value,
  892:           )
  893:         }
  894:         placeholder={
  895:           placeholder
  896:         }
  897:         className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3"
  898:       />
  899:     </label>
  900:   );
  901: }

FILE: app\teacher\interventions\page.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
    5: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    6: import { Activity, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
    7: 
    8: import CreateInterventionModal from "@/components/teacher/interventions/CreateInterventionModal";
    9: import InterventionActionContext from "@/components/teacher/interventions/InterventionActionContext";
   10: import InterventionEffectivenessOverview from "@/components/teacher/interventions/InterventionEffectivenessOverview";
   11: import InterventionFilters from "@/components/teacher/interventions/InterventionFilters";
   12: import InterventionImpactCard from "@/components/teacher/interventions/InterventionImpactCard";
   13: import InterventionStudentRow from "@/components/teacher/interventions/InterventionStudentRow";
   14: import InterventionIntelligencePanel from "@/components/teacher/intelligence/InterventionIntelligencePanel";
   15: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   16: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   17: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   18: import { useUserProfile } from "@/hooks/useUserProfile";
   19: import {
   20:   getInterventionCandidates,
   21:   type InterventionCandidate,
   22: } from "@/services/interventionAnalyticsService";
   23: import { getTeacherInterventions } from "@/services/interventionService";
   24: import type { Intervention } from "@/types/intervention";
   25: 
   26: export default function TeacherInterventionsPage() {
   27:   const { user } = useAuth();
   28:   const { profile } = useUserProfile();
   29:   const searchParams = useSearchParams();
   30: 
   31:   const requestedStudentId =
   32:     searchParams.get("studentId")?.trim() || "";
   33: 
   34:   const requestedStudentName =
   35:     searchParams.get("studentName")?.trim() || "";
   36: 
   37:   const requestedClassName =
   38:     searchParams.get("className")?.trim() || "";
   39: 
   40:   const requestedTopic =
   41:     searchParams.get("topic")?.trim() || "";
   42: 
   43:   const deepLinkApplied =
   44:     useRef(false);
   45: 
   46:   const [candidates, setCandidates] =
   47:     useState<InterventionCandidate[]>([]);
   48:   const [interventions, setInterventions] =
   49:     useState<Intervention[]>([]);
   50:   const [selected, setSelected] =
   51:     useState<InterventionCandidate | null>(null);
   52:   const [selectedImpactId, setSelectedImpactId] =
   53:     useState<string | null>(null);
   54:   const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   55:   const [priority, setPriority] = useState("all");
   56:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   57:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   58: 
   59:   async function load() {
   60:     const teacherId =
   61:       user?.uid;
   62: 
   63:     if (!teacherId) {
   64:       setLoading(false);
   65:       return;
   66:     }
   67: 
   68:     setLoading(true);
   69:     setError("");
   70: 
   71:     try {
   72:       const [
   73:         nextCandidates,
   74:         nextInterventions,
   75:       ] = await Promise.all([
   76:         getInterventionCandidates(
   77:           teacherId,
   78:         ),
   79:         getTeacherInterventions(
   80:           teacherId,
   81:         ),
   82:       ]);
   83: 
   84:       setCandidates(
   85:         nextCandidates,
   86:       );
   87: 
   88:       setInterventions(
   89:         nextInterventions,
   90:       );
   91: 
   92:       setSelectedImpactId(
   93:         (current) => {
   94:           if (!current) {
   95:             return null;
   96:           }
   97: 
   98:           return nextInterventions.some(
   99:             (item) =>
  100:               item.id === current,
  101:           )
  102:             ? current
  103:             : null;
  104:         },
  105:       );
  106:     } catch (caughtError: unknown) {
  107:       const firebaseCode =
  108:         typeof caughtError === "object" &&
  109:         caughtError !== null &&
  110:         "code" in caughtError &&
  111:         typeof (caughtError as { code?: unknown }).code === "string"
  112:           ? (caughtError as { code: string }).code
  113:           : "";
  114: 
  115:       setCandidates([]);
  116:       setInterventions([]);
  117: 
  118:       setError(
  119:         firebaseCode === "permission-denied" ||
  120:           firebaseCode === "firestore/permission-denied"
  121:           ? "Intervention data is unavailable for this teacher account."
  122:           : caughtError instanceof Error
  123:             ? caughtError.message
  124:             : "Intervention data could not be loaded.",
  125:       );
  126:     } finally {
  127:       setLoading(false);
  128:     }
  129:   }
  130: 
  131:   useEffect(() => {
  132:     void load();
  133:   }, [user?.uid]);
  134: 
  135:   /*
  136:    * Analytics -> Intervention hand-off.
  137:    *
  138:    * The same learner may belong to multiple classes, so the hand-off carries
  139:    * both learner identity and class name. We pre-filter the intervention list
  140:    * and automatically open the matching candidate once per navigation.
  141:    */
  142:   useEffect(() => {
  143:     if (
  144:       loading ||
  145:       deepLinkApplied.current ||
  146:       !requestedStudentId
  147:     ) {
  148:       return;
  149:     }
  150: 
  151:     const requested =
  152:       candidates.find(
  153:         (candidate) =>
  154:           candidate.student.uid ===
  155:             requestedStudentId &&
  156:           (!requestedClassName ||
  157:             candidate.className ===
  158:               requestedClassName),
  159:       );
  160: 
  161:     if (!requested) {
  162:       if (requestedStudentName) {
  163:         setSearch(
  164:           requestedStudentName,
  165:         );
  166:       }
  167: 
  168:       return;
  169:     }
  170: 
  171:     deepLinkApplied.current = true;
  172: 
  173:     setSearch(
  174:       requestedStudentName ||
  175:         requested.student.name,
  176:     );
  177: 
  178:     setSelected(
  179:       requested,
  180:     );
  181:   }, [
  182:     loading,
  183:     candidates,
  184:     requestedStudentId,
  185:     requestedStudentName,
  186:     requestedClassName,
  187:   ]);
  188: 
  189:   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
  190:     const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();
  191: 
  192:     return candidates.filter(
  193:       (candidate) =>
  194:         (!term ||
  195:           candidate.student.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
  196:           candidate.priorityTopic.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
  197:           candidate.className.toLowerCase().includes(term)) &&
  198:         (priority === "all" || candidate.priority === priority),
  199:     );
  200:   }, [candidates, priority, search]);
  201: 
  202:   const effectivenessSources = useMemo(
  203:     () =>
  204:       interventions.map((intervention) => ({
  205:         id: intervention.id,
  206:         studentName: intervention.studentName,
  207:         topic: intervention.topic,
  208:         status: intervention.status,
  209:       })),
  210:     [interventions],
  211:   );
  212: 
  213:   if (loading) {
  214:     return (
  215:       <div className="space-y-6">
  216:         <Skeleton className="h-48" />
  217:         <Skeleton className="h-96" />
  218:       </div>
  219:     );
  220:   }
  221: 
  222:   if (error) {
  223:     return (
  224:       <div className="space-y-6">
  225:         <Card className="border border-red-200 bg-red-50">
  226:           <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-red-700">
  227:             Intervention Centre
  228:           </p>
  229:           <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  230:             Intervention data unavailable
  231:           </h1>
  232:           <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  233:             {error}
  234:           </p>
  235:           <button
  236:             type="button"
  237:             onClick={() => void load()}
  238:             className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-red-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-black text-white transition hover:bg-red-800"
  239:           >
  240:             Try again
  241:           </button>
  242:         </Card>
  243:       </div>
  244:     );
  245:   }
  246: 
  247:   return (
  248:     <div className="space-y-8">
  249:       <Card className="border-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-teal-800 to-cyan-800 text-white">
  250:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  251:           <div>
  252:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase text-teal-100">
  253:               Adaptive support
  254:             </p>
  255:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">
  256:               Intervention Centre
  257:             </h1>
  258:             <p className="mt-3 text-teal-100">
  259:               Turn assessment evidence into targeted lesson, quiz and exam
  260:               pathways, then measure whether support actually improves learner
  261:               outcomes.
  262:             </p>
  263:           </div>
  264: 
  265:           <Link
  266:             href="/teacher"
  267:             className="rounded-xl border border-white/20 px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  268:           >
  269:             Teacher Dashboard
  270:           </Link>
  271:         </div>
  272:       </Card>
  273: 
  274:       <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  275:         <Summary label="Students reviewed" value={candidates.length} />

  277:           label="Active interventions"
  278:           value={
  279:             interventions.filter((item) => item.status === "active").length
  280:           }
  281:         />

  285:             interventions.filter((item) => item.status === "completed").length

  413:                         (step) => step.status === "completed",

  442:                   </div>

  444:               );
  445:             })

  447:             <p className="text-slate-500">
  448:               No interventions yet.

  468:           void load();

  471:     </div>

  425: function ResultRows({
  426:   student,
  427:   expanded,
  428:   onToggle,
  429: }: {
  430:   student: TeacherQuizStudentResult;
  431:   expanded: boolean;
  432:   onToggle: () => void;

  439:           <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{student.studentEmail}</p>
  440:         </td>
  441: 
  442:         <td className="px-5 py-4">
  443:           {student.status === "completed" ? (
  444:             <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-emerald-800">
  445:               Completed
  446:             </span>
  447:           ) : (
  448:             <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-slate-600">
  449:               Not started
  450:             </span>
  451:           )}
  452:         </td>
  453: 
  454:         <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold text-slate-900">
  455:           {student.status === "completed" ? `${student.score}/${student.totalQuestions}` : "â€”"}
  456:         </td>
  457: 
  458:         <td className="px-5 py-4 font-black text-slate-950">
  459:           {student.status === "completed" ? `${student.percentage}%` : "â€”"}
  460:         </td>
  461: 
  462:         <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold text-slate-700">
  463:           {student.status === "completed" ? student.earnedXP : "â€”"}
  464:         </td>
  465: 
  466:         <td className="px-5 py-4 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">
  467:           {student.status === "completed" ? formatDuration(student.timeTakenSeconds) : "â€”"}
  468:         </td>
  469: 
  470:         <td className="px-5 py-4 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">
  471:           {formatDate(student.completedAt, true)}
  472:         </td>
  473:

  475:           {student.integrityTerminated ? (
  476:             <button
  477:               type="button"
  478:               onClick={onToggle}
  479:               className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black text-red-800"
  480:             >
  481:               <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" />
  482:               Auto-submitted
  483:               {expanded ? <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" />}
  484:             </button>
  485:           ) : student.integrityIncidents.length > 0 ? (
  486:             <button
  487:               type="button"
  488:               onClick={onToggle}
  489:               className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black text-amber-800"
  490:             >
  491:               {student.integrityIncidents.length} event{student.integrityIncidents.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
  492:               {expanded ? <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" />}
  493:             </button>
  494:           ) : (
  495:             <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">None</span>
  496:           )}
  497:         </td>
  498:       </tr>
  499: 
  500:       {expanded && (
  501:         <tr>
  502:           <td colSpan={8} className="bg-slate-50 px-5 py-5">

  504:               <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
  505:                 <ShieldAlert className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 text-red-600" />
  506:                 <div>
  507:                   <h3 className="font-black text-slate-950">Integrity record</h3>
  508:                   <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
  509:                     {student.integrityTerminated
  510:                       ? student.integrityTerminationReason || "This assessment was automatically submitted by the integrity rules."
  511:                       : "Integrity activity was recorded during this assessment."}

  515:                   </p>

  521:                   {student.integrityIncidents.map((incident, index) => (
  522:                     <div
  523:                       key={`${incident.id}-${index}`}
  524:                       className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4"
  525:                     >
  526:                       <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  527:                         <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900">
  528:                           {formatIncidentType(incident.type)}
  529:                         </p>
  530:                         <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
  531:                           {formatDate(incident.occurredAt, true)}
  532:                         </p>
  533:                       </div>
  534: 
  535:                       <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">
  536:                         Question {incident.questionNumber ?? "â€”"}
  537:                       </p>
  538:                       <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700">
  539:                         {incident.detail}
  540:                       </p>
  541:                     </div>
  542:                   ))}
  543:                 </div>
  544:               ) : (
  545:                 <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  546:                   No individual integrity incidents were stored for this attempt.
  547:                 </p>
  548:               )}
  549:             </div>
  550:           </td>
  551:         </tr>
  552:       )}
  553:     </>
  554:   );
  555: }
  556: 
  557: function SummaryCard({
  558:   label,
  559:   value,
  560:   detail,
  561:   icon,
  562:   danger = false,
  563: }: {
  564:   label: string;
  565:   value: string | number;
  566:   detail: string;
  567:   icon: ReactNode;
  568:   danger?: boolean;
  569: }) {
  570:   return (
  571:     <Card className={danger ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50" : ""}>
  572:       <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
  573:         <div>
  574:           <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">{label}</p>
  575:           <p className={`mt-2 text-3xl font-black ${danger ? "text-red-900" : "text-slate-950"}`}>
  576:             {value}
  577:           </p>
  578:           <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">{detail}</p>
  579:         </div>
  580:         <div className={`rounded-xl p-3 ${danger ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"}`}>
  581:           {icon}
  582:         </div>
  583:       </div>
  584:     </Card>
  585:   );
  586: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   AlertCircle,
    5:   BarChart3,
    6:   Brain,
    7:   CalendarDays,
    8:   CheckCircle2,
    9:   Clock3,
   10:   Eye,
   11:   Loader2,
   12:   Search,
   13:   Users,
   14: } from "lucide-react";
   15: import Link from "next/link";
   16: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
   17: 
   18: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   19: import Skeleton from "@/components/ui/Skeleton";
   20: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   21: import {
   22:   getTeacherQuizAssignments,
   23:   type TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary,
   24: } from "@/services/teacherQuizAssignmentService";
   25: 
   26: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   27:   if (!value) {
   28:     return "No due date";
   29:   }
   30: 
   31:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   32:     day: "2-digit",
   33:     month: "long",
   34:     year: "numeric",
   35:   }).format(value);
   36: }
   37: 
   38: function getDueStatus(dueDate: Date | null): {
   39:   label: string;
   40:   overdue: boolean;
   41: } {
   42:   if (!dueDate) {
   43:     return {
   44:       label: "No due date",
   45:       overdue: false,
   46:     };
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   const today = new Date();
   50:   const due = new Date(dueDate);
   51: 
   52:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   53:   due.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   54: 
   55:   const differenceInDays = Math.ceil(
   56:     (due.getTime() - today.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24),
   57:   );
   58: 
   59:   if (differenceInDays < 0) {
   60:     const days = Math.abs(differenceInDays);
   61: 
   62:     return {
   63:       label: `${days} ${days === 1 ? "day" : "days"} overdue`,
   64:       overdue: true,
   65:     };
   66:   }
   67: 
   68:   if (differenceInDays === 0) {
   69:     return {
   70:       label: "Due today",
   71:       overdue: false,
   72:     };
   73:   }
   74: 
   75:   if (differenceInDays === 1) {
   76:     return {
   77:       label: "Due tomorrow",
   78:       overdue: false,
   79:     };
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   return {
   83:     label: `${differenceInDays} days remaining`,
   84:     overdue: false,
   85:   };
   86: }
   87: 
   88: export default function TeacherQuizAssignmentsPage() {
   89:   const { user } = useAuth();
   90: 
   91:   const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<
   92:     TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary[]
   93:   >([]);
   94: 
   95:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   96: 
   97:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   98: 
   99:   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  100: 
  101:   const loadAssignments = useCallback(async () => {
  102:     if (!user?.uid) {
  103:       setAssignments([]);
  104:       setLoading(false);
  105:       return;
  106:     }
  107: 
  108:     try {
  109:       setLoading(true);
  110:       setError("");
  111: 
  112:       const loadedAssignments = await getTeacherQuizAssignments(user.uid);
  113: 
  114:       setAssignments(loadedAssignments);
  115:     } catch (caughtError) {
  116:       console.error("Failed to load quiz assignments:", caughtError);
  117: 
  118:       setAssignments([]);
  119: 
  120:       setError(
  121:         caughtError instanceof Error
  122:           ? caughtError.message
  123:           : "Quiz assignments could not be loaded.",
  124:       );
  125:     } finally {
  126:       setLoading(false);
  127:     }
  128:   }, [user?.uid]);
  129: 
  130:   useEffect(() => {
  131:     void loadAssignments();
  132:   }, [loadAssignments]);
  133: 
  134:   const filteredAssignments = useMemo(() => {
  135:     const search = searchTerm.trim().toLowerCase();
  136: 
  137:     if (!search) {
  138:       return assignments;
  139:     }
  140: 
  141:     return assignments.filter(
  142:       (assignment) =>
  143:         assignment.title.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
  144:         assignment.className.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
  145:         assignment.description.toLowerCase().includes(search),
  146:     );
  147:   }, [assignments, searchTerm]);
  148: 
  149:   const totalStudents = useMemo(
  150:     () =>
  151:       assignments.reduce(
  152:         (total, assignment) => total + assignment.studentCount,
  153:         0,
  154:       ),
  155:     [assignments],
  156:   );
  157: 
  158:   const totalCompleted = useMemo(
  159:     () =>
  160:       assignments.reduce(
  161:         (total, assignment) => total + assignment.completedCount,
  162:         0,
  163:       ),
  164:     [assignments],
  165:   );
  166: 
  167:   const overallAverage = useMemo(() => {
  168:     const completedAssignments = assignments.filter(
  169:       (assignment) => assignment.completedCount > 0,
  170:     );
  171: 
  172:     if (completedAssignments.length === 0) {
  173:       return 0;
  174:     }
  175: 
  176:     return Math.round(
  177:       completedAssignments.reduce(
  178:         (total, assignment) => total + assignment.averagePercentage,
  179:         0,
  180:       ) / completedAssignments.length,
  181:     );
  182:   }, [assignments]);
  183: 
  184:   if (loading) {
  185:     return (
  186:       <div className="space-y-8">
  187:         <Skeleton className="h-56 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  188: 
  189:         <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  190:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  191:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  192:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  193:           <Skeleton className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  194:         </div>
  195: 
  196:         <Skeleton className="h-96 rounded-3xl" />
  197:       </div>
  198:     );
  199:   }
  200: 
  201:   return (
  202:     <div className="space-y-8">
  203:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-700 via-indigo-700 to-blue-700 p-7 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-9">
  204:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  205:           <div>
  206:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-violet-100">
  207:               Quiz assessment
  208:             </p>
  209: 
  210:             <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
  211:               Quiz Markbooks
  212:             </h1>
  213: 
  214:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-violet-100">
  215:               Review quiz completion, scores, grades, XP and time taken across
  216:               your classes.
  217:             </p>
  218:           </div>
  219: 
  220:           <Link
  221:             href="/teacher/quiz-library"
  222:             className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700 transition hover:bg-violet-50"
  223:           >
  224:             <Brain className="h-4 w-4" />
  225:             Quiz Library
  226:           </Link>
  227:         </div>
  228:       </section>
  229: 
  230:       <section className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  231:         <SummaryCard
  232:           label="Quiz Assignments"
  233:           value={assignments.length}
  234:           description="Assigned quizzes"
  235:           icon={<Brain className="h-6 w-6" />}
  236:           iconClassName="bg-violet-50 text-violet-600"
  237:         />
  238: 
  239:         <SummaryCard
  240:           label="Students"
  241:           value={totalStudents}
  242:           description="Total assignment places"
  243:           icon={<Users className="h-6 w-6" />}
  244:           iconClassName="bg-blue-50 text-blue-600"
  245:         />
  246: 
  247:         <SummaryCard
  248:           label="Completed"
  249:           value={`${totalCompleted}/${totalStudents}`}
  250:           description="Submitted quizzes"
  251:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6" />}
  252:           iconClassName="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600"
  253:         />
  254: 
  255:         <SummaryCard
  256:           label="Average Score"
  257:           value={`${overallAverage}%`}
  258:           description="Across completed quizzes"
  259:           icon={<BarChart3 className="h-6 w-6" />}
  260:           iconClassName="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600"
  261:         />
  262:       </section>
  263: 
  264:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 sm:p-7">
  265:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  266:           <div>
  267:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-indigo-600">
  268:               Assigned quizzes
  269:             </p>
  270: 
  271:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  272:               Your quiz markbooks
  273:             </h2>
  274:           </div>
  275: 
  276:           <label className="relative block">
  277:             <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
  278: 
  279:             <input
  280:               type="search"
  281:               value={searchTerm}
  282:               onChange={(event) => setSearchTerm(event.target.value)}
  283:               placeholder="Search quizzes..."

  285:             />
  286:           </label>

  307:                 >
  308:                   <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4" />
  309:                   Try again
  310:                 </button>
  311:               </div>

  351:                           {assignment.className}
  352:                         </span>
  353: 
  354:                         <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold capitalize text-slate-600">
  355:                           {assignment.status}
  356:                         </span>

  372:                     </div>

  376:                     <Metric
  377:                       label="Students"
  378:                       value={`${assignment.studentCount}`}
  379:                       icon={<Users className="h-4 w-4" />}
  380:                     />
  381:

   37: type FirestoreTeacherResource = Omit<
   38:   SavedTeacherResource,
   39:   "id" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"
   40: > & {
   41:   createdAt?: {
   42:     toDate?: () => Date;
   43:   };
   44:   updatedAt?: {
   45:     toDate?: () => Date;
   46:   };
   47: };

   49: function convertTimestamp(
   50:   value: FirestoreTeacherResource["createdAt"],
   51: ): Date | null {
   52:   if (value && typeof value.toDate === "function") {
   53:     return value.toDate();
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   return null;
   57: }
   58: 
   59: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   60:   if (!value) {
   61:     return "Recently saved";
   62:   }
   63: 
   64:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   65:     day: "numeric",
   66:     month: "long",
   67:     year: "numeric",
   68:     hour: "2-digit",
   69:     minute: "2-digit",
   70:   }).format(value);
   71: }
   72: 
   73: function formatResourceType(value: string): string {
   74:   return value
   75:     .replace(/-/g, " ")
   76:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   77: }

   79: function BulletList({
