  172: export default function TeacherResourceViewerPage() {
  173:   const params = useParams<{
  174:     resourceId: string;
  175:   }>();
  176: 
  177:   const router = useRouter();
  178: 
  179:   const resourceId = params.resourceId;
  180: 
  181:   const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  182: 
  183:   const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true);
  184: 
  185:   const [resource, setResource] = useState<SavedTeacherResource | null>(null);
  186: 
  187:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  188: 
  189:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  190: 
  191:   const [isAssignModalOpen, setIsAssignModalOpen] = useState(false);
  192: 
  193:   useEffect(() => {
  194:     const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
  195:       setCurrentUser(user);
  196:       setAuthLoading(false);
  197:     });
  198: 
  199:     return unsubscribe;
  200:   }, []);
  201: 
  202:   const loadResource = useCallback(async () => {
  203:     if (!currentUser || !resourceId) {
  204:       setResource(null);
  205:       setLoading(false);
  206:       return;
  207:     }
  208: 
  209:     setLoading(true);
  210:     setError(null);
  211: 
  212:     try {
  213:       const resourceReference = doc(db, "teacherResources", resourceId);
  214: 
  215:       const resourceSnapshot = await getDoc(resourceReference);
  216: 
  217:       if (!resourceSnapshot.exists()) {
  218:         setResource(null);
  219:         setError("This teaching resource could not be found.");
  220:         return;
  221:       }
  222: 
  223:       const data = resourceSnapshot.data() as FirestoreTeacherResource;
  224: 
  225:       if (data.teacherId !== currentUser.uid) {
  226:         setResource(null);
  227:         setError("You do not have permission to view this resource.");
  228:         return;
  229:       }
  230: 
  231:       setResource({
  232:         id: resourceSnapshot.id,
  233:         teacherId: data.teacherId,
  234:         sourceResourceId: data.sourceResourceId,
  235:         title: data.title,
  236:         topic: data.topic,
  237:         resourceType: data.resourceType,
  238:         yearGroup: data.yearGroup,
  239:         examBoard: data.examBoard,
  240:         duration: data.duration,
  241:         difficulty: data.difficulty,
  242:         content: data.content,
  243:         status: data.status,
  244:         createdAt: convertTimestamp(data.createdAt),
  245:         updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),
  246:       });
  247:     } catch (caughtError) {
  248:       console.error("Failed to load teacher resource:", caughtError);
  249: 
  250:       setError(
  251:         caughtError instanceof Error
  252:           ? caughtError.message
  253:           : "The teaching resource could not be loaded.",
  254:       );
  255:     } finally {
  256:       setLoading(false);
  257:     }
  258:   }, [currentUser, resourceId]);
  259: 
  260:   useEffect(() => {
  261:     if (!authLoading) {
  262:       void loadResource();
  263:     }
  264:   }, [authLoading, loadResource]);
  265: 
  266:   if (authLoading || loading) {
  267:     return <ResourceViewerSkeleton />;
  268:   }
  269: 
  270:   if (error || !resource || !currentUser) {
  271:     return (
  272:       <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  273:         <Link
  274:           href="/teacher/resources"
  275:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-700"
  276:         >
  277:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  278:           Back to Resource Library
  279:         </Link>
  280: 
  281:         <div className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  282:           <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-red-100 text-red-600">
  283:             <AlertCircle className="h-7 w-7" />
  284:           </div>
  285: 
  286:           <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold text-red-950">
  287:             Resource unavailable
  288:           </h1>
  289: 
  290:           <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  291:             {error || "This teaching resource could not be found."}
  292:           </p>
  293: 
  294:           <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
  295:             <button
  296:               type="button"
  297:               onClick={() => void loadResource()}
  298:               className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
  299:             >
  300:               <RefreshCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  301:               Try again
  302:             </button>
  303: 
  304:             <button
  305:               type="button"
  306:               onClick={() => router.push("/teacher/resources")}
  307:               className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-red-700"
  308:             >
  309:               <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
  310:               Resource Library
  311:             </button>
  312:           </div>
  313:         </div>
  314:       </main>
  315:     );
  316:   }
  317: 
  318:   const content = resource.content;
  319: 
  320:   return (
  321:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  322:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  323:         <Link
  324:           href="/teacher/resources"
  325:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-600 transition hover:text-indigo-700"
  326:         >
  327:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  328:           Back to Resource Library
  329:         </Link>
  330: 
  331:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 xl:flex-row xl:items-center">
  332:           <button
  333:             type="button"
  334:             onClick={() => {
  335:               setIsAssignModalOpen(true);
  336:             }}
  337:             disabled={resource.status !== "published"}
  338:             title={
  339:               resource.status === "published"
  340:                 ? "Assign this resource to a class"
  341:                 : "Publish this resource before assigning it"
  342:             }
  343:             className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-300 disabled:text-slate-600"
  344:           >
  345:             <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
  346:             Assign to class
  347:           </button>
  348: 
  349:           <Link
  350:             href={`/teacher/resources/${resource.id}/edit`}
  351:             className="inline-flex min-h-12 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700 transition hover:bg-indigo-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:ring-offset-2"
  352:           >
  353:             <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
  354:             Edit resource
  355:           </Link>
  356: 
  357:           <ExportMenu resource={resource} />
  358: 
  359:           <ResourcePublishControls
  360:             resourceId={resource.id}
  361:             status={resource.status}
  362:             content={resource.content}
  363:             onStatusChange={(newStatus) => {
  364:               setResource((current) => {
  365:                 if (!current) {
  366:                   return current;
  367:                 }
  368:

   43: function formatDate(value: Date | null, includeTime = false): string {
   44:   if (!value) {
   45:     return "â€”";
   46:   }
   47: 
   48:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   49:     "en-GB",
   50:     includeTime
   51:       ? {
   52:           day: "2-digit",
   53:           month: "short",
   54:           year: "numeric",
   55:           hour: "2-digit",
   56:           minute: "2-digit",
   57:         }
   58:       : {
   59:           day: "2-digit",
   60:           month: "short",
   61:           year: "numeric",
   62:         },
   63:   ).format(value);
   64: }
   65: 
   66: function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
   67:   if (seconds <= 0) {
   68:     return "No data";
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
   72: 
   73:   const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;
   74: 
   75:   if (minutes === 0) {
   76:     return `${remainingSeconds}s`;
   77:   }
   78: 
   79:   return `${minutes}m ${remainingSeconds}s`;
   80: }
   81: 
   82: function formatQualification(value: string): string {
   83:   if (!value.trim()) {
   84:     return "Not selected";
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   return value
   88:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   89:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) => character.toUpperCase());
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function getInitials(name: string): string {
   93:   return (
   94:     name
   95:       .split(" ")
   96:       .filter(Boolean)
   97:       .slice(0, 2)
   98:       .map((part) => part[0])
   99:       .join("")
  100:       .toUpperCase() || "ST"
  101:   );
  102: }

  104: export default function TeacherStudentAnalyticsPage() {
  105:   const params = useParams<{
  106:     studentId: string;
  107:   }>();
  108: 
  109:   const studentId = params.studentId;
  110: 
  111:   const { user } = useAuth();
  112: 
  113:   const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState<StudentAnalyticsData | null>(null);
  114: 
  115:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  116: 
  117:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  118: 
  119:   const loadAnalytics = useCallback(async () => {
  120:     if (!studentId || !user?.uid) {
  121:       return;
  122:     }
  123: 
  124:     try {
  125:       setLoading(true);
  126:       setError("");
  127: 
  128:       const loadedAnalytics = await getStudentAnalytics(studentId, user.uid);
  129: 
  130:       if (!loadedAnalytics) {
  131:         setAnalytics(null);
  132: 
  133:         setError(
  134:           "This student could not be found or is not enrolled in one of your classes.",
  135:         );
  136: 
  137:         return;
  138:       }
  139: 
  140:       setAnalytics(loadedAnalytics);
  141:     } catch (caughtError) {
  142:       console.error("Failed to load student analytics:", caughtError);
  143: 
  144:       setAnalytics(null);
  145: 
  146:       setError(
  147:         caughtError instanceof Error
  148:           ? caughtError.message
  149:           : "Student analytics could not be loaded.",
  150:       );
  151:     } finally {
  152:       setLoading(false);
  153:     }
  154:   }, [studentId, user?.uid]);
  155: 
  156:   useEffect(() => {
  157:     void loadAnalytics();
  158:   }, [loadAnalytics]);
  159: 
  160:   const sortedOutstanding = useMemo(
  161:     () =>
  162:       analytics
  163:         ? [...analytics.outstandingActivities].sort((first, second) => {
  164:             const firstOverdue = first.status === "overdue";
  165: 
  166:             const secondOverdue = second.status === "overdue";
  167: 
  168:             if (firstOverdue !== secondOverdue) {
  169:               return firstOverdue ? -1 : 1;
  170:             }
  171: 
  172:             return (
  173:               (first.dueDate?.getTime() || Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) -
  174:               (second.dueDate?.getTime() || Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)
  175:             );
  176:           })
  177:         : [],
  178:     [analytics],
  179:   );
  180: 
  181:   if (loading) {
  182:     return (
  183:       <div className="space-y-8">
  184:         <Skeleton className="h-64 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  185: 
  186:         <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  187:           {Array.from({
  188:             length: 8,
  189:           }).map((_, index) => (
  190:             <Skeleton key={index} className="h-28 rounded-3xl" />
  191:           ))}
  192:         </div>
  193: 
  194:         <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full rounded-3xl" />
  195:       </div>
  196:     );
  197:   }
  198: 
  199:   if (!analytics || error) {
  200:     return (
  201:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8 text-center">
  202:         <AlertCircle className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-red-600" />
  203: 
  204:         <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  205:           Student analytics unavailable
  206:         </h1>
  207: 
  208:         <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-sm text-red-800">
  209:           {error || "The student analytics could not be loaded."}
  210:         </p>
  211: 
  212:         <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
  213:           <button
  214:             type="button"
  215:             onClick={() => {
  216:               void loadAnalytics();
  217:             }}
  218:             className="rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
  219:           >
  220:             Try again
  221:           </button>
  222: 
  223:           <Link
  224:             href="/teacher/students"
  225:             className="rounded-xl border border-red-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700"
  226:           >
  227:             Back to students
  228:           </Link>
  229:         </div>
  230:       </Card>
  231:     );
  232:   }
  233: 
  234:   const { student, classes, metrics } = analytics;
  235: 
  236:   return (
  237:     <div className="space-y-8">
  238:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-700 via-teal-700 to-cyan-700 p-7 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-9">
  239:         <Link
  240:           href="/teacher/students"
  241:           className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-100 hover:text-white"
  242:         >
  243:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  244:           All students
  245:         </Link>
  246: 
  247:         <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-7 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
  248:           <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
  249:             <div className="flex h-20 w-20 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-3xl bg-white/15 text-2xl font-black">
  250:               {getInitials(student.name)}
  251:             </div>
  252: 
  253:             <div>
  254:               <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-100">
  255:                 Student analytics
  256:               </p>
  257: 
  258:               <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
  259:                 {student.name}
  260:               </h1>
  261: 
  262:               <p className="mt-2 text-emerald-100">
  263:                 {student.email || "No email available"}
  264:               </p>
  265: 
  266:               <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  267:                 {classes.map((studentClass) => (
  268:                   <span
  269:                     key={studentClass.id}
  270:                     className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold"
  271:                   >
  272:                     {studentClass.name}
  273:                   </span>
  274:                 ))}
  275: 
  276:                 {classes.length === 0 && (
  277:                   <span className="rounded-full bg-white/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold">
  278:                     No teacher class
  279:                   </span>
  280:                 )}
  281:               </div>
  282:             </div>
  283:           </div>
  284: 
  285:           <div className="grid min-w-72 grid-cols-2 gap-3 rounded-3xl border border-white/20 bg-white/10 p-5">
  286:             <HeroMetric label="Current grade" value={metrics.currentGrade} />
  287: 
  288:             <HeroMetric
  289:               label="Predicted grade"
  290:               value={metrics.predictedGrade}
  291:             />
  292: 
  293:             <HeroMetric
  294:               label="Completion"
  295:               value={`${metrics.completionRate}%`}
  296:             />
  297: 
  298:             <HeroMetric
  299:               label="Assessment average"
  300:               value={`${metrics.combinedAssessmentAverage}%`}
  301:             />
  302:           </div>
  303:         </div>
  304:       </section>
  305: 
  306:       <section className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  307:         <SummaryCard
  308:           label="Assignments"
  309:           value={`${metrics.completedAssignments}/${metrics.totalAssignments}`}
  310:           description="Completed activities"
  311:           icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6" />}
  312:           iconClassName="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600"
  313:         />
  314: 
  315:         <SummaryCard
  316:           label="Outstanding"
  317:           value={metrics.outstandingAssignments}
  318:           description={`${metrics.overdueAssignments} overdue`}
  319:           icon={<Clock3 className="h-6 w-6" />}
  320:           iconClassName="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600"
  321:         />
  322: 
  323:         <SummaryCard
  324:           label="Quiz Average"
  325:           value={`${metrics.quizAverage}%`}
  326:           description={`${metrics.completedQuizAssignments} completed quizzes`}
  327:           icon={<Brain className="h-6 w-6" />}
  328:           iconClassName="bg-violet-50 text-violet-600"
  329:         />
  330: 
  331:         <SummaryCard
  332:           label="Highest Score"
  333:           value={`${metrics.highestQuizScore}%`}
  334:           description={`Lowest ${metrics.lowestQuizScore}%`}
  335:           icon={<Award className="h-6 w-6" />}
  336:           iconClassName="bg-blue-50 text-blue-600"
  337:         />
  338: 
  339:         <SummaryCard
  340:           label="Written Exams"
  341:           value={`${metrics.completedExamAssignments}/${metrics.totalExamAssignments}`}
  342:           description={`${metrics.awaitingMarkingExamAssignments} awaiting marking`}
  343:           icon={<FileText className="h-6 w-6" />}
  344:           iconClassName="bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600"
  345:         />
  346: 
  347:         <SummaryCard
  348:           label="Exam Average"
  349:           value={`${metrics.examAverage}%`}
  350:           description={
  351:             metrics.completedExamAssignments > 0
  352:               ? `Best ${metrics.highestExamScore}%`
  353:               : "No marked written exams yet"
  354:           }
  355:           icon={<BarChart3 className="h-6 w-6" />}
  356:           iconClassName="bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-600"
  357:         />
  358: 
  359:         <SummaryCard
  360:           label="XP"
  361:           value={metrics.totalXP}
  362:           description={`${metrics.assignmentXP} earned from assignments`}
  363:           icon={<Star className="h-6 w-6" />}
  364:           iconClassName="bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-600"
  365:         />
  366: 
  367:         <SummaryCard
  368:           label="Streak"
  369:           value={`${metrics.streak} days`}
  370:           description="Current learning streak"
  371:           icon={<Flame className="h-6 w-6" />}
  372:           iconClassName="bg-orange-50 text-orange-600"
  373:         />
  374: 
  375:         <SummaryCard
  376:           label="Lessons"
  377:           value={metrics.completedLessons}
  378:           description={`${metrics.completedTopics} topics completed`}
  379:           icon={<BookOpen className="h-6 w-6" />}
  380:           iconClassName="bg-teal-50 text-teal-600"
  381:         />
  382: 
  383:         <SummaryCard
  384:           label="Trend"
  385:           value={
  386:             metrics.improvementTrend > 0
  387:               ? `+${metrics.improvementTrend}%`
  388:               : `${metrics.improvementTrend}%`
  389:           }
  390:           description={
  391:             metrics.improvementTrend >= 0
  392:               ? "Recent improvement"
  393:               : "Recent decline"
  394:           }
  395:           icon={
  396:             metrics.improvementTrend >= 0 ? (
  397:               <TrendingUp className="h-6 w-6" />
  398:             ) : (
  399:               <TrendingDown className="h-6 w-6" />
  400:             )
  401:           }
  402:           iconClassName={
  403:             metrics.improvementTrend >= 0
  404:               ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600"
  405:               : "bg-red-50 text-red-600"
  406:           }
  407:         />
  408:       </section>
  409: 
  410:       <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  411:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  412:           <SectionHeading
  413:             eyebrow="Performance"
  414:             title="Topic analysis"
  415:             description="Quiz and written-exam performance grouped by assessed topic."
  416:           />
  417: 
  418:           {analytics.topicPerformance.length === 0 ? (
  419:             <EmptyMessage message="No topic performance data is available yet." />
  420:           ) : (
  421:             <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
  422:               {analytics.topicPerformance.map((topic) => (
  423:                 <TopicRow key={topic.id} topic={topic} />
  424:               ))}
  425:             </div>
  426:           )}
  427:         </Card>
  428: 
  429:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  430:           <SectionHeading
  431:             eyebrow="Intervention"
  432:             title="Teacher recommendations"
  433:             description="Automatically generated from completion and assessment data."
  434:           />
  435: 
  436:           <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
  437:             {analytics.recommendations.map((recommendation) => (
  438:               <RecommendationCard
  439:                 key={recommendation.id}
  440:                 recommendation={recommendation}
  441:               />
  442:             ))}
  443:           </div>
  444:         </Card>
  445:       </section>
  446: 
  447:       <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  448:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  449:           <SectionHeading
  450:             eyebrow="Strengths"
  451:             title="Secure areas"
  452:             description="Topics currently assessed at 70% or higher."
  453:           />
  454: 
  455:           {analytics.strongestTopics.length === 0 ? (
  456:             <EmptyMessage message="No secure assessed topics have been identified yet." />
  457:           ) : (
  458:             <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
  459:               {analytics.strongestTopics.map((topic) => (
  460:                 <TopicHighlight key={topic.id} topic={topic} positive />
  461:               ))}
  462:             </div>
  463:           )}
  464:         </Card>
  465: 
  466:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  467:           <SectionHeading
  468:             eyebrow="Support"
  469:             title="Priority areas"
  470:             description="Topics currently assessed below 50%."
  471:           />
  472: 
  473:           {analytics.weakestTopics.length === 0 ? (
  474:             <EmptyMessage message="No priority assessed topics are currently identified." />
  475:           ) : (
  476:             <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
  477:               {analytics.weakestTopics.map((topic) => (
  478:                 <TopicHighlight key={topic.id} topic={topic} positive={false} />
  479:               ))}
  480:             </div>
  481:           )}
  482:         </Card>
  483:       </section>
  484: 
  485:       <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-0">
  486:         <div className="border-b border-slate-200 p-6 sm:p-7">
  487:           <SectionHeading
  488:             eyebrow="Learning history"
  489:             title="Recent activity"
  490:             description="The studentâ€™s most recent completed learning activities."
  491:           />
  492:         </div>
  493: 
  494:         {analytics.recentActivities.length === 0 ? (
  495:           <EmptyMessage message="No completed activity has been recorded yet." />
  496:         ) : (
  497:           <div className="overflow-x-auto">
  498:             <table className="min-w-full">
  499:               <thead className="bg-slate-50">
  500:                 <tr className="text-left text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  501:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Activity</th>
  502: 
  503:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Type</th>
  504: 
  505:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Score</th>
  506: 
  507:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">XP</th>
  508: 
  509:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Time</th>
  510: 
  511:                   <th className="px-6 py-4">Completed</th>
  512:                 </tr>
  513:               </thead>
  514: 
  515:               <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
  516:                 {analytics.recentActivities.map((activity) => (
  517:                   <ActivityRow key={activity.id} activity={activity} />
  518:                 ))}
  519:               </tbody>
  520:             </table>
  521:           </div>
  522:         )}
  523:       </Card>
  524: 
  525:       <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  526:         <SectionHeading
  527:           eyebrow="Outstanding work"
  528:           title="Assignments requiring attention"
  529:           description="Incomplete and overdue activities assigned to this student."
  530:         />
  531: 
  532:         {sortedOutstanding.length === 0 ? (
  533:           <EmptyMessage message="There is no outstanding assigned work." />
  534:         ) : (
  535:           <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
  536:             {sortedOutstanding.map((activity) => (
  537:               <OutstandingCard key={activity.id} activity={activity} />
  538:             ))}
  539:           </div>
  540:         )}
  541:       </Card>
  542: 
  543:       <section className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
  544:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  545:           <SectionHeading
  546:             eyebrow="Programme"
  547:             title="Course details"
  548:             description="The studentâ€™s selected learning programme."
  549:           />
  550: 
  551:           <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
  552:             <DetailRow
  553:               label="Qualification"
  554:               value={formatQualification(student.qualification)}
  555:             />
  556: 
  557:             <DetailRow
  558:               label="Exam Board"
  559:               value={student.examBoard.trim().toUpperCase() || "Not selected"}
  560:             />
  561: 
  562:             <DetailRow
  563:               label="Current Course"
  564:               value={formatQualification(student.currentCourse)}
  565:             />
  566: 
  567:             <DetailRow
  568:               label="Classes"
  569:               value={
  570:                 classes.map((studentClass) => studentClass.name).join(", ") ||
  571:                 "No classes"
  572:               }
  573:             />
  574: 
  575:             <DetailRow
  576:               label="Average Quiz Time"
  577:               value={formatDuration(metrics.averageTimeTakenSeconds)}
  578:             />
  579:           </div>
  580:         </Card>
  581: <StudentIntelligenceRecord studentId={studentId} />
  582:         <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  583:           <SectionHeading
  584:             eyebrow="Progress"
  585:             title="Learning totals"
  586:             description="Recorded curriculum and assignment progress."
  587:           />
  588: 
  589:           <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
  590:             <MiniMetric
  591:               label="Resources"
  592:               value={`${metrics.completedResourceAssignments}/${metrics.totalResourceAssignments}`}
  593:               icon={<BookOpen className="h-5 w-5" />}
  594:             />
  595: 
  596:             <MiniMetric
  597:               label="Quizzes"
  598:               value={`${metrics.completedQuizAssignments}/${metrics.totalQuizAssignments}`}
  599:               icon={<Brain className="h-5 w-5" />}
  600:             />
  601: 
  602:             <MiniMetric
  603:               label="Written Exams"
  604:               value={`${metrics.completedExamAssignments}/${metrics.totalExamAssignments}`}
  605:               icon={<FileText className="h-5 w-5" />}
  606:             />
  607: 
  608:             <MiniMetric
  609:               label="Topics"
  610:               value={`${metrics.completedTopics}`}
  611:               icon={<Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
  612:             />
  613: 
  614:             <MiniMetric
  615:               label="Units"
  616:               value={`${metrics.completedUnits}`}
  617:               icon={<GraduationCap className="h-5 w-5" />}
  618:             />
  619:           </div>
  620:         </Card>
  621:       </section>
  622: 
  623:       {user?.uid && (
  624:         <section
  625:           id="progress-report"
  626:           className="scroll-mt-6"
  627:         >
  628:           <StudentProgressReportPanel
  629:             teacherId={user.uid}
  630:             studentId={studentId}
  631:           />
  632:         </section>
  633:       )}
  634:     </div>
  635:   );
  636: }
  637: 
  638: function HeroMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  639:   return (
  640:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/10 p-4">
  641:       <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-100">
  642:         {label}
  643:       </p>
  644: 
  645:       <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{value}</p>
  646:     </div>
  647:   );
  648: }
  649: 
  650: function SummaryCard({
  651:   label,
  652:   value,
  653:   description,
  654:   icon,
  655:   iconClassName,
  656: }: {
  657:   label: string;
  658:   value: number | string;
  659:   description: string;
  660:   icon: React.ReactNode;
  661:   iconClassName: string;
  662: }) {
  663:   return (
  664:     <Card className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  665:       <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  666:         <div>
  667:           <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">{label}</p>
  668: 
  669:           <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  670: 
  671:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{description}</p>
  672:         </div>
  673: 
  674:         <div
  675:           className={`flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl ${iconClassName}`}
  676:         >
  677:           {icon}
  678:         </div>
  679:       </div>
  680:     </Card>
  681:   );
  682: }
  683: 
  684: function SectionHeading({
  685:   eyebrow,
  686:   title,
  687:   description,
  688: }: {
  689:   eyebrow: string;
  690:   title: string;
  691:   description: string;
  692: }) {
  693:   return (
  694:     <div>
  695:       <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-teal-600">
  696:         {eyebrow}
  697:       </p>
  698: 
  699:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{title}</h2>
  700: 
  701:       <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{description}</p>
  702:     </div>
  703:   );
  704: }
  705: 
  706: function TopicRow({ topic }: { topic: StudentTopicPerformance }) {
  707:   const barClassName =
  708:     topic.classification === "strength"
  709:       ? "bg-emerald-500"
  710:       : topic.classification === "support"
  711:         ? "bg-red-500"
  712:         : "bg-amber-500";
  713: 
  714:   return (
  715:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  716:       <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  717:         <div>
  718:           <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">{topic.topic}</p>
  719: 
  720:           <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
  721:             {topic.attempts} attempt
  722:             {topic.attempts === 1 ? "" : "s"}
  723:           </p>
  724:         </div>
  725: 
  726:         <p className="font-black text-slate-900">{topic.averageScore}%</p>
  727:       </div>
  728: 
  729:       <div className="mt-3 h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-200">
  730:         <div
  731:           className={`h-full rounded-full ${barClassName}`}
  732:           style={{
  733:             width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, topic.averageScore))}%`,
  734:           }}
  735:         />
  736:       </div>
  737:     </div>
  738:   );
  739: }
  740: 
  741: function TopicHighlight({
  742:   topic,
  743:   positive,
  744: }: {
  745:   topic: StudentTopicPerformance;
  746:   positive: boolean;
  747: }) {
  748:   return (
  749:     <div
  750:       className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 ${
  751:         positive
  752:           ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50"
  753:           : "border-red-200 bg-red-50"
  754:       }`}
  755:     >
  756:       <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
  757:         <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">{topic.topic}</p>
  758: 
  759:         <p
  760:           className={`font-black ${
  761:             positive ? "text-emerald-700" : "text-red-700"
  762:           }`}
  763:         >
  764:           {topic.averageScore}%
  765:         </p>
  766:       </div>
  767:     </div>
  768:   );
  769: }
  770: 
  771: function RecommendationCard({
  772:   recommendation,
  773: }: {
  774:   recommendation: StudentAnalyticsRecommendation;
  775: }) {
  776:   const classes =
  777:     recommendation.priority === "high"
  778:       ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50"
  779:       : recommendation.priority === "medium"
  780:         ? "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50"
  781:         : "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50";
  782: 
  783:   return (
  784:     <div className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 ${classes}`}>
  785:       <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
  786:         {recommendation.priority === "positive" ? (
  787:           <Sparkles className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
  788:         ) : (
  789:           <Lightbulb className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-amber-600" />
  790:         )}
  791: 
  792:         <div>
  793:           <p className="font-black text-slate-950">{recommendation.title}</p>
  794: 
  795:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-700">
  796:             {recommendation.description}
  797:           </p>
  798:         </div>
  799:       </div>
  800:     </div>
  801:   );
  802: }
  803: 
  804: function ActivityRow({ activity }: { activity: StudentAnalyticsActivity }) {
  805:   return (
  806:     <tr className="hover:bg-slate-50">
  807:       <td className="px-6 py-5">
  808:         <p className="font-bold text-slate-950">{activity.title}</p>
  809: 
  810:         <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{activity.className}</p>
  811:       </td>
  812: 
  813:       <td className="px-6 py-5">
  814:         <span
  815:           className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold ${
  816:             activity.type === "quiz"
  817:               ? "bg-violet-100 text-violet-700"
  818:               : activity.type === "exam"
  819:                 ? "bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700"
  820:                 : "bg-teal-100 text-teal-700"
  821:           }`}
  822:         >
  823:           {activity.type === "quiz"
  824:             ? "Quiz"
  825:             : activity.type === "exam"
  826:               ? "Written Exam"
  827:               : "Resource"}
  828:         </span>
  829:       </td>
  830: 
  831:       <td className="px-6 py-5 font-semibold text-slate-700">
  832:         {activity.percentage !== null ? `${activity.percentage}%` : "Completed"}
  833:       </td>
  834: 
  835:       <td className="px-6 py-5 font-semibold text-slate-700">
  836:         {activity.earnedXP > 0 ? `â­ ${activity.earnedXP}` : "â€”"}
  837:       </td>
  838: 
  839:       <td className="px-6 py-5 font-semibold text-slate-700">
  840:         {formatDuration(activity.timeTakenSeconds)}
  841:       </td>
  842: 
  843:       <td className="px-6 py-5 text-sm text-slate-600">
  844:         {formatDate(activity.completedAt, true)}
  845:       </td>
  846:     </tr>
  847:   );
  848: }
  849: 
  850: function OutstandingCard({ activity }: { activity: StudentAnalyticsActivity }) {
  851:   const overdue = activity.status === "overdue";
  852: 
  853:   return (
  854:     <div
  855:       className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 ${
  856:         overdue ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50" : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50"
  857:       }`}
  858:     >
  859:       <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  860:         <div>
  861:           <p className="font-black text-slate-950">{activity.title}</p>
  862: 
  863:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">{activity.className}</p>
  864:         </div>
  865: 
  866:         <StatusBadge status={activity.status} />
  867:       </div>
  868: 
  869:       <p className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">
  870:         <CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4" />
  871:         Due {formatDate(activity.dueDate)}
  872:       </p>
  873:     </div>
  874:   );
  875: }
  876: 
  877: function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: AnalyticsAssignmentStatus }) {
  878:   if (status === "overdue") {
  879:     return (
  880:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-red-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-red-700">
  881:         <AlertCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  882:         Overdue
  883:       </span>
  884:     );
  885:   }
  886: 
  887:   if (status === "in_progress") {
  888:     return (
  889:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-amber-700">
  890:         <Clock3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  891:         In progress
  892:       </span>
  893:     );
  894:   }
  895: 
  896:   if (status === "completed") {
  897:     return (
  898:       <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
  899:         <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  900:         Completed
  901:       </span>
  902:     );
  903:   }
  904: 
  905:   return (
  906:     <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-slate-200 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600">
  907:       <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
  908:       Not started
  909:     </span>
  910:   );
  911: }
  912:

   20: function simulatorTitle(
   21:   simulator: SimulatorType,
   22: ): string {
   23:   return simulator
   24:     .split("-")
   25:     .map(
   26:       (word) =>
   27:         word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() +
   28:         word.slice(1),
   29:     )
   30:     .join(" ");
   31: }
   32: 
   33: export default function VisualisersPage() {
   34:   const { profile } = useAuth();
   35: 
   36:   const coverage = useMemo(() => {
   37:     if (
   38:       !profile?.qualification ||
   39:       !profile.examBoard
   40:     ) {
   41:       return null;
   42:     }
   43:

   45:       profile.qualification,
   46:       profile.examBoard,
   47:     );
   48:   }, [
   49:     profile?.qualification,
   50:     profile?.examBoard,
   51:   ]);
   52:

   54:     useMemo<VisualiserEntry[]>(() => {
   55:       if (!coverage) {
   56:         return [];
   57:       }

   60:         new Set<string>();
   61: 
   62:       const result:
   63:         VisualiserEntry[] = [];

   67:         coverage.units

   71:           unitCoverage.topics
   72:         ) {
   73:           const firstLesson =
   74:             topic.lessons[0];
   75: 
   76:           if (
   77:             topic.simulator &&
   78:             firstLesson
   79:           ) {
   80:             const key =
   81:               `${topic.id}:${topic.simulator}:${firstLesson.id}`;
   82: 
   83:             if (!seen.has(key)) {
   84:               seen.add(key);
   85: 
   86:               result.push({
   87:                 key,
   88:                 simulator:
   89:                   topic.simulator,
   90:                 title:
   91:                   simulatorTitle(

   99:                   `/learn/${topic.id}?lesson=${firstLesson.id}`,
  100:               });
  101:             }
  102:           }
  103: 
  104:           for (
  105:             const lesson of
  106:             topic.lessons
  107:           ) {
  108:             if (!lesson.simulator) {
  109:               continue;
  110:             }
  111: 
  112:             const key =
  113:               `${topic.id}:${lesson.simulator}:${lesson.id}`;
  114: 
  115:             if (seen.has(key)) {
  116:               continue;
  117:             }
  118: 
  119:             seen.add(key);
  120: 
  121:             result.push({
  122:               key,
  123:               simulator:
  124:                 lesson.simulator,
  125:               title:
  126:                 simulatorTitle(
  127:                   lesson.simulator,
  128:                 ),

  130:                 topic.title,

  132:                 lesson.title,

  134:                 `/learn/${topic.id}?lesson=${lesson.id}`,
  135:             });
  136:           }
  137:         }

  164:           href="/profile/curriculum"

  175:       <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-950 via-blue-950 to-indigo-950 p-8 text-white shadow-xl">
  176:         <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-cyan-200">
  177:           Interactive learning labs
  178:         </p>

FILE: components\adaptive\ReviewSchedule.tsx

    1: import type { AdaptiveTopicMastery } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    2: 
    3: export default function ReviewSchedule({
    4:   topics,
    5: }: {
    6:   topics: AdaptiveTopicMastery[];
    7: }) {
    8:   const upcoming = [...topics]
    9:     .sort(
   10:       (first, second) =>
   11:         first.nextReviewAt.getTime() - second.nextReviewAt.getTime(),
   12:     )
   13:     .slice(0, 6);
   14: 
   15:   return (
   16:     <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6">
   17:       <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-teal-600">
   18:         Spaced retrieval
   19:       </p>
   20:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
   21:         Review schedule
   22:       </h2>
   23: 
   24:       <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
   25:         {upcoming.map((topic) => {
   26:           const due = topic.nextReviewAt.getTime() <= Date.now();
   27: 
   28:           return (
   29:             <div
   30:               key={topic.id}
   31:               className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4"
   32:             >
   33:               <div>
   34:                 <p className="font-black text-slate-900">{topic.topic}</p>
   35:                 <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
   36:                   Every {topic.reviewIntervalDays} day
   37:                   {topic.reviewIntervalDays === 1 ? "" : "s"}
   38:                 </p>
   39:               </div>
   40: 
   41:               <span
   42:                 className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
   43:                   due ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700" : "bg-teal-100 text-teal-700"
   44:                 }`}
   45:               >
   46:                 {due
   47:                   ? "Due now"
   48:                   : topic.nextReviewAt.toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}
   49:               </span>
   50:             </div>
   51:           );
   52:         })}
   53:       </div>
   54:     </div>
   55:   );
   56: }

FILE: components\exam-trainer\ExamQuestionTrainer.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    4: import Link from "next/link";
    5: 
    6: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
    7: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    8: import {
    9:   examTrainerQuestionBank,
   10:   examTrainerTopics,
   11: } from "@/data/examTrainerQuestionBank";
   12: import {
   13:   createExamTrainerAttempt,
   14:   createExamTrainerAttemptId,
   15:   getLatestInProgressExamTrainerAttempt,
   16:   saveExamTrainerDraft,
   17:   submitExamTrainerAttempt,
   18: } from "@/services/examTrainerAttemptService";
   19: import {
   20:   buildExamQuestions,
   21:   markExamTrainerAttempt,
   22: } from "@/services/examTrainerService";
   23: import type {
   24:   ExamTrainerAnswer,
   25:   ExamTrainerAttempt,
   26:   ExamTrainerDifficulty,
   27:   ExamTrainerQuestion,
   28:   ExamTrainerReport,
   29: } from "@/types/examTrainer";
   30: 
   31: type Stage = "loading" | "setup" | "exam" | "marking" | "report";
   32: 
   33: function formatSeconds(totalSeconds: number): string {
   34:   const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
   35:   const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
   36:   return `${minutes}:${seconds.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
   37: }
   38: 
   39: export default function ExamQuestionTrainer() {
   40:   const { user, profile } = useAuth();
   41: 
   42:   const [stage, setStage] = useState<Stage>("loading");
   43:   const [topic, setTopic] = useState("all");
   44:   const [difficulty, setDifficulty] = useState<ExamTrainerDifficulty | "all">(
   45:     "all",
   46:   );
   47:   const [questionCount, setQuestionCount] = useState(6);
   48:   const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState<ExamTrainerAttempt | null>(null);
   49:   const [report, setReport] = useState<ExamTrainerReport | null>(null);
   50:   const [resumeAttempt, setResumeAttempt] = useState<ExamTrainerAttempt | null>(
   51:     null,
   52:   );
   53:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   54: 
   55:   const submittingRef = useRef(false);
   56:   const lastSavedSignatureRef = useRef("");
   57: 
   58:   const questions = attempt?.questions ?? [];
   59:   const answers = attempt?.answers ?? [];
   60:   const currentIndex = attempt?.currentQuestionIndex ?? 0;
   61:   const secondsRemaining = attempt?.secondsRemaining ?? 0;
   62:   const currentQuestion = questions[currentIndex];
   63: 
   64:   const availableCount = useMemo(
   65:     () =>
   66:       examTrainerQuestionBank.filter((question) => {
   67:         const topicMatches = topic === "all" || question.topic === topic;
   68:         const difficultyMatches =
   69:           difficulty === "all" || question.difficulty === difficulty;
   70: 
   71:         return topicMatches && difficultyMatches;
   72:       }).length,
   73:     [difficulty, topic],
   74:   );
   75: 
   76:   useEffect(() => {
   77:     if (!user) {
   78:       setStage("setup");
   79:       return;
   80:     }
   81: 
   82:     const userId = user.uid;
   83:     let active = true;
   84: 
   85:     async function loadResumeAttempt() {
   86:       try {
   87:         const existing = await getLatestInProgressExamTrainerAttempt(userId);
   88: 
   89:         if (active && existing) {
   90:           setResumeAttempt(existing);
   91:         }
   92:       } catch (loadError) {
   93:         console.error("Resume attempt load error:", loadError);
   94:       } finally {
   95:         if (active) {
   96:           setStage("setup");
   97:         }
   98:       }
   99:     }
  100: 
  101:     void loadResumeAttempt();
  102: 
  103:     return () => {
  104:       active = false;
  105:     };
  106:   }, [user]);
  107: 
  108:   useEffect(() => {
  109:     if (stage !== "exam" || !attempt) return;
  110: 
  111:     if (attempt.secondsRemaining <= 0) {
  112:       void submitExam();
  113:       return;
  114:     }
  115: 
  116:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  117:       setAttempt((current) =>
  118:         current
  119:           ? {
  120:               ...current,
  121:               secondsRemaining: Math.max(0, current.secondsRemaining - 1),
  122:               updatedAt: new Date(),
  123:             }
  124:           : current,
  125:       );
  126:     }, 1000);
  127: 
  128:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  129:   }, [attempt?.id, attempt?.secondsRemaining, stage]);
  130: 
  131:   useEffect(() => {
  132:     if (stage !== "exam" || !attempt) return;
  133: 
  134:     const signature = JSON.stringify({
  135:       id: attempt.id,
  136:       answers: attempt.answers,
  137:       currentQuestionIndex: attempt.currentQuestionIndex,
  138:       secondsRemaining: Math.floor(attempt.secondsRemaining / 5),
  139:     });
  140: 
  141:     if (signature === lastSavedSignatureRef.current) return;
  142: 
  143:     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
  144:       void saveExamTrainerDraft(attempt)
  145:         .then(() => {
  146:           lastSavedSignatureRef.current = signature;
  147:         })
  148:         .catch((saveError) => {
  149:           console.error("Exam trainer autosave error:", saveError);
  150:         });
  151:     }, 700);
  152: 
  153:     return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  154:   }, [attempt, stage]);
  155: 
  156:   function updateAttempt(
  157:     updater: (current: ExamTrainerAttempt) => ExamTrainerAttempt,
  158:   ) {
  159:     setAttempt((current) => (current ? updater(current) : current));
  160:   }
  161: 
  162:   async function startExam() {
  163:     if (!user) {
  164:       setError("Please sign in before starting an exam.");
  165:       return;
  166:     }
  167: 
  168:     const selectedQuestions = buildExamQuestions({
  169:       topic,
  170:       difficulty,
  171:       count: questionCount,
  172:       questionBank: examTrainerQuestionBank,
  173:     });
  174: 
  175:     if (selectedQuestions.length === 0) {
  176:       setError("No questions match the selected options.");
  177:       return;
  178:     }
  179: 
  180:     const startedAt = new Date();
  181:     const durationSeconds = Math.max(10, selectedQuestions.length * 3) * 60;
  182: 
  183:     const newAttempt: ExamTrainerAttempt = {
  184:       id: createExamTrainerAttemptId(user.uid, startedAt),
  185:       studentId: user.uid,
  186:       selectedTopic: topic,
  187:       selectedDifficulty: difficulty,
  188:       requestedQuestionCount: questionCount,
  189:       questions: selectedQuestions,
  190:       answers: selectedQuestions.map((question) => ({
  191:         questionId: question.id,
  192:         response: "",
  193:       })),
  194:       currentQuestionIndex: 0,
  195:       durationSeconds,
  196:       secondsRemaining: durationSeconds,
  197:       status: "in_progress",
  198:       report: null,
  199:       startedAt,
  200:       updatedAt: startedAt,
  201:       submittedAt: null,
  202:     };
  203: 
  204:     try {
  205:       await createExamTrainerAttempt(newAttempt);
  206:       setAttempt(newAttempt);
  207:       setResumeAttempt(null);
  208:       setReport(null);
  209:       setError("");
  210:       setStage("exam");
  211:     } catch (createError) {
  212:       console.error("Exam attempt creation error:", createError);
  213:       setError("The exam could not be created or saved.");
  214:     }
  215:   }
  216: 
  217:   function resumeSavedAttempt() {
  218:     if (!resumeAttempt) return;
  219: 
  220:     setAttempt(resumeAttempt);
  221:     setTopic(resumeAttempt.selectedTopic);
  222:     setDifficulty(resumeAttempt.selectedDifficulty);
  223:     setQuestionCount(resumeAttempt.requestedQuestionCount);
  224:     setError("");
  225:     setStage("exam");
  226:   }
  227: 
  228:   function updateAnswer(response: string) {
  229:     if (!currentQuestion) return;
  230: 
  231:     updateAttempt((current) => ({
  232:       ...current,
  233:       answers: current.answers.map((answer) =>
  234:         answer.questionId === currentQuestion.id
  235:           ? { ...answer, response }
  236:           : answer,
  237:       ),
  238:       updatedAt: new Date(),
  239:     }));

   35: const DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS = 8 * 60;
   36: const FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS = 5;
   37: 
   38: function parseQuizDurationSeconds(value: string): number {
   39:   const normalised = value.trim().toLowerCase();
   40: 
   41:   if (!normalised) {
   42:     return DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   const hoursMatch = normalised.match(
   46:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:hours?|hrs?|hr|h)\b/,
   47:   );
   48: 
   49:   const minutesMatch = normalised.match(
   50:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:minutes?|mins?|min|m)\b/,
   51:   );
   52: 
   53:   const secondsMatch = normalised.match(
   54:     /(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:seconds?|secs?|sec|s)\b/,
   55:   );
   56: 
   57:   const hours = hoursMatch ? Number(hoursMatch[1]) : 0;
   58:   const minutes = minutesMatch ? Number(minutesMatch[1]) : 0;
   59:   const seconds = secondsMatch ? Number(secondsMatch[1]) : 0;
   60: 
   61:   const totalSeconds = Math.round(
   62:     hours * 60 * 60 +
   63:       minutes * 60 +
   64:       seconds,
   65:   );
   66: 
   67:   return totalSeconds > 0
   68:     ? totalSeconds
   69:     : DEFAULT_QUIZ_DURATION_SECONDS;
   70: }
   71: 
   72: function getGrade(scorePercent: number) {
   73:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Grade 9";
   74:   if (scorePercent >= 80) return "Grade 8";
   75:   if (scorePercent >= 70) return "Grade 7";
   76:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Grade 6";
   77:   if (scorePercent >= 50) return "Grade 5";
   78:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "Grade 4";
   79:   if (scorePercent >= 30) return "Grade 3";
   80:   if (scorePercent >= 20) return "Grade 2";
   81:   return "Grade 1";
   82: }
   83: 
   84: function getMessage(scorePercent: number) {
   85:   if (scorePercent >= 90) return "Outstanding performance!";
   86:   if (scorePercent >= 75) return "Excellent work!";
   87:   if (scorePercent >= 60) return "Good progress!";
   88:   if (scorePercent >= 40) return "You are getting there.";
   89:   return "Keep practising. You can improve this.";
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function createIncident(
   93:   type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
   94:   questionNumber: number | null,
   95:   detail: string,
   96: ): QuizIntegrityIncident {
   97:   return {
   98:     id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
   99:     type,
  100:     occurredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  101:     questionNumber,
  102:     detail,
  103:   };
  104: }

  174:       (total, question) => total + question.xpReward,

  181:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  182:     return userAnswer === correctAnswer;
  183:   }).length;

  191:     const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  192:

  197:

  202:   const awardedXP =

  205:       : earnedXP;
  206:

  210:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();

  214:
