  218:       const correctAnswer = question.correctAnswer.trim().toLowerCase();
  219:       return userAnswer !== correctAnswer;
  220:     })
  221:     .slice(0, 3);
  222: 
  223:   useEffect(() => {
  224:     let cancelled = false;
  225: 
  226:     async function loadLinkedAssignment() {
  227:       if (!assignmentId) {
  228:         setLinkedAssignment(null);
  229:         setAssignmentLoading(false);
  230:         return;
  231:       }
  232: 
  233:       try {
  234:         setAssignmentLoading(true);
  235:         setAssignmentError("");
  236: 
  237:         const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "assignments", assignmentId));
  238: 
  239:         if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  240:           throw new Error("The linked quiz assignment could not be found.");
  241:         }
  242: 
  243:         const data = snapshot.data();
  244: 
  245:         if (data.type !== "quiz") {
  246:           throw new Error("This assignment is not a quiz assignment.");
  247:         }
  248: 
  249:         if (!data.classId || !data.teacherId) {
  250:           throw new Error(
  251:             "The assignment is missing its class or teacher information.",
  252:           );
  253:         }
  254: 
  255:         if (!cancelled) {
  256:           setLinkedAssignment({
  257:             id: snapshot.id,
  258:             classId: data.classId,
  259:             teacherId: data.teacherId,
  260:             resourceId: data.resourceId || quiz.topicId,
  261:             deliveryMode:
  262:               data.deliveryMode === "assessment" ? "assessment" : "practice",
  263:           });
  264:         }
  265:       } catch (error) {
  266:         console.error("Quiz assignment load error:", error);
  267: 
  268:         if (!cancelled) {
  269:           setAssignmentError(
  270:             error instanceof Error
  271:               ? error.message
  272:               : "The linked quiz assignment could not be loaded.",
  273:           );
  274:         }
  275:       } finally {
  276:         if (!cancelled) {
  277:           setAssignmentLoading(false);
  278:         }
  279:       }
  280:     }
  281: 
  282:     void loadLinkedAssignment();
  283: 
  284:     return () => {
  285:       cancelled = true;
  286:     };
  287:   }, [assignmentId, quiz.topicId]);
  288: 
  289:   useEffect(() => {
  290:     setTimeLeft(quizDurationSeconds);
  291:   }, [quiz.id, quizDurationSeconds]);
  292: 
  293:   useEffect(() => {
  294:     return () => {
  295:       if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  296:         clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  297:       }
  298:     };
  299:   }, []);
  300: 
  301:   function appendIntegrityIncident(
  302:     type: QuizIntegrityIncident["type"],
  303:     detail: string,
  304:   ) {
  305:     const incident = createIncident(
  306:       type,
  307:       currentQuestionNumber,
  308:       detail,
  309:     );
  310: 
  311:     integrityIncidentsRef.current = [
  312:       ...integrityIncidentsRef.current,
  313:       incident,
  314:     ];
  315:   }
  316: 
  317:   async function leaveFullscreenSafely() {
  318:     if (document.fullscreenElement) {
  319:       await document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => undefined);
  320:     }
  321:   }
  322: 
  323:   async function finishQuiz({
  324:     terminated = false,
  325:     reason = "",
  326:   }: {
  327:     terminated?: boolean;
  328:     reason?: string;
  329:   } = {}) {
  330:     if (finishingRef.current || showResults) {
  331:       return;
  332:     }
  333: 
  334:     finishingRef.current = true;
  335: 
  336:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  337:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  338:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  339:     }
  340: 
  341:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  342:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  343: 
  344:     if (terminated) {
  345:       /*
  346:        * Set the ref first so save effects see termination synchronously,
  347:        * before React finishes applying the state update.
  348:        */
  349:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = true;
  350:       setIntegrityTerminated(true);
  351:       setIntegrityTerminationReason(reason);
  352: 
  353:       appendIntegrityIncident(
  354:         "auto_submit",
  355:         reason || "The assessment was automatically submitted.",
  356:       );
  357:     }
  358: 
  359:     await leaveFullscreenSafely();
  360:     setShowResults(true);
  361:   }
  362: 
  363:   useEffect(() => {
  364:     if (
  365:       showResults ||
  366:       assignmentLoading ||
  367:       (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted)
  368:     ) {
  369:       return;
  370:     }
  371: 
  372:     const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
  373:       setTimeLeft((previousTime) => {
  374:         if (previousTime <= 1) {
  375:           window.clearInterval(timer);
  376: 
  377:           void finishQuiz({
  378:             terminated: assessmentMode,
  379:             reason: assessmentMode
  380:               ? "The assessment timer expired and the quiz was automatically submitted."
  381:               : "",
  382:           });
  383: 
  384:           return 0;
  385:         }
  386: 
  387:         return previousTime - 1;
  388:       });
  389:     }, 1000);
  390: 
  391:     return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  392:   }, [
  393:     showResults,
  394:     assignmentLoading,
  395:     assessmentMode,
  396:     integrityStarted,
  397:   ]);
  398: 
  399:   function beginFullscreenCountdown() {
  400:     if (
  401:       !assessmentMode ||
  402:       !integrityStarted ||
  403:       showResults ||
  404:       finishingRef.current ||
  405:       fullscreenExitActiveRef.current
  406:     ) {
  407:       return;
  408:     }
  409: 
  410:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = true;
  411: 
  412:     setFullscreenCountdown(FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS);
  413: 
  414:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  415:       "fullscreen_exit",
  416:       `Fullscreen was exited. A ${FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS}-second return countdown started.`,
  417:     );
  418: 
  419:     let remaining = FULLSCREEN_EXIT_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS;
  420: 
  421:     countdownTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
  422:       remaining -= 1;
  423:       setFullscreenCountdown(Math.max(remaining, 0));
  424: 
  425:       if (remaining <= 0) {
  426:         if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  427:           clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  428:           countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  429:         }
  430: 
  431:         void finishQuiz({
  432:           terminated: true,
  433:           reason:
  434:             "The learner exited fullscreen and did not return within 5 seconds.",
  435:         });
  436:       }
  437:     }, 1000);
  438:   }
  439: 
  440:   function resolveFullscreenExit() {
  441:     if (!fullscreenExitActiveRef.current) {
  442:       return;
  443:     }
  444: 
  445:     fullscreenExitActiveRef.current = false;
  446: 
  447:     if (countdownTimerRef.current) {
  448:       clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);
  449:       countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  450:     }
  451: 
  452:     setFullscreenCountdown(null);
  453: 
  454:     appendIntegrityIncident(
  455:       "fullscreen_restored",
  456:       "Fullscreen was restored before the five-second termination countdown expired.",
  457:     );
  458:   }
  459: 
  460:   useEffect(() => {
  461:     if (
  462:       !assessmentMode ||
  463:       !integrityStarted ||
  464:       showResults
  465:     ) {
  466:       return;
  467:     }
  468: 
  469:     function onFullscreenChange() {
  470:       if (finishingRef.current) {
  471:         return;
  472:       }
  473: 
  474:       if (
  475:         document.fullscreenElement ===
  476:         assessmentRootRef.current
  477:       ) {
  478:         resolveFullscreenExit();
  479:       } else {
  480:         beginFullscreenCountdown();
  481:       }
  482:     }
  483: 
  484:     function onVisibilityChange() {
  485:       if (finishingRef.current) {
  486:         return;
  487:       }
  488: 
  489:       if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") {
  490:         appendIntegrityIncident(
  491:           "page_hidden",
  492:           "The assessment page became hidden.",
  493:         );
  494: 
  495:         setIntegrityWarning(
  496:           "The assessment page was hidden. This incident has been recorded.",
  497:         );
  498:       } else {
  499:         appendIntegrityIncident(
  500:           "page_visible",
  501:           "The assessment page became visible again.",
  502:         );
  503:       }
  504:     }
  505: 
  506:     document.addEventListener(
  507:       "fullscreenchange",
  508:       onFullscreenChange,
  509:     );
  510:     document.addEventListener(
  511:       "visibilitychange",
  512:       onVisibilityChange,
  513:     );
  514: 
  515:     return () => {
  516:       document.removeEventListener(
  517:         "fullscreenchange",
  518:         onFullscreenChange,
  519:       );
  520:       document.removeEventListener(
  521:         "visibilitychange",
  522:         onVisibilityChange,
  523:       );
  524:     };
  525:   }, [
  526:     assessmentMode,
  527:     integrityStarted,
  528:     showResults,
  529:     currentIndex,
  530:   ]);
  531: 
  532:   async function enterAssessmentMode() {
  533:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  534:       return;
  535:     }
  536: 
  537:     if (!document.fullscreenEnabled) {
  538:       toast.error(
  539:         "Fullscreen is not available in this browser. Ask your teacher for support.",
  540:       );
  541:       return;
  542:     }
  543: 
  544:     try {
  545:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  546: 
  547:       const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  548:       setIntegritySessionStartedAt(startedAt);
  549:       setIntegrityStarted(true);
  550:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  551:       integrityTerminatedRef.current = false;
  552:       setIntegrityTerminated(false);
  553:       setIntegrityTerminationReason("");
  554:       finishingRef.current = false;
  555:       integrityIncidentsRef.current = [];
  556: 
  557:       toast.success("Monitored assessment started.");
  558:     } catch (error) {
  559:       console.error(
  560:         "Unable to enter monitored assessment mode:",
  561:         error,
  562:       );
  563: 
  564:       toast.error(
  565:         "Fullscreen assessment mode could not be started.",
  566:       );
  567:     }
  568:   }
  569: 
  570:   async function returnToFullscreen() {
  571:     if (!assessmentRootRef.current) {
  572:       return;
  573:     }
  574: 
  575:     try {
  576:       await assessmentRootRef.current.requestFullscreen();
  577:     } catch {
  578:       toast.error(
  579:         "Fullscreen could not be restored. Return before the countdown reaches zero.",
  580:       );
  581:     }
  582:   }
  583: 
  584:   useEffect(() => {
  585:     async function saveNormalQuizResult() {
  586:       if (
  587:         !showResults ||
  588:         resultSaved ||
  589:         !user ||
  590:         quizSaveInProgress.current
  591:       ) {
  592:         return;
  593:       }
  594: 
  595:       quizSaveInProgress.current = true;
  596: 
  597:       try {
  598:         await saveQuizResult({
  599:           uid: user.uid,
  600:           quizId: quiz.id,
  601:           topicId: quiz.topicId,
  602:           title: quiz.title,
  603:           scorePercent,
  604:           correctCount,
  605:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  606:           earnedXP:
  607:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  608:               ? 0
  609:               : awardedXP,
  610:         });
  611: 
  612:         setResultSaved(true);
  613: 
  614:         toast.success(
  615:           integrityTerminated
  616:             ? "Assessment result saved. No XP was awarded."
  617:             : `Quiz result saved! +${awardedXP} XP`,
  618:         );
  619:       } catch (error) {
  620:         console.error("Quiz save error:", error);
  621:         toast.error("Could not save quiz result.");
  622:       } finally {
  623:         quizSaveInProgress.current = false;
  624:       }
  625:     }
  626: 
  627:     void saveNormalQuizResult();
  628:   }, [
  629:     showResults,
  630:     resultSaved,
  631:     user,
  632:     quiz.id,
  633:     quiz.topicId,
  634:     quiz.title,
  635:     quiz.questions.length,
  636:     scorePercent,
  637:     correctCount,
  638:     awardedXP,
  639:     integrityTerminated,
  640:   ]);
  641: 
  642:   useEffect(() => {
  643:     async function saveLinkedAssignmentResult() {
  644:       if (
  645:         !showResults ||
  646:         !assignmentId ||
  647:         !linkedAssignment ||
  648:         assignmentResultSaved ||
  649:         !user ||
  650:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current
  651:       ) {
  652:         return;
  653:       }
  654: 
  655:       assignmentSaveInProgress.current = true;
  656: 
  657:       try {
  658:         await saveAssignmentResult({
  659:           assignmentId,
  660:           studentId: user.uid,
  661:           classId: linkedAssignment.classId,
  662:           teacherId: linkedAssignment.teacherId,
  663:           assignmentType: "quiz",
  664:           resourceId: linkedAssignment.resourceId || quiz.topicId,
  665:           score: correctCount,
  666:           totalQuestions: quiz.questions.length,
  667:           percentage: scorePercent,
  668:           earnedXP:
  669:             integrityTerminatedRef.current
  670:               ? 0
  671:               : awardedXP,
  672:           timeTakenSeconds: Math.max(0, quizDurationSeconds - timeLeft),
  673: 
  674:           ...(assessmentMode
  675:             ? {
  676:                 deliveryMode: "assessment" as const,
  677:                 integritySessionStartedAt,
  678:                 integrityIncidents: integrityIncidentsRef.current,
  679:                 integrityTerminated,
  680:                 integrityTerminationReason,
  681:               }
  682:             : {
  683:                 deliveryMode: "practice" as const,
  684:               }),
  685:         });
  686: 
  687:         setAssignmentResultSaved(true);
  688: 
  689:         toast.success(
  690:           integrityTerminated
  691:             ? "Assessment automatically submitted."
  692:             : "Assignment marked as completed.",
  693:         );
  694:       } catch (error) {
  695:         console.error("Assignment result save error:", error);
  696: 
  697:         toast.error(
  698:           error instanceof Error
  699:             ? error.message
  700:             : "Could not mark the assignment as completed.",
  701:         );
  702:       } finally {
  703:         assignmentSaveInProgress.current = false;
  704:       }
  705:     }
  706: 
  707:     void saveLinkedAssignmentResult();
  708:   }, [
  709:     showResults,
  710:     assignmentId,
  711:     linkedAssignment,
  712:     assignmentResultSaved,
  713:     user,
  714:     quiz.topicId,
  715:     quiz.questions.length,
  716:     correctCount,
  717:     scorePercent,
  718:     awardedXP,
  719:     timeLeft,
  720:     quizDurationSeconds,
  721:     assessmentMode,
  722:     integritySessionStartedAt,
  723:     integrityTerminated,
  724:     integrityTerminationReason,
  725:   ]);
  726: 
  727:   function formatTime(seconds: number) {
  728:     const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  729:     const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;
  730: 
  731:     return `${minutes}:${remainingSeconds
  732:       .toString()
  733:       .padStart(2, "0")}`;
  734:   }
  735: 
  736:   function saveAnswer(answer: string) {
  737:     if (assessmentMode && !integrityStarted) {
  738:       return;
  739:     }
  740: 
  741:     setAnswers((previousAnswers) => ({
  742:       ...previousAnswers,
  743:       [currentQuestion.id]: answer,
  744:     }));
  745:   }
  746: 
  747:   function goNext() {
  748:     if (currentIndex < quiz.questions.length - 1) {
  749:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex + 1);
  750:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  751:       return;
  752:     }
  753: 
  754:     void finishQuiz();
  755:   }
  756: 
  757:   function goPrevious() {
  758:     if (currentIndex > 0) {
  759:       setCurrentIndex((previousIndex) => previousIndex - 1);
  760:       setIntegrityWarning("");
  761:     }
  762:   }
  763: 
  764:   if (assignmentLoading) {
  765:     return (
  766:       <Card>
  767:         <p className="font-bold text-slate-700">
  768:           Preparing your assigned quiz...
  769:         </p>
  770:       </Card>
  771:     );
  772:   }
  773: 
  774:   if (assignmentError) {
  775:     return (
  776:       <Card>
  777:         <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  778:           Quiz assignment unavailable
  779:         </h1>
  780: 
  781:         <p className="mt-3 text-red-700">
  782:           {assignmentError}
  783:         </p>
  784:       </Card>
  785:     );
  786:   }
  787: 
  788:   if (
  789:     assessmentMode &&
  790:     !showResults &&
  791:     !integrityStarted
  792:   ) {
  793:     return (
  794:       <div
  795:         ref={assessmentRootRef}
  796:         className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 p-6"
  797:       >
  798:         <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
  799:           <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border-0 p-0">
  800:             <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-indigo-950 to-violet-950 p-8 text-white">
  801:               <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-200">
  802:                 <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
  803: 
  804:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">
  805:                   Monitored Quiz Assessment
  806:                 </p>
  807:               </div>
  808: 
  809:               <h1 className="mt-4 text-4xl font-black">
  810:                 {quiz.title}
  811:               </h1>
  812: 
  813:               <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-indigo-100">
  814:                 {quiz.questions.length} questions Â· {quiz.estimatedTime}
  815:               </p>
  816:             </div>
  817: 
  818:             <div className="space-y-6 p-8">
  819:               <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5">
  820:                 <p className="font-black text-amber-950">
  821:                   Integrity monitoring
  822:                 </p>
  823: 
  824:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-900">
  825:                   This is integrity monitoring, not a guaranteed lockdown
  826:                   browser. The assessment records fullscreen exits and page
  827:                   visibility changes to give your teacher contextual evidence.
  828:                 </p>
  829:               </div>
  830: 
  831:               <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
  832:                 <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  833:                   <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  834:                     Fullscreen required
  835:                   </p>
  836: 
  837:                   <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  838:                     Leaving fullscreen starts a visible five-second countdown.
  839:                     If you do not return before it reaches zero, the quiz is
  840:                     automatically submitted.
  841:                   </p>
  842:                 </div>
  843: 
  844:                 <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  845:                   <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  846:                     Page visibility monitored
  847:                   </p>
  848: 
  849:                   <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  850:                     Switching away from the assessment is recorded with the
  851:                     current question number and timestamp.
  852:                   </p>
  853:                 </div>
  854:               </div>
  855: 
  856:               <button
  857:                 type="button"
  858:                 onClick={() => void enterAssessmentMode()}
  859:                 className="w-full rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 px-6 py-4 text-lg font-black text-white transition hover:bg-indigo-700"
  860:               >
  861:                 Enter Monitored Assessment
  862:               </button>
  863:             </div>
  864:           </Card>
  865:         </div>
  866:       </div>
  867:     );
  868:   }
  869: 
  870:   if (showResults) {
  871:     if (integrityTerminated) {
  872:       return (
  873:         <div className="space-y-6">
  874:           <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border-0 p-0">
  875:             <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-red-950 to-rose-900 p-8 text-white">
  876:               <div className="flex justify-center">
  877:                 <div className="rounded-full bg-red-500/20 p-4">
  878:                   <AlertTriangle className="h-10 w-10 text-red-200" />
  879:                 </div>
  880:               </div>
  881: 
  882:               <p className="mt-5 text-center text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-red-200">
  883:                 Monitored assessment
  884:               </p>
  885: 
  886:               <h1 className="mt-3 text-center text-4xl font-black">
  887:                 Assessment Ended
  888:               </h1>
  889: 
  890:               <p className="mt-3 text-center text-lg font-bold text-red-100">
  891:                 Automatically submitted due to an integrity event
  892:               </p>
  893:             </div>
  894: 
  895:             <div className="space-y-6 p-8">
  896:               <div className="rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-5">
  897:                 <p className="font-black text-red-950">
  898:                   Why the assessment ended
  899:                 </p>
  900: 
  901:                 <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  902:                   {integrityTerminationReason ||
  903:                     "The assessment was automatically submitted by the integrity-monitoring rules."}
  904:                 </p>
  905: 
  906:                 <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-red-800">
  907:                   Your answers up to the point of submission have been saved.
  908:                   Your teacher can review the raw score together with the
  909:                   integrity record.
  910:                 </p>
  911:               </div>
  912: 
  913:               <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
  914:                 <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  915:                   <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  916:                     Answers correct
  917:                   </p>
  918: 
  919:                   <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  920:                     {correctCount} / {quiz.questions.length}
  921:                   </p>
  922:                 </div>
  923: 
  924:                 <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5">
  925:                   <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  926:                     Raw score
  927:                   </p>
  928: 
  929:                   <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  930:                     {scorePercent}%
  931:                   </p>
  932:                 </div>
  933:

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import { useProgress } from "@/contexts/ProgressContext";
    6: 
    7: import SimulatorControls from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorControls";
    8: import SimulatorDifficulty from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorDifficulty";
    9: import SimulatorFeedback from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorFeedback";
   10: import SimulatorStats from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorStats";
   11: 
   12: import {
   13:   useSimulator,
   14:   type SimulatorDifficulty as DifficultyLevel,
   15: } from "@/components/Simulators/common/useSimulator";
   16: 
   17: type Question = {
   18:   values: number[];
   19: };
   20: 
   21: type ProcedureFeedbackType = "success" | "error" | "info" | null;
   22: 
   23: function createValues(length: number): number[] {
   24:   return Array.from({ length }, () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 20) + 1);
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function createQuestion(difficulty: DifficultyLevel): Question {
   28:   return {
   29:     values: createValues(
   30:       difficulty === "foundation" ? 5 : difficulty === "intermediate" ? 6 : 7,
   31:     ),
   32:   };
   33: }
   34: 
   35: function normaliseList(value: string): number[] {
   36:   return value
   37:     .split(/[\s,]+/)
   38:     .map((item) => Number(item))
   39:     .filter((item) => Number.isFinite(item));
   40: }
   41: 
   42: export default function BubbleSortSimulator() {
   43:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
   44: 
   45:   const simulator = useSimulator<Question>({
   46:     initialQuestion: createQuestion("foundation"),
   47:     generateQuestion: createQuestion,
   48: 
   49:     xpByDifficulty: {
   50:       foundation: 10,
   51:       intermediate: 15,
   52:       higher: 20,
   53:     },
   54: 
   55:     onAwardXP: addXP,
   56:   });
   57: 
   58:   const {
   59:     difficulty,
   60:     question,
   61: 
   62:     checked,
   63:     correct,
   64: 
   65:     hintVisible,
   66:     workingVisible,
   67: 
   68:     attempts,
   69:     correctAnswers,
   70:     accuracy,
   71:     xp,
   72:     streak,
   73: 
   74:     markAnswer,
   75:     resetQuestion,
   76:     newQuestion,
   77:     changeDifficulty,
   78: 
   79:     toggleHint,
   80:     toggleWorking,
   81:   } = simulator;
   82: 
   83:   /*
   84:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
   85:    * Scored challenge
   86:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
   87:    */
   88: 
   89:   const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
   90: 
   91:   const sortedValues = useMemo(
   92:     () => [...question.values].sort((a, b) => a - b),
   93:     [question.values],
   94:   );
   95: 
   96:   const submittedValues = normaliseList(answer);
   97: 
   98:   const canCheck = submittedValues.length === question.values.length;
   99: 
  100:   function handleCheck() {
  101:     if (!canCheck) {
  102:       return;
  103:     }
  104: 
  105:     const matches = sortedValues.every(
  106:       (value, index) => value === submittedValues[index],
  107:     );
  108: 
  109:     markAnswer(matches);
  110:   }
  111: 
  112:   function handleTryAgain() {
  113:     setAnswer("");
  114:     resetQuestion();
  115:   }
  116: 
  117:   /*
  118:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
  119:    * Procedural trainer state
  120:    * ---------------------------------------------------------
  121:    */
  122: 
  123:   const [procedureValues, setProcedureValues] = useState<number[]>(
  124:     question.values,
  125:   );
  126: 
  127:   const [compareIndex, setCompareIndex] = useState(0);
  128:   const [pass, setPass] = useState(1);
  129: 
  130:   const [swappedInPass, setSwappedInPass] = useState(false);
  131: 
  132:   const [selectedIndices, setSelectedIndices] = useState<number[]>([]);
  133: 
  134:   const [procedureComplete, setProcedureComplete] = useState(false);
  135: 
  136:   const [awaitingEndPass, setAwaitingEndPass] = useState(false);
  137: 
  138:   const [procedureCorrectSteps, setProcedureCorrectSteps] = useState(0);
  139: 
  140:   const [procedureMistakes, setProcedureMistakes] = useState(0);
  141: 
  142:   const [passesCompleted, setPassesCompleted] = useState(0);
  143: 
  144:   const [procedureFeedback, setProcedureFeedback] = useState("");
  145: 
  146:   const [procedureFeedbackType, setProcedureFeedbackType] =
  147:     useState<ProcedureFeedbackType>(null);
  148: 
  149:   const procedureAttempts = procedureCorrectSteps + procedureMistakes;
  150: 
  151:   const procedureAccuracy =
  152:     procedureAttempts === 0
  153:       ? 0
  154:       : Math.round((procedureCorrectSteps / procedureAttempts) * 100);
  155: 
  156:   const pairLeft = compareIndex;
  157:   const pairRight = compareIndex + 1;
  158: 
  159:   const currentLeft = procedureValues[pairLeft];
  160:   const currentRight = procedureValues[pairRight];
  161: 
  162:   const shouldSwap =
  163:     currentLeft !== undefined &&
  164:     currentRight !== undefined &&
  165:     currentLeft > currentRight;
  166: 
  167:   const lastComparisonIndex = procedureValues.length - pass - 1;
  168: 
  169:   const currentPairIsLastInPass = compareIndex >= lastComparisonIndex;
  170: 
  171:   /*
  172:    * Foundation:
  173:    * simulator automatically identifies the pair.
  174:    *
  175:    * Intermediate / Higher:
  176:    * student selects the pair.
  177:    */
  178:   const pairReady =
  179:     difficulty === "foundation" ||
  180:     (selectedIndices.length === 2 &&
  181:       selectedIndices.includes(pairLeft) &&
  182:       selectedIndices.includes(pairRight));
  183: 
  184:   function resetProcedure(values = question.values) {
  185:     setProcedureValues([...values]);
  186:     setCompareIndex(0);
  187:     setPass(1);
  188:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  189:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  190:     setProcedureComplete(false);
  191:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  192: 
  193:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  194:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  195:     setPassesCompleted(0);
  196: 
  197:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  198:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  199:   }
  200: 
  201:   function handleNewQuestion() {
  202:     setAnswer("");
  203: 
  204:     /*
  205:      * We clear the old procedure immediately.
  206:      * When the hook generates the next question, the fallback rendering
  207:      * below uses question.values until the student starts the trainer.
  208:      */
  209:     setProcedureValues([]);
  210:     setCompareIndex(0);
  211:     setPass(1);
  212:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  213:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  214:     setProcedureComplete(false);
  215:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  216: 
  217:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  218:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  219:     setPassesCompleted(0);
  220: 
  221:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  222:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  223: 
  224:     newQuestion();
  225:   }
  226: 
  227:   function handleDifficultyChange(nextDifficulty: DifficultyLevel) {
  228:     setAnswer("");
  229: 
  230:     setProcedureValues([]);
  231:     setCompareIndex(0);
  232:     setPass(1);
  233:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  234:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  235:     setProcedureComplete(false);
  236:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  237: 
  238:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  239:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  240:     setPassesCompleted(0);
  241: 
  242:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  243:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  244: 
  245:     changeDifficulty(nextDifficulty);
  246:   }
  247: 
  248:   /*
  249:    * If a new question has been generated, procedureValues may temporarily
  250:    * be empty. In that case use the new question list.
  251:    */
  252:   const displayedProcedureValues =
  253:     procedureValues.length === question.values.length
  254:       ? procedureValues
  255:       : question.values;
  256: 
  257:   function ensureProcedureInitialised(): number[] {
  258:     if (procedureValues.length === question.values.length) {
  259:       return procedureValues;
  260:     }
  261: 
  262:     const initial = [...question.values];
  263: 
  264:     setProcedureValues(initial);
  265: 
  266:     return initial;
  267:   }
  268: 
  269:   function selectProcedureValue(index: number) {
  270:     if (difficulty === "foundation" || procedureComplete || awaitingEndPass) {
  271:       return;
  272:     }
  273: 
  274:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  275:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  276: 
  277:     setSelectedIndices((current) => {
  278:       if (current.includes(index)) {
  279:         return current.filter((item) => item !== index);
  280:       }
  281: 
  282:       if (current.length >= 2) {
  283:         return [index];
  284:       }
  285: 
  286:       return [...current, index];
  287:     });
  288:   }
  289: 
  290:   function recordMistake(message: string) {
  291:     setProcedureMistakes((current) => current + 1);
  292:     setProcedureFeedback(message);
  293:     setProcedureFeedbackType("error");
  294:   }
  295: 
  296:   function recordSuccess(message: string) {
  297:     setProcedureCorrectSteps((current) => current + 1);
  298:     setProcedureFeedback(message);
  299:     setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  300:   }
  301: 
  302:   function validateSelectedPair(): boolean {
  303:     if (difficulty === "foundation") {
  304:       return true;
  305:     }
  306: 
  307:     if (selectedIndices.length !== 2) {
  308:       recordMistake(
  309:         "Select exactly two adjacent values before choosing Keep or Swap.",
  310:       );
  311: 
  312:       return false;
  313:     }
  314: 
  315:     const ordered = [...selectedIndices].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  316: 
  317:     if (ordered[1] - ordered[0] !== 1) {
  318:       recordMistake(
  319:         "Bubble sort compares adjacent values only. Select two neighbouring items.",
  320:       );
  321: 
  322:       return false;
  323:     }
  324: 
  325:     if (ordered[0] !== pairLeft || ordered[1] !== pairRight) {
  326:       recordMistake(
  327:         `That is not the next comparison. Bubble sort must now compare index ${pairLeft} with index ${pairRight}.`,
  328:       );
  329: 
  330:       return false;
  331:     }
  332: 
  333:     return true;
  334:   }
  335: 
  336:   function advanceAfterCorrectDecision(didSwap: boolean, nextValues: number[]) {
  337:     const nextSwappedInPass = swappedInPass || didSwap;
  338: 
  339:     setProcedureValues(nextValues);
  340:     setSwappedInPass(nextSwappedInPass);
  341:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  342: 
  343:     /*
  344:      * We have reached the end of the current pass.
  345:      */
  346:     if (currentPairIsLastInPass) {
  347:       /*
  348:        * Higher students must explicitly recognise the end of a pass.
  349:        */
  350:       if (difficulty === "higher") {
  351:         setAwaitingEndPass(true);
  352: 
  353:         setProcedureFeedback(
  354:           "Correct decision. You have reached the end of this pass. Decide what should happen next.",
  355:         );
  356: 
  357:         setProcedureFeedbackType("info");
  358: 
  359:         return;
  360:       }
  361: 
  362:       finishPass(nextSwappedInPass, nextValues);
  363: 
  364:       return;
  365:     }
  366: 
  367:     setCompareIndex((current) => current + 1);
  368:   }
  369: 
  370:   function finishPass(hadSwap: boolean, currentValues: number[]) {
  371:     setPassesCompleted((current) => current + 1);
  372:     setAwaitingEndPass(false);
  373:     setSelectedIndices([]);
  374: 
  375:     /*
  376:      * No swap during a complete pass means the list is sorted.
  377:      */
  378:     if (!hadSwap) {
  379:       setProcedureComplete(true);
  380: 
  381:       setProcedureFeedback(
  382:         "Excellent. A complete pass produced no swaps, so the list is sorted.",
  383:       );
  384: 
  385:       setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  386: 
  387:       return;
  388:     }
  389: 
  390:     /*
  391:      * Every pass fixes at least one value at the right-hand side.
  392:      */
  393:     if (pass >= currentValues.length - 1) {
  394:       setProcedureComplete(true);
  395: 
  396:       setProcedureFeedback(
  397:         "Excellent. All required passes are complete and the list is sorted.",
  398:       );
  399: 
  400:       setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  401: 
  402:       return;
  403:     }
  404: 
  405:     setPass((current) => current + 1);
  406:     setCompareIndex(0);
  407:     setSwappedInPass(false);
  408: 
  409:     setProcedureFeedback(
  410:       `Pass ${pass} complete. Begin pass ${pass + 1} from the left-hand side.`,
  411:     );
  412: 
  413:     setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  414:   }
  415: 
  416:   function handleSwap() {
  417:     if (procedureComplete || awaitingEndPass) {
  418:       return;
  419:     }
  420: 
  421:     const currentValues = ensureProcedureInitialised();
  422: 
  423:     if (!validateSelectedPair()) {
  424:       return;
  425:     }
  426: 
  427:     const left = currentValues[pairLeft];
  428:     const right = currentValues[pairRight];
  429: 
  430:     if (left === undefined || right === undefined) {
  431:       return;
  432:     }
  433: 
  434:     if (left <= right) {
  435:       recordMistake(
  436:         `${left} is not greater than ${right}. These values are already in ascending order, so they should be kept.`,
  437:       );
  438: 
  439:       return;
  440:     }
  441: 
  442:     const nextValues = [...currentValues];
  443: 
  444:     [nextValues[pairLeft], nextValues[pairRight]] = [
  445:       nextValues[pairRight],
  446:       nextValues[pairLeft],
  447:     ];
  448: 
  449:     recordSuccess(
  450:       `Correct. ${left} > ${right}, so the adjacent values must be swapped.`,
  451:     );
  452: 
  453:     advanceAfterCorrectDecision(true, nextValues);
  454:   }
  455: 
  456:   function handleKeep() {
  457:     if (procedureComplete || awaitingEndPass) {
  458:       return;
  459:     }
  460: 
  461:     const currentValues = ensureProcedureInitialised();
  462: 
  463:     if (!validateSelectedPair()) {
  464:       return;
  465:     }
  466: 
  467:     const left = currentValues[pairLeft];
  468:     const right = currentValues[pairRight];
  469: 
  470:     if (left === undefined || right === undefined) {
  471:       return;
  472:     }
  473: 
  474:     if (left > right) {
  475:       recordMistake(
  476:         `${left} > ${right}. Bubble sort requires these adjacent values to be swapped.`,
  477:       );
  478: 
  479:       return;
  480:     }
  481: 
  482:     recordSuccess(
  483:       `Correct. ${left} â‰¤ ${right}, so the values remain in this order.`,
  484:     );
  485: 
  486:     advanceAfterCorrectDecision(false, [...currentValues]);
  487:   }
  488: 
  489:   function handleEndPass() {
  490:     if (difficulty !== "higher" || procedureComplete) {
  491:       return;
  492:     }
  493: 
  494:     if (!awaitingEndPass) {
  495:       recordMistake(
  496:         "The pass is not finished yet. Continue comparing the next adjacent pair.",
  497:       );
  498: 
  499:       return;
  500:     }
  501: 
  502:     setProcedureCorrectSteps((current) => current + 1);
  503: 
  504:     finishPass(
  505:       swappedInPass ||
  506:         /*
  507:          * If the final comparison was a swap, procedureValues has already
  508:          * changed. We can determine whether the current pass contained a
  509:          * swap by comparing against the sorted state through swappedInPass,
  510:          * but the final action may have been the first swap in the pass.
  511:          *
  512:          * The safest indicator is that the list at this point differs from
  513:          * the pass-start ordering only through valid swaps. For the purpose
  514:          * of early termination, a pass with any final swap must continue.
  515:          *
  516:          * currentPairIsLastInPass plus shouldSwap before mutation cannot be
  517:          * used here after state updates, so we conservatively treat an end
  518:          * pass as having swaps unless swappedInPass is false AND the list is
  519:          * currently sorted.
  520:          */
  521:         !displayedProcedureValues.every(
  522:           (value, index, array) => index === 0 || array[index - 1] <= value,
  523:         ),
  524:       displayedProcedureValues,
  525:     );
  526:   }
  527: 
  528:   const working = `Starting list:
  529: 
  530: ${question.values.join(", ")}
  531: 
  532: Bubble sort procedure:
  533: 
  534: 1. Start at the left of the list.
  535: 2. Compare two adjacent values.
  536: 3. If the left value is greater than the right value, swap them.
  537: 4. Otherwise keep them in the same order.
  538: 5. Move one position to the right.
  539: 6. Continue until the end of the pass.
  540: 7. Repeat passes until a full pass produces no swaps.
  541: 
  542: Final sorted list:
  543: 
  544: ${sortedValues.join(", ")}`;
  545: 
  546:   const automaticPairIndices =
  547:     difficulty === "foundation" ? [pairLeft, pairRight] : [];
  548: 
  549:   const highlightedIndices =
  550:     difficulty === "foundation" ? automaticPairIndices : selectedIndices;
  551: 
  552:   return (
  553:     <section className="space-y-8 rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm md:p-8">
  554:       <header>
  555:         <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  556:           Algorithm laboratory
  557:         </p>
  558: 
  559:         <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">Bubble Sort Challenge</h2>
  560: 
  561:         <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  562:           Predict the final order, then perform Bubble Sort yourself using the
  563:           correct comparisons, swaps and passes.
  564:         </p>
  565:       </header>
  566: 
  567:       <SimulatorDifficulty
  568:         value={difficulty}
  569:         onChange={handleDifficultyChange}
  570:       />
  571: 
  572:       <SimulatorStats
  573:         attempts={attempts}
  574:         correct={correctAnswers}
  575:         accuracy={accuracy}
  576:         xp={xp}
  577:         streak={streak}
  578:       />
  579: 
  580:       {/* ---------------------------------------------------- */}
  581:       {/* Scored final-list challenge                           */}
  582:       {/* ---------------------------------------------------- */}
  583: 
  584:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6">
  585:         <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  586:           Final-order challenge
  587:         </p>
  588: 
  589:         <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black">
  590:           Sort this list into ascending order.
  591:         </h3>
  592: 
  593:         <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  594:           {question.values.map((value, itemIndex) => (
  595:             <div
  596:               key={`${value}-${itemIndex}`}
  597:               className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white text-xl font-black"
  598:             >
  599:               {value}
  600:             </div>
  601:           ))}
  602:         </div>
  603:       </section>
  604: 
  605:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  606:         <label htmlFor="bubble-answer" className="font-black">
  607:           Enter the final sorted list
  608:         </label>
  609: 
  610:         <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  611:           Separate values using commas or spaces.
  612:         </p>
  613: 
  614:         <input
  615:           id="bubble-answer"
  616:           value={answer}
  617:           disabled={checked}
  618:           onChange={(event) => setAnswer(event.target.value)}
  619:           placeholder="Example: 2, 3, 6, 7, 9"
  620:           className="mt-3 w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-300 px-5 py-4 font-mono text-xl font-black outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100 disabled:bg-slate-100"
  621:         />
  622: 
  623:         <div className="mt-5">
  624:           <SimulatorControls
  625:             canCheck={canCheck}
  626:             checked={checked}
  627:             hintVisible={hintVisible}
  628:             workingVisible={workingVisible}
  629:             resetLabel="Try again"
  630:             newExampleLabel="New question"
  631:             onCheck={handleCheck}
  632:             onHint={toggleHint}
  633:             onToggleWorking={toggleWorking}
  634:             onReset={handleTryAgain}
  635:             onNewExample={handleNewQuestion}
  636:           />
  637:         </div>
  638:       </section>
  639: 
  640:       <SimulatorFeedback
  641:         checked={checked}
  642:         correct={correct}
  643:         successMessage={`Correct. The sorted list is ${sortedValues.join(", ")}.`}
  644:         errorMessage={`Not quite. The correct sorted order is ${sortedValues.join(", ")}.`}
  645:         hintVisible={hintVisible}
  646:         hint="Bubble sort compares adjacent values. If the value on the left is larger, swap the pair."
  647:         workingVisible={workingVisible}
  648:         working={working}
  649:         examinerTip="Bubble sort compares adjacent items, swaps incorrectly ordered pairs and repeats passes until a complete pass produces no swaps."
  650:       />
  651: 
  652:       {/* ---------------------------------------------------- */}
  653:       {/* Hands-on procedural trainer                           */}
  654:       {/* ---------------------------------------------------- */}
  655: 
  656:       <div className="border-t border-slate-200 pt-8">
  657:         <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-violet-600">
  658:           Hands-on algorithm practice
  659:         </p>
  660: 
  661:         <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">Perform Bubble Sort</h3>
  662: 
  663:         <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  664:           Follow the actual Bubble Sort procedure. Make each comparison and
  665:           decide whether the adjacent values should be kept or swapped.
  666:         </p>
  667: 
  668:         {/* Procedural stats */}
  669: 
  670:         <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  671:           <article className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-5">
  672:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
  673:               Correct steps
  674:             </p>
  675: 
  676:             <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{procedureCorrectSteps}</p>
  677:           </article>
  678: 
  679:           <article className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-5">
  680:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
  681:               Mistakes
  682:             </p>
  683: 
  684:             <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{procedureMistakes}</p>
  685:           </article>
  686: 
  687:           <article className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-5">
  688:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
  689:               Procedure accuracy
  690:             </p>
  691: 
  692:             <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{procedureAccuracy}%</p>
  693:           </article>
  694: 
  695:           <article className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-5">
  696:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
  697:               Passes completed
  698:             </p>
  699: 
  700:             <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{passesCompleted}</p>
  701:           </article>
  702:         </div>
  703: 
  704:         {/* Current procedure status */}
  705: 
  706:         <section className="mt-6 rounded-3xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 p-6">
  707:           <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  708:             <div>
  709:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-violet-600">
  710:                 Current pass
  711:               </p>
  712: 
  713:               <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">
  714:                 {procedureComplete ? "Complete" : pass}
  715:               </p>
  716:             </div>
  717: 
  718:             <div>
  719:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-violet-600">
  720:                 Current comparison
  721:               </p>
  722: 
  723:               <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">
  724:                 {procedureComplete
  725:                   ? "Finished"
  726:                   : awaitingEndPass
  727:                     ? "End of pass"
  728:                     : `${pairLeft} and ${pairRight}`}
  729:               </p>
  730:             </div>
  731: 
  732:             <div>
  733:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-violet-600">
  734:                 Difficulty behaviour
  735:               </p>
  736: 
  737:               <p className="mt-2 font-black">
  738:                 {difficulty === "foundation"
  739:                   ? "Pair highlighted for you"
  740:                   : difficulty === "intermediate"
  741:                     ? "Select the correct adjacent pair"
  742:                     : "Select pair and recognise pass completion"}
  743:               </p>
  744:             </div>
  745:           </div>
  746:         </section>
  747: 
  748:         {/* Values */}
  749: 
  750:         <div className="mt-6 rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 p-7">
  751:           <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-4">
  752:             {displayedProcedureValues.map((value, itemIndex) => {
  753:               const selected = highlightedIndices.includes(itemIndex);
  754: 
  755:               const fixed =
  756:                 itemIndex > displayedProcedureValues.length - pass - 1;
  757: 
  758:               return (
  759:                 <button
  760:                   key={`${value}-${itemIndex}`}
  761:                   type="button"
  762:                   disabled={
  763:                     difficulty === "foundation" ||
  764:                     procedureComplete ||
  765:                     awaitingEndPass
  766:                   }
  767:                   onClick={() => selectProcedureValue(itemIndex)}
  768:                   className={`relative flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border-2 text-2xl font-black transition ${
  769:                     selected
  770:                       ? "border-blue-300 bg-blue-600 text-white"
  771:                       : fixed
  772:                         ? "border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500 text-white"
  773:                         : "border-slate-600 bg-slate-800 text-white hover:border-blue-400"
  774:                   }`}
  775:                 >
  776:                   <span>{value}</span>
  777: 
  778:                   <span className="absolute -bottom-6 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400">
  779:                     {itemIndex}
  780:                   </span>
  781:                 </button>
  782:               );
  783:             })}
  784:           </div>
  785: 
  786:           <div className="mt-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-300">
  787:             {procedureComplete ? (
  788:               <p className="font-black text-emerald-300">Sorting complete.</p>
  789:             ) : awaitingEndPass ? (
  790:               <p className="font-black text-amber-300">
  791:                 You have reached the end of this pass.
  792:               </p>
  793:             ) : difficulty === "foundation" ? (
  794:               <p>
  795:                 The current adjacent pair is highlighted. Decide whether to keep
  796:                 or swap it.
  797:               </p>
  798:             ) : selectedIndices.length === 0 ? (
  799:               <p>
  800:                 Select the next two adjacent values that Bubble Sort should
  801:                 compare.
  802:               </p>
  803:             ) : selectedIndices.length === 1 ? (
  804:               <p>Select the second adjacent value.</p>
  805:             ) : pairReady ? (
  806:               <p>Correct pair selected. Decide whether to Keep or Swap.</p>
  807:             ) : (
  808:               <p>
  809:                 Decide whether this is the correct pair for the next comparison.
  810:               </p>
  811:             )}
  812:           </div>
  813:         </div>
  814: 
  815:         {/* Actions */}
  816: 
  817:         <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  818:           {!awaitingEndPass && !procedureComplete && (
  819:             <>
  820:               <button
  821:                 type="button"
  822:                 onClick={handleKeep}
  823:                 className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50 px-6 py-3 font-black text-emerald-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-100"
  824:               >
  825:                 Keep order
  826:               </button>
  827: 
  828:               <button
  829:                 type="button"
  830:                 onClick={handleSwap}
  831:                 className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 font-black text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700"
  832:               >
  833:                 Swap
  834:               </button>
  835:             </>
  836:           )}
  837: 
  838:           {difficulty === "higher" && awaitingEndPass && !procedureComplete && (
  839:             <button
  840:               type="button"
  841:               onClick={handleEndPass}
  842:               className="rounded-xl bg-violet-600 px-6 py-3 font-black text-white transition hover:bg-violet-700"
  843:             >
  844:               End pass â†’
  845:             </button>
  846:           )}
  847: 
  848:           <button
  849:             type="button"
  850:             onClick={() => resetProcedure()}
  851:             className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-6 py-3 font-black text-slate-800"
  852:           >
  853:             Reset procedure
  854:           </button>
  855:         </div>
  856: 
  857:         {/* Procedure feedback */}
  858: 
  859:         {procedureFeedback && (
  860:           <section
  861:             className={`mt-6 rounded-2xl border p-5 ${
  862:               procedureFeedbackType === "success"
  863:                 ? "border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900"
  864:                 : procedureFeedbackType === "error"
  865:                   ? "border-red-300 bg-red-50 text-red-900"
  866:                   : "border-blue-300 bg-blue-50 text-blue-900"
  867:             }`}
  868:           >
  869:             <p className="font-black">
  870:               {procedureFeedbackType === "success"
  871:                 ? "âœ“ Correct procedure"
  872:                 : procedureFeedbackType === "error"
  873:                   ? "âœ• Try that step again"
  874:                   : "Procedure update"}
  875:             </p>
  876: 
  877:             <p className="mt-2 leading-7">{procedureFeedback}</p>
  878:           </section>
  879:         )}
  880: 
  881:         {procedureComplete && (
  882:           <section className="mt-6 rounded-3xl border border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50 p-6">
  883:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-700">
  884:               Procedure complete
  885:             </p>
  886: 
  887:             <h4 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-emerald-950">
  888:               Bubble Sort completed successfully
  889:             </h4>
  890: 
  891:             <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
  892:               <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4">
  893:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase text-slate-500">
  894:                   Final list
  895:                 </p>
  896: 
  897:                 <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-lg font-black">
  898:                   {displayedProcedureValues.join(", ")}
  899:                 </p>
  900:               </div>
  901: 
  902:               <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4">
  903:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase text-slate-500">
  904:                   Accuracy
  905:                 </p>
  906: 
  907:                 <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{procedureAccuracy}%</p>
  908:               </div>
  909: 
  910:               <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4">
  911:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase text-slate-500">
  912:                   Mistakes
  913:                 </p>
  914: 
  915:                 <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">{procedureMistakes}</p>
  916:               </div>
  917:             </div>
  918:           </section>
  919:         )}
  920:       </div>
  921:     </section>
  922:   );
  923: }
