FILE: components\Simulators\CaesarCipherSimulator.tsx
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    3: 
    4: import SimulatorControls from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorControls";
    5: import SimulatorDifficulty from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorDifficulty";
    6: import SimulatorFeedback from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorFeedback";
    7: import SimulatorStats from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorStats";
    8: import {
    9:   useSimulator,
   10:   type SimulatorDifficulty as Difficulty,
   11: } from "@/components/Simulators/common/useSimulator";
   12: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   13: import { useProgress } from "@/contexts/ProgressContext";
   14: 
   15: type Q = {
   16:   plain: string;
   17:   shift: number;
   18:   answer: string;
   19:   hint: string;
   20:   working: string;
   21: };
   22: const enc = (t: string, k: number) =>
   23:   t
   24:     .toUpperCase()
   25:     .replace(/[A-Z]/g, (c) =>
   26:       String.fromCharCode(65 + ((c.charCodeAt(0) - 65 + k) % 26)),
   27:     );
   28: const words = {
   29:   foundation: ["CAT", "DATA", "CODE"],
   30:   intermediate: ["BINARY", "NETWORK", "PYTHON"],
   31:   higher: ["ALGORITHM", "ENCRYPTION", "COMPUTER"],
   32: };
   33: function make(d: Difficulty): Q {
   34:   const w = words[d][Math.floor(Math.random() * words[d].length)],
   35:     shift =
   36:       d === "foundation"
   37:         ? 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)
   38:         : 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 12);
   39:   return {
   40:     plain: w,
   41:     shift,
   42:     answer: enc(w, shift),
   43:     hint: `Move every letter ${shift} places forward through the alphabet.`,
   44:     working: `${w} â†’ ${enc(w, shift)} using a Caesar shift of ${shift}.`,
   45:   };
   46: }
   47: export default function CaesarCipherSimulator() {
   48:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
   49:   const gen = useCallback((d: Difficulty) => make(d), []);
   50:   const s = useSimulator<Q>({
   51:     initialQuestion: make("foundation"),
   52:     generateQuestion: gen,
   53:     onAwardXP: addXP,
   54:   });
   55:   const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
   56:   const [lab, setLab] = useState("HELLO");
   57:   const [shift, setShift] = useState(3);
   58:   const reset = () => {
   59:       setAnswer("");
   60:       s.resetQuestion();
   61:     },
   62:     next = () => {
   63:       setAnswer("");
   64:       s.newQuestion();
   65:     };
   66:   return (
   67:     <Card>
   68:       <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
   69:         Interactive challenge + explorer
   70:       </p>
   71:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">Caesar Cipher</h2>
   72:       <p className="mt-3 text-slate-600">
   73:         Encrypt text by shifting letters through the alphabet.
   74:       </p>
   75:       <div className="mt-6">
   76:         <SimulatorDifficulty
   77:           value={s.difficulty}
   78:           onChange={(d) => {
   79:             setAnswer("");
   80:             s.changeDifficulty(d);
   81:           }}
   82:         />
   83:       </div>
   84:       <div className="mt-6">
   85:         <SimulatorStats
   86:           attempts={s.attempts}
   87:           correct={s.correctAnswers}
   88:           accuracy={s.accuracy}
   89:           xp={s.xp}
   90:           streak={s.streak}
   91:         />
   92:       </div>
   93:       <section className="mt-6 rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 p-6 text-white">
   94:         <p>
   95:           Encrypt <b>{s.question.plain}</b> using shift{" "}
   96:           <b>{s.question.shift}</b>.
   97:         </p>
   98:       </section>
   99:       <input
  100:         value={answer}
  101:         onChange={(e) => setAnswer(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
  102:         className="mt-4 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 font-mono"
  103:         placeholder="Ciphertext"
  104:       />
  105:       <div className="mt-5">
  106:         <SimulatorControls
  107:           canCheck={!!answer}
  108:           checked={s.checked}
  109:           hintVisible={s.hintVisible}
  110:           workingVisible={s.workingVisible}
  111:           onCheck={() => s.markAnswer(answer.trim() === s.question.answer)}
  112:           onHint={s.toggleHint}
  113:           onToggleWorking={s.toggleWorking}
  114:           onReset={reset}
  115:           onNewExample={next}
  116:         />
  117:       </div>
  118:       <div className="mt-5">
  119:         <SimulatorFeedback
  120:           checked={s.checked}
  121:           correct={s.correct}
  122:           hintVisible={s.hintVisible}
  123:           hint={s.question.hint}
  124:           workingVisible={s.workingVisible}
  125:           working={s.question.working}
  126:           examinerTip="A Caesar cipher is useful for learning substitution, but it is not secure modern encryption."
  127:         />
  128:       </div>
  129:       <section className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border p-6">
  130:         <h3 className="text-xl font-black">Explore mode</h3>
  131:         <input
  132:           value={lab}
  133:           onChange={(e) => setLab(e.target.value)}
  134:           className="mt-4 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  135:         />
  136:         <label className="mt-4 block font-bold">Shift: {shift}</label>
  137:         <input
  138:           type="range"
  139:           min="1"
  140:           max="25"
  141:           value={shift}
  142:           onChange={(e) => setShift(Number(e.target.value))}
  143:           className="w-full"
  144:         />
  145:         <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-4 font-mono text-emerald-300">
  146:           {enc(lab, shift)}
  147:         </div>
  148:       </section>
  149:     </Card>
  150:   );
  151: }

====================================================================================================
FILE: components\Simulators\common\useSimulator.tsx
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: export type SimulatorDifficulty = "foundation" | "intermediate" | "higher";
    6: 
    7: export type SimulatorQuestionResult = {
    8:   questionId: string;
    9:   difficulty: SimulatorDifficulty;
   10: 
   11:   firstAttemptCorrect: boolean;
   12:   eventuallyCorrect: boolean;
   13: 
   14:   answerChecks: number;
   15: 
   16:   xpAwarded: number;
   17: 
   18:   hintUsed: boolean;
   19:   workingViewed: boolean;
   20: };
   21: 
   22: type Options<TQuestion> = {
   23:   initialQuestion: TQuestion;
   24: 
   25:   generateQuestion: (difficulty: SimulatorDifficulty) => TQuestion;
   26: 
   27:   xpByDifficulty?: Partial<Record<SimulatorDifficulty, number>>;
   28: 
   29:   onAwardXP?: (amount: number) => void | Promise<void>;
   30: 
   31:   /*
   32:    * Optional future-facing values.
   33:    *
   34:    * Existing simulators DO NOT need to provide these.
   35:    * They are here so the adaptive-practice layer can later
   36:    * identify simulator/question sources cleanly.
   37:    */
   38:   simulatorId?: string;
   39: 
   40:   getQuestionId?: (
   41:     question: TQuestion,
   42:     difficulty: SimulatorDifficulty,
   43:   ) => string;
   44: };
   45: 
   46: function createSessionQuestionId(
   47:   simulatorId: string,
   48:   sequence: number,
   49: ): string {
   50:   return `${simulatorId}-${sequence}`;
   51: }
   52: 
   53: export function useSimulator<TQuestion>({
   54:   initialQuestion,
   55:   generateQuestion,
   56: 
   57:   xpByDifficulty = {
   58:     foundation: 10,
   59:     intermediate: 15,
   60:     higher: 20,
   61:   },
   62: 
   63:   onAwardXP,
   64: 
   65:   simulatorId = "simulator",
   66: 
   67:   getQuestionId,
   68: }: Options<TQuestion>) {
   69:   /*
   70:    * =========================================================
   71:    * QUESTION / DIFFICULTY
   72:    * =========================================================
   73:    */
   74: 
   75:   const [difficulty, setDifficulty] =
   76:     useState<SimulatorDifficulty>("foundation");
   77: 
   78:   const [question, setQuestionState] = useState<TQuestion>(initialQuestion);
   79: 
   80:   /*
   81:    * Every genuinely new generated question receives
   82:    * a new session sequence number.
   83:    *
   84:    * Reset/Try Again does NOT change it.
   85:    */
   86:   const questionSequenceRef = useRef(1);
   87: 
   88:   const initialQuestionId =
   89:     getQuestionId?.(initialQuestion, "foundation") ??
   90:     createSessionQuestionId(simulatorId, questionSequenceRef.current);
   91: 
   92:   const [questionId, setQuestionId] = useState(initialQuestionId);
   93: 
   94:   /*
   95:    * =========================================================
   96:    * CURRENT RESPONSE STATE
   97:    * =========================================================
   98:    */
   99: 
  100:   const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);
  101: 
  102:   const [correct, setCorrect] = useState(false);
  103: 
  104:   /*
  105:    * questionScored means:
  106:    *
  107:    * "Has this generated question already contributed
  108:    * to the Questions statistic?"
  109:    *
  110:    * It does NOT mean that a student has lost the right
  111:    * to correct their answer.
  112:    */
  113:   const [questionScored, setQuestionScored] = useState(false);
  114: 
  115:   /*
  116:    * A question may only contribute to Correct once.
  117:    */
  118:   const [questionCorrectRecorded, setQuestionCorrectRecorded] = useState(false);
  119: 
  120:   /*
  121:    * XP can only be awarded once per generated question.
  122:    */
  123:   const [questionRewarded, setQuestionRewarded] = useState(false);
  124: 
  125:   /*
  126:    * Number of times Check Answer has actually been used
  127:    * on this generated question.
  128:    */
  129:   const [questionAnswerChecks, setQuestionAnswerChecks] = useState(0);
  130: 
  131:   /*
  132:    * Records whether the very first submitted answer
  133:    * was correct.
  134:    *
  135:    * This is useful later for adaptive mastery.
  136:    */
  137:   const [firstAttemptCorrect, setFirstAttemptCorrect] = useState<
  138:     boolean | null
  139:   >(null);
  140: 
  141:   /*
  142:    * =========================================================
  143:    * SUPPORT STATE
  144:    * =========================================================
  145:    */
  146: 
  147:   const [hintVisible, setHintVisible] = useState(false);
  148: 
  149:   const [workingVisible, setWorkingVisible] = useState(false);
  150: 
  151:   /*
  152:    * Session-level support analytics.
  153:    */
  154:   const [hintsUsed, setHintsUsed] = useState(0);
  155: 
  156:   const [workingViewed, setWorkingViewed] = useState(0);
  157: 
  158:   /*
  159:    * Question-level support analytics.
  160:    *
  161:    * Opening and closing a hint repeatedly only counts once
  162:    * for that generated question.
  163:    */
  164:   const [currentQuestionHintUsed, setCurrentQuestionHintUsed] = useState(false);
  165: 
  166:   const [currentQuestionWorkingViewed, setCurrentQuestionWorkingViewed] =
  167:     useState(false);
  168: 
  169:   /*
  170:    * =========================================================
  171:    * SESSION SCORE STATE
  172:    * =========================================================
  173:    */
  174: 
  175:   /*
  176:    * attempts = number of unique generated questions
  177:    * that have received at least one submitted answer.
  178:    *
  179:    * It is intentionally NOT number of button presses.
  180:    */
  181:   const [attempts, setAttempts] = useState(0);
  182: 
  183:   /*
  184:    * A question becomes correct if the learner eventually
  185:    * solves it correctly.
  186:    *
  187:    * This allows:
  188:    *
  189:    * wrong â†’ Try Again â†’ correct
  190:    *
  191:    * without creating a second Question entry.
  192:    */
  193:   const [correctAnswers, setCorrectAnswers] = useState(0);
  194: 
  195:   const [xp, setXp] = useState(0);
  196: 
  197:   const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0);
  198: 
  199:   const [highestStreak, setHighestStreak] = useState(0);
  200: 
  201:   /*
  202:    * =========================================================
  203:    * HISTORY FOR FUTURE ADAPTIVE PRACTICE
  204:    * =========================================================
  205:    */
  206: 
  207:   const [questionResults, setQuestionResults] = useState<
  208:     SimulatorQuestionResult[]
  209:   >([]);
  210: 
  211:   /*
  212:    * =========================================================
  213:    * DERIVED ANALYTICS
  214:    * =========================================================
  215:    */
  216: 
  217:   const accuracy = useMemo(() => {
  218:     if (attempts === 0) {
  219:       return 0;
  220:     }
  221: 
  222:     return Math.round((correctAnswers / attempts) * 100);
  223:   }, [attempts, correctAnswers]);
  224: 
  225:   const currentXPReward = xpByDifficulty[difficulty] ?? 10;
  226: 
  227:   /*
  228:    * =========================================================
  229:    * RESPONSE RESET
  230:    * =========================================================
  231:    */
  232: 
  233:   const clearResponseState = useCallback(() => {
  234:     setChecked(false);
  235:     setCorrect(false);
  236: 
  237:     setHintVisible(false);
  238:     setWorkingVisible(false);
  239:   }, []);
  240: 
  241:   /*
  242:    * =========================================================
  243:    * QUESTION HISTORY
  244:    * =========================================================
  245:    */
  246: 
  247:   const saveCurrentQuestionResult = useCallback(
  248:     (eventuallyCorrect: boolean, awardedXP: number, answerChecks: number) => {
  249:       /*
  250:        * Replace an existing result for the current question
  251:        * rather than creating duplicate history entries.
  252:        */
  253:       setQuestionResults((current) => {
  254:         const result: SimulatorQuestionResult = {
  255:           questionId,
  256:           difficulty,
  257: 
  258:           firstAttemptCorrect: firstAttemptCorrect ?? false,
  259: 
  260:           eventuallyCorrect,
  261: 
  262:           answerChecks,
  263: 
  264:           xpAwarded: awardedXP,
  265: 
  266:           hintUsed: currentQuestionHintUsed,
  267: 
  268:           workingViewed: currentQuestionWorkingViewed,
  269:         };
  270: 
  271:         const existingIndex = current.findIndex(
  272:           (item) => item.questionId === questionId,
  273:         );
  274: 
  275:         if (existingIndex === -1) {
  276:           return [...current, result];
  277:         }
  278: 
  279:         const updated = [...current];
  280: 
  281:         updated[existingIndex] = {
  282:           ...updated[existingIndex],
  283:           ...result,
  284: 
  285:           /*
  286:            * Never erase previously awarded XP.
  287:            */
  288:           xpAwarded: Math.max(
  289:             updated[existingIndex].xpAwarded,
  290:             result.xpAwarded,
  291:           ),
  292: 
  293:           /*
  294:            * Once true, these remain true.
  295:            */
  296:           eventuallyCorrect:
  297:             updated[existingIndex].eventuallyCorrect ||
  298:             result.eventuallyCorrect,
  299: 
  300:           hintUsed: updated[existingIndex].hintUsed || result.hintUsed,
  301: 
  302:           workingViewed:
  303:             updated[existingIndex].workingViewed || result.workingViewed,
  304: 
  305:           answerChecks: Math.max(
  306:             updated[existingIndex].answerChecks,
  307:             result.answerChecks,
  308:           ),
  309:         };
  310: 
  311:         return updated;
  312:       });
  313:     },
  314:     [
  315:       questionId,
  316:       difficulty,
  317:       firstAttemptCorrect,
  318:       currentQuestionHintUsed,
  319:       currentQuestionWorkingViewed,
  320:     ],
  321:   );
  322: 
  323:   /*
  324:    * =========================================================
  325:    * START NEW QUESTION
  326:    * =========================================================
  327:    */
  328: 
  329:   const beginNewQuestion = useCallback(
  330:     (nextQuestion: TQuestion, nextDifficulty: SimulatorDifficulty) => {
  331:       questionSequenceRef.current += 1;
  332: 
  333:       const nextQuestionId =
  334:         getQuestionId?.(nextQuestion, nextDifficulty) ??
  335:         createSessionQuestionId(simulatorId, questionSequenceRef.current);
  336: 
  337:       setQuestionState(nextQuestion);
  338: 
  339:       setQuestionId(nextQuestionId);
  340: 
  341:       /*
  342:        * Reset all protections because this is a genuinely
  343:        * new generated question.
  344:        */
  345:       setQuestionScored(false);
  346: 
  347:       setQuestionCorrectRecorded(false);
  348: 
  349:       setQuestionRewarded(false);
  350: 
  351:       setQuestionAnswerChecks(0);
  352: 
  353:       setFirstAttemptCorrect(null);
  354: 
  355:       setCurrentQuestionHintUsed(false);
  356: 
  357:       setCurrentQuestionWorkingViewed(false);
  358: 
  359:       clearResponseState();
  360:     },
  361:     [clearResponseState, getQuestionId, simulatorId],
  362:   );
  363: 
  364:   /*
  365:    * =========================================================
  366:    * MARK ANSWER
  367:    * =========================================================
  368:    */
  369: 
  370:   const markAnswer = useCallback(
  371:     (isCorrect: boolean) => {
  372:       /*
  373:        * A visible checked response must be cleared with
  374:        * Try Again before another answer can be checked.
  375:        */
  376:       if (checked) {
  377:         return;
  378:       }
  379: 
  380:       const nextAnswerChecks = questionAnswerChecks + 1;
  381: 
  382:       setQuestionAnswerChecks(nextAnswerChecks);
  383: 
  384:       setChecked(true);
  385: 
  386:       setCorrect(isCorrect);
  387: 
  388:       /*
  389:        * Record first-attempt performance once only.
  390:        */
  391:       if (firstAttemptCorrect === null) {
  392:         setFirstAttemptCorrect(isCorrect);
  393:       }
  394: 
  395:       /*
  396:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  397:        * QUESTIONS
  398:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  399:        *
  400:        * The generated question contributes exactly once to
  401:        * Questions, regardless of retries.
  402:        */
  403:       if (!questionScored) {
  404:         setQuestionScored(true);
  405: 
  406:         setAttempts((current) => current + 1);
  407: 
  408:         /*
  409:          * A first-attempt mistake breaks the current streak.
  410:          *
  411:          * A repeated mistake on the same question should not
  412:          * repeatedly reset or otherwise manipulate analytics.
  413:          */
  414:         if (!isCorrect) {
  415:           setStreak(0);
  416:         }
  417:       }
  418: 
  419:       /*
  420:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  421:        * WRONG ANSWER
  422:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  423:        */
  424: 
  425:       if (!isCorrect) {
  426:         saveCurrentQuestionResult(false, 0, nextAnswerChecks);
  427: 
  428:         return;
  429:       }
  430: 
  431:       /*
  432:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  433:        * EVENTUAL CORRECTNESS
  434:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  435:        *
  436:        * A learner who gets the question wrong first and then
  437:        * succeeds after Try Again should still receive credit
  438:        * for eventually mastering that question.
  439:        *
  440:        * Correct can only increase once for this question.
  441:        */
  442:       if (!questionCorrectRecorded) {
  443:         setQuestionCorrectRecorded(true);
  444: 
  445:         setCorrectAnswers((current) => current + 1);
  446: 
  447:         setStreak((current) => {
  448:           const next = current + 1;
  449: 
  450:           setHighestStreak((highest) => Math.max(highest, next));
  451: 
  452:           return next;
  453:         });
  454:       }
  455: 
  456:       /*
  457:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  458:        * XP
  459:        * -----------------------------------------------------
  460:        *
  461:        * XP can only ever be awarded once for a generated
  462:        * question.
  463:        */
  464:       let awardedXP = 0;
  465: 
  466:       if (!questionRewarded) {
  467:         awardedXP = currentXPReward;
  468: 
  469:         setQuestionRewarded(true);
  470: 
  471:         setXp((current) => current + awardedXP);
  472: 
  473:         /*
  474:          * Update real CS Master XP once only.
  475:          */
  476:         if (onAwardXP) {
  477:           void onAwardXP(awardedXP);
  478:         }
  479:       }
  480: 
  481:       saveCurrentQuestionResult(true, awardedXP, nextAnswerChecks);
  482:     },
  483:     [
  484:       checked,
  485:       questionAnswerChecks,
  486:       firstAttemptCorrect,
  487:       questionScored,
  488:       questionCorrectRecorded,
  489:       questionRewarded,
  490:       currentXPReward,
  491:       onAwardXP,
  492:       saveCurrentQuestionResult,
  493:     ],
  494:   );
  495: 
  496:   /*
  497:    * =========================================================
  498:    * TRY AGAIN / RESET QUESTION
  499:    * =========================================================
  500:    *
  501:    * SAME generated question.
  502:    *
  503:    * Important:
  504:    *
  505:    * - Questions does not increase again.
  506:    * - Correct cannot increase more than once.
  507:    * - XP cannot be awarded more than once.
  508:    * - The learner CAN still earn XP after initially getting
  509:    *   the question wrong.
  510:    */
  511:   const resetQuestion = useCallback(() => {
  512:     clearResponseState();
  513:   }, [clearResponseState]);
  514: 
  515:   /*
  516:    * =========================================================
  517:    * NEW QUESTION
  518:    * =========================================================
  519:    */
  520: 
  521:   const newQuestion = useCallback(() => {
  522:     const nextQuestion = generateQuestion(difficulty);
  523: 
  524:     beginNewQuestion(nextQuestion, difficulty);
  525:   }, [difficulty, generateQuestion, beginNewQuestion]);
  526: 
  527:   /*
  528:    * =========================================================
  529:    * DIFFICULTY CHANGE
  530:    * =========================================================
  531:    */
  532: 
  533:   const changeDifficulty = useCallback(
  534:     (nextDifficulty: SimulatorDifficulty) => {
  535:       setDifficulty(nextDifficulty);
  536: 
  537:       const nextQuestion = generateQuestion(nextDifficulty);
  538: 
  539:       beginNewQuestion(nextQuestion, nextDifficulty);
  540:     },
  541:     [generateQuestion, beginNewQuestion],
  542:   );
  543: 
  544:   /*
  545:    * =========================================================
  546:    * HINT
  547:    * =========================================================
  548:    */
  549: 
  550:   const toggleHint = useCallback(() => {
  551:     setHintVisible((current) => {
  552:       const next = !current;
  553: 
  554:       /*
  555:        * Count actual use only when opening the hint.
  556:        */
  557:       if (next && !currentQuestionHintUsed) {
  558:         setCurrentQuestionHintUsed(true);
  559: 
  560:         setHintsUsed((value) => value + 1);
  561:       }
  562: 
  563:       return next;
  564:     });
  565:   }, [currentQuestionHintUsed]);
  566: 
  567:   /*
  568:    * =========================================================
  569:    * SHOW WORKING
  570:    * =========================================================
  571:    */
  572: 
  573:   const toggleWorking = useCallback(() => {
  574:     setWorkingVisible((current) => {
  575:       const next = !current;
  576: 
  577:       if (next && !currentQuestionWorkingViewed) {
  578:         setCurrentQuestionWorkingViewed(true);
  579: 
  580:         setWorkingViewed((value) => value + 1);
  581:       }
  582: 
  583:       return next;
  584:     });
  585:   }, [currentQuestionWorkingViewed]);
  586: 
  587:   /*
  588:    * =========================================================
  589:    * MANUAL QUESTION SETTER
  590:    * =========================================================
  591:    *
  592:    * Keep compatibility with existing simulators that use
  593:    * setQuestion directly.
  594:    *
  595:    * This setter intentionally ONLY changes the question value.
  596:    * Existing simulator behaviour relying on setQuestion should
  597:    * therefore continue to work.
  598:    */
  599:   const setQuestion = useCallback(
  600:     (value: TQuestion | ((current: TQuestion) => TQuestion)) => {
  601:       setQuestionState((current) =>
  602:         typeof value === "function"
  603:           ? (value as (current: TQuestion) => TQuestion)(current)
  604:           : value,
  605:       );
  606:     },
  607:     [],
  608:   );
  609: 
  610:   /*
  611:    * =========================================================
  612:    * SESSION RESET
  613:    * =========================================================
  614:    *
  615:    * We probably will not expose this in ordinary lessons,
  616:    * but it is useful for tests and future standalone simulator
  617:    * sessions.
  618:    */
  619:   const resetSession = useCallback(() => {
  620:     setAttempts(0);
  621: 
  622:     setCorrectAnswers(0);
  623: 
  624:     setXp(0);
  625: 
  626:     setStreak(0);
  627: 
  628:     setHighestStreak(0);
  629: 
  630:     setHintsUsed(0);
  631: 
  632:     setWorkingViewed(0);
  633: 
  634:     setQuestionResults([]);
  635: 
  636:     setQuestionScored(false);
  637: 
  638:     setQuestionCorrectRecorded(false);
  639: 
  640:     setQuestionRewarded(false);
  641: 
  642:     setQuestionAnswerChecks(0);
  643: 
  644:     setFirstAttemptCorrect(null);
  645: 
  646:     setCurrentQuestionHintUsed(false);
  647: 
  648:     setCurrentQuestionWorkingViewed(false);
  649: 
  650:     clearResponseState();
  651:   }, [clearResponseState]);
  652: 
  653:   /*
  654:    * =========================================================
  655:    * PUBLIC API
  656:    * =========================================================
  657:    *
  658:    * All existing properties are retained so current simulators
  659:    * should remain compatible.
  660:    *
  661:    * New analytics are added underneath them.
  662:    */
  663:   return {
  664:     /*
  665:      * Existing API
  666:      */
  667:     difficulty,
  668: 
  669:     question,
  670: 
  671:     checked,
  672: 
  673:     correct,
  674: 
  675:     questionScored,
  676: 
  677:     hintVisible,
  678: 
  679:     workingVisible,
  680: 
  681:     attempts,
  682: 
  683:     correctAnswers,
  684: 
  685:     accuracy,
  686: 
  687:     xp,
  688: 
  689:     streak,
  690: 
  691:     highestStreak,
  692: 
  693:     markAnswer,
  694: 
  695:     resetQuestion,
  696: 
  697:     newQuestion,
  698: 
  699:     changeDifficulty,
  700: 
  701:     toggleHint,
  702: 
  703:     toggleWorking,
  704: 
  705:     setQuestion,
  706: 
  707:     /*
  708:      * New shared-session API
  709:      */
  710:     questionId,
  711: 
  712:     questionRewarded,
  713: 
  714:     questionCorrectRecorded,
  715: 
  716:     questionAnswerChecks,
  717: 
  718:     firstAttemptCorrect,
  719: 
  720:     hintsUsed,
  721: 
  722:     workingViewed,
  723: 
  724:     currentQuestionHintUsed,
  725: 
  726:     currentQuestionWorkingViewed,
  727: 
  728:     questionResults,
  729: 
  730:     currentXPReward,
  731: 
  732:     resetSession,
  733:   };
  734: }

FILE: components\Simulators\CyberSecurityScenarioSimulator.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import SimulatorControls from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorControls";
    6: import SimulatorDifficulty from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorDifficulty";
    7: import SimulatorFeedback from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorFeedback";
    8: import SimulatorStats from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorStats";
    9: import {
   10:   useSimulator,
   11:   type SimulatorDifficulty as Difficulty,
   12: } from "@/components/Simulators/common/useSimulator";
   13: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   14: import { useProgress } from "@/contexts/ProgressContext";
   15: 
   16: type SecurityChoice = "safe" | "unsafe";
   17: 
   18: type Question = {
   19:   prompt: string;
   20:   safe: string;
   21:   unsafe: string;
   22:   hint: string;
   23:   working: string;
   24: };
   25: 
   26: type ChoiceOption = readonly [SecurityChoice, string];
   27: 
   28: const BANK: Record<Difficulty, Question[]> = {
   29:   foundation: [
   30:     {
   31:       prompt: "An email says your account will close unless you click a link.",
   32:       safe: "Open the official website yourself and report the email.",
   33:       unsafe: "Click the link and sign in immediately.",
   34:       hint: "Urgency is a common social-engineering technique.",
   35:       working:
   36:         "Do not trust the supplied link. Verify through a known official route and report the message.",
   37:     },
   38:     {
   39:       prompt: "You find an unknown USB drive in school.",
   40:       safe: "Give it to authorised IT staff without connecting it.",
   41:       unsafe: "Plug it in to find the owner.",
   42:       hint: "Unknown removable media may contain malware.",
   43:       working:
   44:         "Do not connect unknown media to a computer. Hand it to authorised staff.",
   45:     },
   46:   ],
   47: 
   48:   intermediate: [
   49:     {
   50:       prompt:
   51:         "A caller claiming to be IT asks for your one-time authentication code.",
   52:       safe: "Refuse and verify the request independently.",
   53:       unsafe: "Share it because the caller knows your name.",
   54:       hint: "Authentication secrets should not be disclosed.",
   55:       working:
   56:         "Names can be researched. End the call and contact IT using a trusted method.",
   57:     },
   58:     {
   59:       prompt: "A website certificate warning appears before a login page.",
   60:       safe: "Stop and verify the site and connection.",
   61:       unsafe: "Ignore the warning and enter credentials.",
   62:       hint: "Certificate warnings can indicate an unsafe connection.",
   63:       working:
   64:         "Do not submit credentials until the site and certificate problem have been verified.",
   65:     },
   66:   ],
   67: 
   68:   higher: [
   69:     {
   70:       prompt:
   71:         "A finance employee receives a realistic message from a senior manager requesting an urgent bank-detail change.",
   72:       safe: "Verify the request using a separate trusted channel before changing anything.",
   73:       unsafe:
   74:         "Process it because the sender name and writing style look correct.",
   75:       hint: "Consider impersonation and business-email compromise.",
   76:       working:
   77:         "Identity indicators can be spoofed. Sensitive changes need independent verification.",
   78:     },
   79:     {
   80:       prompt: "A user reports repeated MFA prompts they did not initiate.",
   81:       safe: "Reject the prompts, change credentials and report the incident.",
   82:       unsafe: "Approve one prompt to make the notifications stop.",
   83:       hint: "Think about MFA fatigue attacks.",
   84:       working:
   85:         "Unexpected prompts may mean stolen credentials. Never approve them; secure and report the account.",
   86:     },
   87:   ],
   88: };
   89: 
   90: function pickQuestion(difficulty: Difficulty): Question {
   91:   const questions = BANK[difficulty];
   92: 
   93:   return questions[Math.floor(Math.random() * questions.length)];
   94: }
   95: 
   96: export default function CyberSecurityScenarioSimulator() {
   97:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
   98: 
   99:   const generateQuestion = useCallback(
  100:     (difficulty: Difficulty) => pickQuestion(difficulty),
  101:     [],
  102:   );
  103: 
  104:   const simulator = useSimulator<Question>({
  105:     initialQuestion: pickQuestion("foundation"),
  106:     generateQuestion,
  107:     onAwardXP: addXP,
  108:   });
  109: 
  110:   const [choice, setChoice] = useState<SecurityChoice | null>(null);
  111: 
  112:   const options = useMemo<ChoiceOption[]>(() => {
  113:     const safe: ChoiceOption = ["safe", simulator.question.safe];
  114:     const unsafe: ChoiceOption = ["unsafe", simulator.question.unsafe];
  115: 
  116:     return Math.random() > 0.5 ? [safe, unsafe] : [unsafe, safe];
  117:   }, [simulator.question]);
  118: 
  119:   function checkAnswer() {
  120:     simulator.markAnswer(choice === "safe");
  121:   }
  122: 
  123:   function resetQuestion() {
  124:     setChoice(null);
  125:     simulator.resetQuestion();
  126:   }
  127: 
  128:   function newScenario() {
  129:     setChoice(null);
  130:     simulator.newQuestion();
  131:   }
  132: 
  133:   function changeDifficulty(difficulty: Difficulty) {
  134:     setChoice(null);
  135:     simulator.changeDifficulty(difficulty);
  136:   }
  137: 
  138:   return (
  139:     <Card>
  140:       <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  141:         Interactive challenge
  142:       </p>
  143: 
  144:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">
  145:         Cyber Security Decision Lab
  146:       </h2>
  147: 
  148:       <p className="mt-3 text-slate-600">
  149:         Identify the safest response and justify defensive behaviour.
  150:       </p>
  151: 
  152:       <div className="mt-6">
  153:         <SimulatorDifficulty
  154:           value={simulator.difficulty}
  155:           onChange={changeDifficulty}
  156:         />
  157:       </div>
  158: 
  159:       <div className="mt-6">
  160:         <SimulatorStats
  161:           attempts={simulator.attempts}
  162:           correct={simulator.correctAnswers}
  163:           accuracy={simulator.accuracy}
  164:           xp={simulator.xp}
  165:           streak={simulator.streak}
  166:         />
  167:       </div>
  168: 
  169:       <section className="mt-6 rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 p-6 text-white">
  170:         <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
  171:           Scenario
  172:         </p>
  173: 
  174:         <p className="mt-3 text-xl font-black leading-8">
  175:           {simulator.question.prompt}
  176:         </p>
  177:       </section>
  178: 
  179:       <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
  180:         {options.map(([value, text]) => (
  181:           <button
  182:             key={value}
  183:             type="button"
  184:             onClick={() => setChoice(value)}
  185:             disabled={simulator.checked}
  186:             className={`rounded-2xl border-2 p-5 text-left font-bold transition ${
  187:               choice === value
  188:                 ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50"
  189:                 : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-blue-300"
  190:             } disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70`}
  191:           >
  192:             {text}
  193:           </button>
  194:         ))}
  195:       </div>
  196: 
  197:       <div className="mt-5">
  198:         <SimulatorControls
  199:           canCheck={choice !== null}
  200:           checked={simulator.checked}
  201:           hintVisible={simulator.hintVisible}
  202:           workingVisible={simulator.workingVisible}
  203:           onCheck={checkAnswer}
  204:           onHint={simulator.toggleHint}
  205:           onToggleWorking={simulator.toggleWorking}
  206:           onReset={resetQuestion}
  207:           onNewExample={newScenario}
  208:           newExampleLabel="New scenario"
  209:         />
  210:       </div>
  211: 
  212:       <div className="mt-5">
  213:         <SimulatorFeedback
  214:           checked={simulator.checked}
  215:           correct={simulator.correct}
  216:           hintVisible={simulator.hintVisible}
  217:           hint={simulator.question.hint}
  218:           workingVisible={simulator.workingVisible}
  219:           working={simulator.question.working}
  220:           successMessage="Correct defensive decision."
  221:           errorMessage="That response creates unnecessary security risk."
  222:           examinerTip="In exam answers, name the threat and explain how the control reduces its likelihood or impact."
  223:         />
  224:       </div>
  225:     </Card>
  226:   );
  227: }

FILE: components\Simulators\LogicGateSimulator.tsx
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { DragEvent, useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
    6: import { useProgress } from "@/contexts/ProgressContext";
    7: 
    8: import SimulatorControls from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorControls";
    9: import SimulatorDifficulty from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorDifficulty";
   10: import SimulatorFeedback from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorFeedback";
   11: import SimulatorStats from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorStats";
   12: 
   13: import {
   14:   type SimulatorDifficulty as SimulatorDifficultyLevel,
   15:   useSimulator,
   16: } from "@/components/Simulators/common/useSimulator";
   17: 
   18: /* =========================================================
   19:    TYPES
   20: ========================================================= */
   21: 
   22: type Bit = 0 | 1;
   23: 
   24: type Gate = "AND" | "OR" | "NOT" | "NAND" | "NOR" | "XOR";
   25: 
   26: type LogicQuestion = {
   27:   id: string;
   28:   gate: Gate;
   29:   a: Bit;
   30:   b: Bit;
   31:   answer: Bit;
   32:   hint: string;
   33:   working: string;
   34:   examinerTip: string;
   35: };
   36: 
   37: type CircuitTask = {
   38:   id: string;
   39:   title: string;
   40:   description: string;
   41: 
   42:   difficulty: SimulatorDifficultyLevel;
   43: 
   44:   slot1: Gate;
   45:   slot2: Gate | null;
   46: 
   47:   expression: string;
   48: 
   49:   hint: string;
   50:   working: string;
   51: };
   52: 
   53: /* =========================================================
   54:    CONSTANTS
   55: ========================================================= */
   56: 
   57: const gates: Gate[] = ["AND", "OR", "NOT", "NAND", "NOR", "XOR"];
   58: 
   59: const truthRows: Array<[Bit, Bit]> = [
   60:   [0, 0],
   61:   [0, 1],
   62:   [1, 0],
   63:   [1, 1],
   64: ];
   65: 
   66: /* =========================================================
   67:    LOGIC HELPERS
   68: ========================================================= */
   69: 
   70: function calculateGate(gate: Gate, a: Bit, b: Bit): Bit {
   71:   if (gate === "NOT") {
   72:     return a === 1 ? 0 : 1;
   73:   }
   74: 
   75:   if (gate === "AND") {
   76:     return a === 1 && b === 1 ? 1 : 0;
   77:   }
   78: 
   79:   if (gate === "OR") {
   80:     return a === 1 || b === 1 ? 1 : 0;
   81:   }
   82: 
   83:   if (gate === "NAND") {
   84:     return a === 1 && b === 1 ? 0 : 1;
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   if (gate === "NOR") {
   88:     return a === 1 || b === 1 ? 0 : 1;
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   return a !== b ? 1 : 0;
   92: }
   93: 
   94: function gateRule(gate: Gate): string {
   95:   switch (gate) {
   96:     case "AND":
   97:       return "AND outputs 1 only when both inputs are 1.";
   98: 
   99:     case "OR":
  100:       return "OR outputs 1 when at least one input is 1.";
  101: 
  102:     case "NOT":
  103:       return "NOT reverses its single input.";
  104: 
  105:     case "NAND":
  106:       return "NAND is the opposite of AND.";
  107: 
  108:     case "NOR":
  109:       return "NOR is the opposite of OR.";
  110: 
  111:     case "XOR":
  112:       return "XOR outputs 1 when the two inputs are different.";
  113: 
  114:     default:
  115:       return "";
  116:   }
  117: }
  118: 
  119: function randomBit(): Bit {
  120:   return Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0 : 1;
  121: }
  122: 
  123: function randomItem<T>(items: T[]): T {
  124:   return items[Math.floor(Math.random() * items.length)];
  125: }
  126: 
  127: function gatePool(difficulty: SimulatorDifficultyLevel): Gate[] {
  128:   if (difficulty === "foundation") {
  129:     return ["AND", "OR", "NOT"];
  130:   }
  131: 
  132:   if (difficulty === "intermediate") {
  133:     return ["AND", "OR", "NOT", "NAND", "NOR"];
  134:   }
  135: 
  136:   return ["AND", "OR", "NOT", "NAND", "NOR", "XOR"];
  137: }
  138: 
  139: /* =========================================================
  140:    QUESTION GENERATOR
  141: ========================================================= */
  142: 
  143: function generateLogicQuestion(
  144:   difficulty: SimulatorDifficultyLevel,
  145: ): LogicQuestion {
  146:   const gate = randomItem(gatePool(difficulty));
  147: 
  148:   const a = randomBit();
  149:   const b = gate === "NOT" ? 0 : randomBit();
  150: 
  151:   const answer = calculateGate(gate, a, b);
  152: 
  153:   const id = `${gate}-${a}-${b}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
  154: 
  155:   return {
  156:     id,
  157:     gate,
  158:     a,
  159:     b,
  160:     answer,
  161: 
  162:     hint:
  163:       gate === "NOT"
  164:         ? `A NOT gate reverses the input. Think about the opposite of ${a}.`
  165:         : `${gateRule(gate)} Apply that rule to A = ${a} and B = ${b}.`,
  166: 
  167:     working:
  168:       gate === "NOT"
  169:         ? `Gate: NOT\nInput A = ${a}\nNOT ${a} = ${answer}\n\nFinal output = ${answer}`
  170:         : `Gate: ${gate}\nInput A = ${a}\nInput B = ${b}\n\n${gateRule(
  171:             gate,
  172:           )}\n\nFinal output = ${answer}`,
  173: 
  174:     examinerTip:
  175:       difficulty === "higher"
  176:         ? "For multi-stage logic questions, calculate each intermediate gate output before moving to the next gate."
  177:         : "Use the truth-table rule for the gate before selecting the output.",
  178:   };
  179: }
  180: 
  181: /* =========================================================
  182:    CIRCUIT TASKS
  183: ========================================================= */
  184: 
  185: const circuitTasks: CircuitTask[] = [
  186:   {
  187:     id: "foundation-and",
  188:     title: "Build an AND circuit",
  189:     description: "Use the correct gate so the circuit calculates A AND B.",
  190:     difficulty: "foundation",
  191:     slot1: "AND",
  192:     slot2: null,
  193:     expression: "A AND B",
  194:     hint: "The required gate should output 1 only when A and B are both 1.",
  195:     working:
  196:       "A AND B requires one AND gate. Connect A and B to the AND gate, then connect its result to OUTPUT.",
  197:   },
  198: 
  199:   {
  200:     id: "foundation-or",
  201:     title: "Build an OR circuit",
  202:     description: "Use the correct gate so the circuit calculates A OR B.",
  203:     difficulty: "foundation",
  204:     slot1: "OR",
  205:     slot2: null,
  206:     expression: "A OR B",
  207:     hint: "The required gate outputs 1 when at least one input is 1.",
  208:     working:
  209:       "A OR B requires one OR gate. A and B enter the OR gate and its result becomes the final output.",
  210:   },
  211: 
  212:   {
  213:     id: "foundation-not",
  214:     title: "Build a NOT circuit",
  215:     description: "Use the correct gate to reverse input A.",
  216:     difficulty: "foundation",
  217:     slot1: "NOT",
  218:     slot2: null,
  219:     expression: "NOT A",
  220:     hint: "You need the gate that reverses a single binary input.",
  221:     working:
  222:       "NOT uses only input A. If A = 1 the output becomes 0; if A = 0 the output becomes 1.",
  223:   },
  224: 
  225:   {
  226:     id: "intermediate-and-or",
  227:     title: "Build a two-stage circuit",
  228:     description: "Create (A AND B) OR C.",
  229:     difficulty: "intermediate",
  230:     slot1: "AND",
  231:     slot2: "OR",
  232:     expression: "(A AND B) OR C",
  233:     hint: "Calculate A AND B first. Then combine that result with C.",
  234:     working:
  235:       "Stage 1: A AND B.\nStage 2: Take the result of Stage 1 and OR it with C.\n\nRequired gates: AND â†’ OR.",
  236:   },
  237: 
  238:   {
  239:     id: "intermediate-or-and",
  240:     title: "Build a two-stage circuit",
  241:     description: "Create (A OR B) AND C.",
  242:     difficulty: "intermediate",
  243:     slot1: "OR",
  244:     slot2: "AND",
  245:     expression: "(A OR B) AND C",
  246:     hint: "The first gate combines A and B using OR.",
  247:     working:
  248:       "Stage 1: A OR B.\nStage 2: AND the Stage 1 result with C.\n\nRequired gates: OR â†’ AND.",
  249:   },
  250: 
  251:   {
  252:     id: "intermediate-nand",
  253:     title: "Build a NAND circuit",
  254:     description: "Create the logical expression A NAND B.",
  255:     difficulty: "intermediate",
  256:     slot1: "NAND",
  257:     slot2: null,
  258:     expression: "A NAND B",
  259:     hint: "NAND produces the opposite result from AND.",
  260:     working: "Use one NAND gate. It behaves like AND followed by NOT.",
  261:   },
  262: 
  263:   {
  264:     id: "higher-xor-and",
  265:     title: "Build a higher circuit",
  266:     description: "Create (A XOR B) AND C.",
  267:     difficulty: "higher",
  268:     slot1: "XOR",
  269:     slot2: "AND",
  270:     expression: "(A XOR B) AND C",
  271:     hint: "The first stage should identify whether A and B are different.",
  272:     working:
  273:       "Stage 1: XOR compares A and B.\nStage 2: AND the XOR result with C.\n\nRequired gates: XOR â†’ AND.",
  274:   },
  275: 
  276:   {
  277:     id: "higher-nand-or",
  278:     title: "Build a higher circuit",
  279:     description: "Create (A NAND B) OR C.",
  280:     difficulty: "higher",
  281:     slot1: "NAND",
  282:     slot2: "OR",
  283:     expression: "(A NAND B) OR C",
  284:     hint: "The first stage is the inverse of A AND B.",
  285:     working:
  286:       "Stage 1: A NAND B.\nStage 2: OR the Stage 1 result with C.\n\nRequired gates: NAND â†’ OR.",
  287:   },
  288: 
  289:   {
  290:     id: "higher-nor-xor",
  291:     title: "Build a higher circuit",
  292:     description: "Create (A NOR B) XOR C.",
  293:     difficulty: "higher",
  294:     slot1: "NOR",
  295:     slot2: "XOR",
  296:     expression: "(A NOR B) XOR C",
  297:     hint: "NOR is the opposite of OR. Its result then enters XOR with C.",
  298:     working:
  299:       "Stage 1: A NOR B.\nStage 2: XOR the Stage 1 result with C.\n\nRequired gates: NOR â†’ XOR.",
  300:   },
  301: ];
  302: 
  303: function getCircuitTask(
  304:   difficulty: SimulatorDifficultyLevel,
  305:   excludeId?: string,
  306: ): CircuitTask {
  307:   let matching = circuitTasks.filter(
  308:     (task) => task.difficulty === difficulty && task.id !== excludeId,
  309:   );
  310: 
  311:   if (matching.length === 0) {
  312:     matching = circuitTasks.filter((task) => task.difficulty === difficulty);
  313:   }
  314: 
  315:   return randomItem(matching);
  316: }
  317: 
  318: /* =========================================================
  319:    SMALL DISPLAY COMPONENTS
  320: ========================================================= */
  321: 
  322: function BitButton({
  323:   value,
  324:   selected,
  325:   onClick,
  326: }: {
  327:   value: Bit;
  328:   selected: boolean;
  329:   onClick: () => void;
  330: }) {
  331:   return (
  332:     <button
  333:       type="button"
  334:       onClick={onClick}
  335:       className={`flex h-14 min-w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-2 text-2xl font-black transition ${
  336:         selected
  337:           ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-600 text-white"
  338:           : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-950 hover:border-blue-300"
  339:       }`}
  340:     >
  341:       {value}
  342:     </button>
  343:   );
  344: }
  345: 
  346: function GateBadge({ gate }: { gate: Gate }) {
  347:   return (
  348:     <div className="rounded-xl border border-violet-300 bg-violet-50 px-6 py-4 text-center">
  349:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-violet-600">
  350:         Logic gate
  351:       </p>
  352: 
  353:       <p className="mt-1 text-xl font-black text-violet-950">{gate}</p>
  354:     </div>
  355:   );
  356: }
  357: 
  358: /* =========================================================
  359:    MAIN COMPONENT
  360: ========================================================= */
  361: 
  362: export default function LogicGateSimulator() {
  363:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
  364: 
  365:   const initialQuestion = useMemo(
  366:     () => generateLogicQuestion("foundation"),
  367:     [],
  368:   );
  369: 
  370:   const {
  371:     difficulty,
  372:     question,
  373: 
  374:     checked,
  375:     correct,
  376: 
  377:     hintVisible,
  378:     workingVisible,
  379: 
  380:     attempts,
  381:     correctAnswers,
  382:     accuracy,
  383:     xp,
  384:     streak,
  385: 
  386:     markAnswer,
  387:     resetQuestion,
  388:     newQuestion,
  389:     changeDifficulty,
  390: 
  391:     toggleHint,
  392:     toggleWorking,
  393:   } = useSimulator<LogicQuestion>({
  394:     initialQuestion,
  395:     generateQuestion: generateLogicQuestion,
  396: 
  397:     xpByDifficulty: {
  398:       foundation: 10,
  399:       intermediate: 15,
  400:       higher: 20,
  401:     },
  402: 
  403:     onAwardXP: addXP,
  404:   });
  405: 
  406:   const [selectedAnswer, setSelectedAnswer] = useState<Bit | null>(null);
  407: 
  408:   /* =======================================================
  409:      EXPLORER STATE
  410:   ======================================================= */
  411: 
  412:   const [explorerGate, setExplorerGate] = useState<Gate>("AND");
  413: 
  414:   const [explorerA, setExplorerA] = useState<Bit>(0);
  415: 
  416:   const [explorerB, setExplorerB] = useState<Bit>(0);
  417: 
  418:   const explorerOutput = calculateGate(explorerGate, explorerA, explorerB);
  419: 
  420:   /* =======================================================
  421:      TRUTH TABLE PRACTICE
  422:   ======================================================= */
  423: 
  424:   const [truthGate, setTruthGate] = useState<Gate>("AND");
  425: 
  426:   const [truthAnswers, setTruthAnswers] = useState<Array<Bit | null>>([
  427:     null,
  428:     null,
  429:     null,
  430:     null,
  431:   ]);
  432: 
  433:   const [truthChecked, setTruthChecked] = useState(false);
  434: 
  435:   const [truthCorrect, setTruthCorrect] = useState(false);
  436: 
  437:   const [truthHintVisible, setTruthHintVisible] = useState(false);
  438: 
  439:   const [truthWorkingVisible, setTruthWorkingVisible] = useState(false);
  440: 
  441:   /* =======================================================
  442:      CIRCUIT BUILDER STATE
  443:   ======================================================= */
  444: 
  445:   const [circuitTask, setCircuitTask] = useState<CircuitTask>(() =>
  446:     getCircuitTask("foundation"),
  447:   );
  448: 
  449:   const [slot1, setSlot1] = useState<Gate | null>(null);
  450: 
  451:   const [slot2, setSlot2] = useState<Gate | null>(null);
  452: 
  453:   const [builderA, setBuilderA] = useState<Bit>(0);
  454: 
  455:   const [builderB, setBuilderB] = useState<Bit>(0);
  456: 
  457:   const [builderC, setBuilderC] = useState<Bit>(0);
  458: 
  459:   const [builderChecked, setBuilderChecked] = useState(false);
  460: 
  461:   const [builderCorrect, setBuilderCorrect] = useState(false);
  462: 
  463:   const [builderMistakes, setBuilderMistakes] = useState(0);
  464: 
  465:   const [builderCorrectAttempts, setBuilderCorrectAttempts] = useState(0);
  466: 
  467:   const [builderHintVisible, setBuilderHintVisible] = useState(false);
  468: 
  469:   const [builderWorkingVisible, setBuilderWorkingVisible] = useState(false);
  470: 
  471:   /* =======================================================
  472:      QUESTION HANDLERS
  473:   ======================================================= */
  474: 
  475:   function checkMainAnswer() {
  476:     if (selectedAnswer === null) {
  477:       return;
  478:     }
  479: 
  480:     markAnswer(selectedAnswer === question.answer);
  481:   }
  482: 
  483:   function resetMainQuestion() {
  484:     setSelectedAnswer(null);
  485:     resetQuestion();
  486:   }
  487: 
  488:   function createMainQuestion() {
  489:     setSelectedAnswer(null);
  490:     newQuestion();
  491:   }
  492: 
  493:   function changeMainDifficulty(nextDifficulty: SimulatorDifficultyLevel) {
  494:     setSelectedAnswer(null);
  495: 
  496:     changeDifficulty(nextDifficulty);
  497: 
  498:     resetCircuitForDifficulty(nextDifficulty);
  499:   }
  500: 
  501:   /* =======================================================
  502:      TRUTH TABLE HANDLERS
  503:   ======================================================= */
  504: 
  505:   function setTruthAnswer(index: number, value: Bit) {
  506:     if (truthChecked) {
  507:       return;
  508:     }
  509: 
  510:     setTruthAnswers((current) => {
  511:       const next = [...current];
  512:       next[index] = value;
  513:       return next;
  514:     });
  515:   }
  516: 
  517:   function checkTruthTable() {
  518:     const requiredRows =
  519:       truthGate === "NOT" ? truthRows.slice(0, 2) : truthRows;
  520: 
  521:     const answers =
  522:       truthGate === "NOT" ? truthAnswers.slice(0, 2) : truthAnswers;
  523: 
  524:     if (answers.some((answer) => answer === null)) {
  525:       return;
  526:     }
  527: 
  528:     const isCorrect = requiredRows.every(
  529:       ([a, b], index) => answers[index] === calculateGate(truthGate, a, b),
  530:     );
  531: 
  532:     setTruthChecked(true);
  533:     setTruthCorrect(isCorrect);
  534:   }
  535: 
  536:   function resetTruthTable() {
  537:     setTruthAnswers([null, null, null, null]);
  538: 
  539:     setTruthChecked(false);
  540:     setTruthCorrect(false);
  541:     setTruthHintVisible(false);
  542:     setTruthWorkingVisible(false);
  543:   }
  544: 
  545:   function changeTruthGate(gate: Gate) {
  546:     setTruthGate(gate);
  547:     resetTruthTable();
  548:   }
  549: 
  550:   /* =======================================================
  551:      BUILDER HELPERS
  552:   ======================================================= */
  553: 
  554:   const builderStage1Output =
  555:     slot1 === null ? null : calculateGate(slot1, builderA, builderB);
  556: 
  557:   const builderFinalOutput =
  558:     slot1 === null
  559:       ? null
  560:       : circuitTask.slot2 === null
  561:         ? builderStage1Output
  562:         : slot2 === null || builderStage1Output === null
  563:           ? null
  564:           : calculateGate(slot2, builderStage1Output, builderC);
  565: 
  566:   const builderAttempts = builderCorrectAttempts + builderMistakes;
  567: 
  568:   const builderAccuracy =
  569:     builderAttempts === 0
  570:       ? 0
  571:       : Math.round((builderCorrectAttempts / builderAttempts) * 100);
  572: 
  573:   function clearBuilderFeedback() {
  574:     setBuilderChecked(false);
  575:     setBuilderCorrect(false);
  576:     setBuilderHintVisible(false);
  577:     setBuilderWorkingVisible(false);
  578:   }
  579: 
  580:   function resetCircuitForDifficulty(nextDifficulty: SimulatorDifficultyLevel) {
  581:     setCircuitTask(getCircuitTask(nextDifficulty));
  582: 
  583:     setSlot1(null);
  584:     setSlot2(null);
  585: 
  586:     setBuilderA(0);
  587:     setBuilderB(0);
  588:     setBuilderC(0);
  589: 
  590:     setBuilderChecked(false);
  591:     setBuilderCorrect(false);
  592: 
  593:     setBuilderMistakes(0);
  594:     setBuilderCorrectAttempts(0);
  595: 
  596:     setBuilderHintVisible(false);
  597:     setBuilderWorkingVisible(false);
  598:   }
  599: 
  600:   function newCircuitTask() {
  601:     setCircuitTask(getCircuitTask(difficulty, circuitTask.id));
  602: 
  603:     setSlot1(null);
  604:     setSlot2(null);
  605: 
  606:     setBuilderA(0);
  607:     setBuilderB(0);
  608:     setBuilderC(0);
  609: 
  610:     clearBuilderFeedback();
  611:   }
  612: 
  613:   function placeGate(slot: 1 | 2, gate: Gate) {
  614:     if (slot === 1) {
  615:       setSlot1(gate);
  616:     } else {
  617:       setSlot2(gate);
  618:     }
  619: 
  620:     clearBuilderFeedback();
  621:   }
  622: 
  623:   function handleGateDragStart(
  624:     event: DragEvent<HTMLButtonElement>,
  625:     gate: Gate,
  626:   ) {
  627:     event.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", gate);
  628: 
  629:     event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "copy";
  630:   }
  631: 
  632:   function handleDrop(event: DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>, slot: 1 | 2) {
  633:     event.preventDefault();
  634: 
  635:     const gate = event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain") as Gate;
  636: 
  637:     if (!gates.includes(gate)) {
  638:       return;
  639:     }
  640: 
  641:     placeGate(slot, gate);
  642:   }
  643: 
  644:   function checkCircuit() {
  645:     const correctSlot1 = slot1 === circuitTask.slot1;
  646: 
  647:     const correctSlot2 =
  648:       circuitTask.slot2 === null ? true : slot2 === circuitTask.slot2;
  649: 
  650:     const isCorrect = correctSlot1 && correctSlot2;
  651: 
  652:     setBuilderChecked(true);
  653:     setBuilderCorrect(isCorrect);
  654: 
  655:     if (isCorrect) {
  656:       setBuilderCorrectAttempts((current) => current + 1);
  657:     } else {
  658:       setBuilderMistakes((current) => current + 1);
  659:     }
  660:   }
  661: 
  662:   /* =======================================================
  663:      RENDER
  664:   ======================================================= */
  665: 
  666:   return (
  667:     <Card>
  668:       <div className="space-y-8">
  669:         {/* =================================================
  670:             HEADER
  671:         ================================================= */}
  672: 
  673:         <header>
  674:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">
  675:             Logic gate laboratory
  676:           </p>
  677: 
  678:           <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">
  679:             Logic Gate Challenge
  680:           </h2>
  681: 
  682:           <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  683:             Predict logic-gate outputs, investigate how gates behave, complete
  684:             truth tables and build working logic circuits yourself.
  685:           </p>
  686:         </header>
  687: 
  688:         {/* =================================================
  689:             DIFFICULTY
  690:         ================================================= */}
  691: 
  692:         <SimulatorDifficulty
  693:           value={difficulty}
  694:           onChange={changeMainDifficulty}
  695:         />
  696: 
  697:         {/* =================================================
  698:             STATS
  699:         ================================================= */}
  700: 
  701:         <SimulatorStats
  702:           attempts={attempts}
  703:           correct={correctAnswers}
  704:           accuracy={accuracy}
  705:           xp={xp}
  706:           streak={streak}
  707:         />
  708: 
  709:         {/* =================================================
  710:             MAIN CHALLENGE
  711:         ================================================= */}
  712: 
  713:         <section className="rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-5">
  714:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  715:             Current challenge
  716:           </p>
  717: 
  718:           <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  719:             What is the final output?
  720:           </h3>
  721: 
  722:           <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
  723:             Work through the logic gates using binary values 0 and 1.
  724:           </p>
  725:         </section>
  726: 
  727:         <section className="rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 p-7 text-white">
  728:           <div
  729:             className={`grid items-center gap-5 ${
  730:               question.gate === "NOT"
  731:                 ? "md:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr]"
  732:                 : "md:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr_auto_1fr]"
  733:             }`}
  734:           >
  735:             <div className="text-center">
  736:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-300">
  737:                 Input A
  738:               </p>
  739: 
  740:               <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">{question.a}</p>
  741:             </div>
  742: 
  743:             <span className="text-xl text-slate-400">â†’</span>
  744: 
  745:             <GateBadge gate={question.gate} />
  746: 
  747:             {question.gate !== "NOT" && (
  748:               <>
  749:                 <span className="text-xl text-slate-400">â†</span>
  750: 
  751:                 <div className="text-center">
  752:                   <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-300">
  753:                     Input B
  754:                   </p>
  755: 
  756:                   <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">{question.b}</p>
  757:                 </div>
  758:               </>
  759:             )}
  760:           </div>
  761: 
  762:           <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/30 p-5 text-center">
  763:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-400">
  764:               Output
  765:             </p>
  766: 
  767:             <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black text-white">
  768:               {checked ? question.answer : "?"}
  769:             </p>
  770:           </div>
  771:         </section>
  772: 
  773:         <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
  774:           <p className="font-black text-slate-950">Select the final output</p>
  775: 
  776:           <div className="mt-4 flex gap-3">
  777:             <BitButton
  778:               value={0}
  779:               selected={selectedAnswer === 0}
  780:               onClick={() => setSelectedAnswer(0)}
  781:             />
  782: 
  783:             <BitButton
  784:               value={1}
  785:               selected={selectedAnswer === 1}
  786:               onClick={() => setSelectedAnswer(1)}
  787:             />
  788:           </div>
  789: 
  790:           <div className="mt-5">
  791:             <SimulatorControls
  792:               canCheck={selectedAnswer !== null}
  793:               checked={checked}
  794:               hintVisible={hintVisible}
  795:               workingVisible={workingVisible}
  796:               onCheck={checkMainAnswer}
  797:               onHint={toggleHint}
  798:               onToggleWorking={toggleWorking}
  799:               onReset={resetMainQuestion}
  800:               onNewExample={createMainQuestion}
  801:               newExampleLabel="New question"
  802:             />
  803:           </div>
  804:         </section>
  805: 
  806:         <SimulatorFeedback
  807:           checked={checked}
  808:           correct={correct}
  809:           successMessage={`Correct. The final output is ${question.answer}.`}
  810:           errorMessage={`Not quite. Review the ${question.gate} gate rule and try again.`}
  811:           hintVisible={hintVisible}
  812:           hint={question.hint}
  813:           workingVisible={workingVisible}
  814:           working={question.working}
  815:           examinerTip={question.examinerTip}
  816:         />
  817: 
  818:         {/* =================================================
  819:             EXPLORER
  820:         ================================================= */}
  821: 
  822:         <hr className="border-slate-200" />
  823: 
  824:         <section>
  825:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-violet-600">
  826:             Hands-on exploration
  827:           </p>
  828: 
  829:           <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  830:             Logic Gate Explorer
  831:           </h3>
  832: 
  833:           <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  834:             Select a gate and toggle its inputs. Watch the output change
  835:             instantly and compare your observations with its truth table.
  836:           </p>
  837: 
  838:           <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  839:             {gates.map((gate) => (
  840:               <button
  841:                 key={gate}
  842:                 type="button"
  843:                 onClick={() => setExplorerGate(gate)}
  844:                 className={`rounded-xl px-4 py-3 font-black transition ${
  845:                   explorerGate === gate
  846:                     ? "bg-violet-600 text-white"
  847:                     : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200"
  848:                 }`}
  849:               >
  850:                 {gate}
  851:               </button>
  852:             ))}
  853:           </div>
  854: 
  855:           <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
  856:             <button
  857:               type="button"
  858:               onClick={() => setExplorerA(explorerA === 0 ? 1 : 0)}
  859:               className="rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 p-7 text-white"
  860:             >
  861:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-300">
  862:                 Input A
  863:               </p>
  864: 
  865:               <p className="mt-2 text-5xl font-black">{explorerA}</p>
  866: 
  867:               <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">Click to toggle</p>
  868:             </button>
  869: 
  870:             {explorerGate !== "NOT" && (
  871:               <button
  872:                 type="button"
  873:                 onClick={() => setExplorerB(explorerB === 0 ? 1 : 0)}
  874:                 className="rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 p-7 text-white"
  875:               >
  876:                 <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-300">
  877:                   Input B
  878:                 </p>
  879: 
  880:                 <p className="mt-2 text-5xl font-black">{explorerB}</p>
  881: 
  882:                 <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">Click to toggle</p>
  883:               </button>
  884:             )}
  885: 
  886:             <div className="rounded-3xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-7 text-center">
  887:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-600">
  888:                 Output
  889:               </p>
  890: 
  891:               <p className="mt-2 text-5xl font-black text-emerald-950">
  892:                 {explorerOutput}
  893:               </p>
  894:             </div>
  895:           </div>
  896: 
  897:           <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 p-5">
  898:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-violet-600">
  899:               {explorerGate} rule
  900:             </p>
  901: 
  902:             <p className="mt-2 font-bold text-violet-950">
  903:               {gateRule(explorerGate)}
  904:             </p>
  905:           </div>
  906: 
  907:           <div className="mt-5 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200">
  908:             <div className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-5 py-4">
  909:               <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  910:                 {explorerGate} truth table
  911:               </p>
  912:             </div>
  913: 
  914:             <table className="w-full">
  915:               <thead>
  916:                 <tr className="text-left text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
  917:                   <th className="px-5 py-3">A</th>
  918: 
  919:                   {explorerGate !== "NOT" && <th className="px-5 py-3">B</th>}
  920: 
  921:                   <th className="px-5 py-3">Output</th>
  922:                 </tr>
  923:               </thead>
  924: 
  925:               <tbody>
  926:                 {(explorerGate === "NOT"
  927:                   ? ([
  928:                       [0, 0],
  929:                       [1, 0],
  930:                     ] as Array<[Bit, Bit]>)
  931:                   : truthRows
  932:                 ).map(([a, b]) => (
  933:                   <tr key={`${a}-${b}`} className="border-t border-slate-100">
  934:                     <td className="px-5 py-4 font-black">{a}</td>
  935: 
  936:                     {explorerGate !== "NOT" && (
  937:                       <td className="px-5 py-4 font-black">{b}</td>
  938:                     )}
  939: 
  940:                     <td className="px-5 py-4 font-black text-emerald-700">
  941:                       {calculateGate(explorerGate, a, b)}
  942:                     </td>
  943:                   </tr>
  944:                 ))}
  945:               </tbody>
  946:             </table>
  947:           </div>
  948:         </section>
  949: 
  950:         {/* =================================================
  951:             TRUTH TABLE PRACTICE
  952:         ================================================= */}
  953: 
  954:         <hr className="border-slate-200" />
  955: 
  956:         <section>
  957:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">
  958:             Hands-on table practice
  959:           </p>
  960: 
  961:           <h3 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  962:             Complete the Truth Table
  963:           </h3>
  964: 
  965:           <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  966:             Choose the gate and calculate every output yourself before checking
  967:             the completed table.
  968:           </p>
  969: 
  970:           <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  971:             {gatePool(difficulty).map((gate) => (
  972:               <button
  973:                 key={gate}
  974:                 type="button"
  975:                 onClick={() => changeTruthGate(gate)}
  976:                 className={`rounded-xl px-4 py-3 font-black ${

  988:             </p>
  989: 
  990:             <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-black">{truthGate}</p>
  991: 
  992:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">{gateRule(truthGate)}</p>
  993:           </div>
  994: 
  995:           <div className="mt-5 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200">
  996:             <table className="w-full">
  997:               <thead className="bg-slate-50">

 1012:                     ] as Array<[Bit, Bit]>)
 1013:                   : truthRows
 1014:                 ).map(([a, b], index) => {
 1015:                   const expected = calculateGate(truthGate, a, b);
 1016: 
 1017:                   const answer = truthAnswers[index];
 1018: 
 1019:                   return (
 1020:                     <tr key={`${a}-${b}`} className="border-t border-slate-100">
 1021:                       <td className="px-5 py-4 font-black">{a}</td>
 1022: 
 1023:                       {truthGate !== "NOT" && (
 1024:                         <td className="px-5 py-4 font-black">{b}</td>
 1025:                       )}
 1026: 
 1027:                       <td className="px-5 py-4">
 1028:                         <div className="flex gap-2">
 1029:                           {([0, 1] as Bit[]).map((value) => (
 1030:                             <button
 1031:                               key={value}
 1032:                               type="button"
 1033:                               disabled={truthChecked}
 1034:                               onClick={() => setTruthAnswer(index, value)}
 1035:                               className={`h-10 w-12 rounded-lg border-2 font-black transition ${
 1036:                                 answer === value
 1037:                                   ? truthChecked
 1038:                                     ? value === expected
 1039:                                       ? "border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500 text-white"

 1043:                               }`}

 1046:                             </button>
 1047:                           ))}

 1049:                       </td>
 1050:                     </tr>
 1051:                   );
 1052:                 })}
 1053:               </tbody>
 1054:             </table>
 1055:           </div>

 1057:           <div className="mt-5">
 1058:             <SimulatorControls
 1059:               canCheck={
 1060:                 truthGate === "NOT"
 1061:                   ? truthAnswers.slice(0, 2).every((value) => value !== null)
 1062:                   : truthAnswers.every((value) => value !== null)
 1063:               }
 1064:               checked={truthChecked}
 1065:               hintVisible={truthHintVisible}
 1066:               workingVisible={truthWorkingVisible}
 1067:               showNewExample={false}
 1068:               checkLabel="Check truth table"
 1069:               resetLabel="Reset table"
 1070:               onCheck={checkTruthTable}
 1071:               onHint={() => setTruthHintVisible((current) => !current)}

 1073:                 setTruthWorkingVisible((current) => !current)

 1085:             hint={gateRule(truthGate)}
 1086:             workingVisible={truthWorkingVisible}
 1087:             working={

 1114:
