   20: 
   21: type ProcedureFeedbackType = "success" | "error" | "info" | null;
   22: 
   23: type MergeGroup = {
   24:   id: string;
   25:   values: number[];
   26: };
   27: 
   28: type MergeState = {
   29:   left: number[];
   30:   right: number[];
   31:   merged: number[];
   32: };
   33: 
   34: function createUniqueValues(length: number): number[] {
   35:   const values = new Set<number>();
   36: 
   37:   while (values.size < length) {
   38:     values.add(Math.floor(Math.random() * 40) + 1);
   39:   }
   40: 
   41:   return Array.from(values);
   42: }
   43: 
   44: function createQuestion(difficulty: DifficultyLevel): Question {
   45:   const length =
   46:     difficulty === "foundation" ? 4 : difficulty === "intermediate" ? 6 : 8;
   47: 
   48:   return {
   49:     values: createUniqueValues(length),
   50:   };
   51: }
   52: 
   53: function normaliseList(value: string): number[] {
   54:   return value
   55:     .split(/[\s,]+/)
   56:     .map((item) => Number(item))
   57:     .filter((item) => Number.isFinite(item));
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function createWorking(question: Question): string {
   61:   const sorted = [...question.values].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   62: 
   63:   return `Merge sort works in two phases:
   64: 
   65: 1. Split the list repeatedly until each sublist contains one item.
   66: 2. Merge neighbouring sublists back together in sorted order.
   67: 3. During each merge, compare the first remaining item from each sublist.
   68: 4. Take the smaller value first.
   69: 5. Continue until one fully sorted list remains.
   70: 
   71: Starting list:
   72: ${question.values.join(", ")}
   73: 
   74: Final sorted list:
   75: ${sorted.join(", ")}`;
   76: }
   77: 
   78: export default function MergeSortSimulator() {
   79:   const { addXP } = useProgress();
   80: 
   81:   const simulator = useSimulator<Question>({
   82:     initialQuestion: createQuestion("foundation"),
   83:     generateQuestion: createQuestion,
   84: 
   85:     xpByDifficulty: {
   86:       foundation: 10,
   87:       intermediate: 15,
   88:       higher: 20,
   89:     },
   90: 
   91:     onAwardXP: addXP,
   92:   });
   93: 
   94:   const {
   95:     difficulty,
   96:     question,
   97: 
   98:     checked,
   99:     correct,
  100: 
  101:     hintVisible,
  102:     workingVisible,
  103: 
  104:     attempts,
  105:     correctAnswers,
  106:     accuracy,
  107:     xp,
  108:     streak,
  109: 
  110:     markAnswer,
  111:     resetQuestion,
  112:     newQuestion,
  113:     changeDifficulty,
  114: 
  115:     toggleHint,
  116:     toggleWorking,
  117:   } = simulator;
  118: 
  119:   /*
  120:    * =========================================================
  121:    * SCORED CHALLENGE
  122:    * =========================================================
  123:    */
  124: 
  125:   const [answer, setAnswer] = useState("");
  126: 
  127:   const sortedValues = useMemo(
  128:     () => [...question.values].sort((a, b) => a - b),
  129:     [question.values],
  130:   );
  131: 
  132:   const submittedValues = normaliseList(answer);
  133: 
  134:   const canCheck = submittedValues.length === question.values.length;
  135: 
  136:   function handleCheck() {
  137:     if (!canCheck) {
  138:       return;
  139:     }
  140: 
  141:     const matches = sortedValues.every(
  142:       (value, index) => value === submittedValues[index],
  143:     );
  144: 
  145:     markAnswer(matches);
  146:   }
  147: 
  148:   function handleTryAgain() {
  149:     setAnswer("");
  150:     resetQuestion();
  151:   }
  152: 
  153:   const working = useMemo(() => createWorking(question), [question]);
  154: 
  155:   /*
  156:    * =========================================================
  157:    * PROCEDURAL TRAINER
  158:    * =========================================================
  159:    */
  160: 
  161:   const initialGroup: MergeGroup = {
  162:     id: "root",
  163:     values: [...question.values],
  164:   };
  165: 
  166:   const [groups, setGroups] = useState<MergeGroup[]>([initialGroup]);
  167: 
  168:   const [selectedGroupId, setSelectedGroupId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  169: 
  170:   const [phase, setPhase] = useState<"split" | "merge" | "complete">("split");
  171: 
  172:   const [mergeQueue, setMergeQueue] = useState<MergeGroup[][]>([]);
  173: 
  174:   const [currentMerge, setCurrentMerge] = useState<MergeState | null>(null);
  175: 
  176:   const [currentMergePairIds, setCurrentMergePairIds] = useState<
  177:     [string, string] | null
  178:   >(null);
  179: 
  180:   const [mergeRoundGroups, setMergeRoundGroups] = useState<MergeGroup[]>([]);
  181: 
  182:   const [nextRoundGroups, setNextRoundGroups] = useState<MergeGroup[]>([]);
  183: 
  184:   const [procedureCorrectSteps, setProcedureCorrectSteps] = useState(0);
  185: 
  186:   const [procedureMistakes, setProcedureMistakes] = useState(0);
  187: 
  188:   const [splitsCompleted, setSplitsCompleted] = useState(0);
  189: 
  190:   const [mergesCompleted, setMergesCompleted] = useState(0);
  191: 
  192:   const [procedureFeedback, setProcedureFeedback] = useState("");
  193: 
  194:   const [procedureFeedbackType, setProcedureFeedbackType] =
  195:     useState<ProcedureFeedbackType>(null);
  196: 
  197:   const procedureAttempts = procedureCorrectSteps + procedureMistakes;
  198: 
  199:   const procedureAccuracy =
  200:     procedureAttempts === 0
  201:       ? 0
  202:       : Math.round((procedureCorrectSteps / procedureAttempts) * 100);
  203: 
  204:   const allGroupsSingle =
  205:     groups.length > 0 && groups.every((group) => group.values.length === 1);
  206: 
  207:   function resetProcedure() {
  208:     setGroups([
  209:       {
  210:         id: "root",
  211:         values: [...question.values],
  212:       },
  213:     ]);
  214: 
  215:     setSelectedGroupId(null);
  216:     setPhase("split");
  217: 
  218:     setMergeQueue([]);
  219:     setCurrentMerge(null);
  220:     setCurrentMergePairIds(null);
  221: 
  222:     setMergeRoundGroups([]);
  223:     setNextRoundGroups([]);
  224: 
  225:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  226:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  227: 
  228:     setSplitsCompleted(0);
  229:     setMergesCompleted(0);
  230: 
  231:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  232:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  233:   }
  234: 
  235:   function handleNewQuestion() {
  236:     setAnswer("");
  237: 
  238:     setGroups([]);
  239:     setSelectedGroupId(null);
  240:     setPhase("split");
  241: 
  242:     setMergeQueue([]);
  243:     setCurrentMerge(null);
  244:     setCurrentMergePairIds(null);
  245: 
  246:     setMergeRoundGroups([]);
  247:     setNextRoundGroups([]);
  248: 
  249:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  250:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  251: 
  252:     setSplitsCompleted(0);
  253:     setMergesCompleted(0);
  254: 
  255:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  256:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  257: 
  258:     newQuestion();
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   function handleDifficultyChange(nextDifficulty: DifficultyLevel) {
  262:     setAnswer("");
  263: 
  264:     setGroups([]);
  265:     setSelectedGroupId(null);
  266:     setPhase("split");
  267: 
  268:     setMergeQueue([]);
  269:     setCurrentMerge(null);
  270:     setCurrentMergePairIds(null);
  271: 
  272:     setMergeRoundGroups([]);
  273:     setNextRoundGroups([]);
  274: 
  275:     setProcedureCorrectSteps(0);
  276:     setProcedureMistakes(0);
  277: 
  278:     setSplitsCompleted(0);
  279:     setMergesCompleted(0);
  280: 
  281:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  282:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  283: 
  284:     changeDifficulty(nextDifficulty);
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   function ensureProcedureInitialised() {
  288:     if (groups.length === 0) {
  289:       setGroups([
  290:         {
  291:           id: "root",
  292:           values: [...question.values],
  293:         },
  294:       ]);
  295:     }
  296:   }
  297: 
  298:   function recordMistake(message: string) {
  299:     setProcedureMistakes((current) => current + 1);
  300:     setProcedureFeedback(message);
  301:     setProcedureFeedbackType("error");
  302:   }
  303: 
  304:   function recordSuccess(message: string) {
  305:     setProcedureCorrectSteps((current) => current + 1);
  306:     setProcedureFeedback(message);
  307:     setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  308:   }
  309: 
  310:   /*
  311:    * =========================================================
  312:    * SPLIT PHASE
  313:    * =========================================================
  314:    */
  315: 
  316:   function nextSplittableGroup(): MergeGroup | undefined {
  317:     return groups.find((group) => group.values.length > 1);
  318:   }
  319: 
  320:   function selectGroup(groupId: string) {
  321:     if (phase !== "split" || difficulty === "foundation") {
  322:       return;
  323:     }
  324: 
  325:     setSelectedGroupId(groupId);
  326:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  327:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  328:   }
  329: 
  330:   function splitGroup(group: MergeGroup) {
  331:     if (group.values.length <= 1) {
  332:       recordMistake("A single-item list cannot be split any further.");
  333:       return;
  334:     }
  335: 
  336:     const middle = Math.ceil(group.values.length / 2);
  337: 
  338:     const leftValues = group.values.slice(0, middle);
  339:     const rightValues = group.values.slice(middle);
  340: 
  341:     const leftGroup: MergeGroup = {
  342:       id: `${group.id}-L`,
  343:       values: leftValues,
  344:     };
  345: 
  346:     const rightGroup: MergeGroup = {
  347:       id: `${group.id}-R`,
  348:       values: rightValues,
  349:     };
  350: 
  351:     setGroups((current) => {
  352:       const index = current.findIndex((item) => item.id === group.id);
  353: 
  354:       if (index === -1) {
  355:         return current;
  356:       }
  357: 
  358:       const updated = [...current];
  359: 
  360:       updated.splice(index, 1, leftGroup, rightGroup);
  361: 
  362:       return updated;
  363:     });
  364: 
  365:     setSplitsCompleted((current) => current + 1);
  366: 
  367:     recordSuccess(
  368:       `Correct. [${group.values.join(", ")}] splits into [${leftValues.join(
  369:         ", ",
  370:       )}] and [${rightValues.join(", ")}].`,
  371:     );
  372: 
  373:     setSelectedGroupId(null);
  374:   }
  375: 
  376:   function handleSplit() {
  377:     ensureProcedureInitialised();
  378: 
  379:     if (phase !== "split") {
  380:       return;
  381:     }
  382: 
  383:     if (difficulty === "foundation") {
  384:       const group = nextSplittableGroup();
  385: 
  386:       if (!group) {
  387:         recordMistake("All groups are already single-item lists.");
  388:         return;
  389:       }
  390: 
  391:       splitGroup(group);
  392:       return;
  393:     }
  394: 
  395:     if (!selectedGroupId) {
  396:       recordMistake("Select a group that still contains more than one value.");
  397:       return;
  398:     }
  399: 
  400:     const selectedGroup = groups.find((group) => group.id === selectedGroupId);
  401: 
  402:     if (!selectedGroup) {
  403:       return;
  404:     }
  405: 
  406:     if (selectedGroup.values.length <= 1) {
  407:       recordMistake(
  408:         "That group already contains one item and cannot be split further.",
  409:       );
  410:       return;
  411:     }
  412: 
  413:     splitGroup(selectedGroup);
  414:   }
  415: 
  416:   /*
  417:    * =========================================================
  418:    * MERGE PHASE SETUP
  419:    * =========================================================
  420:    */
  421: 
  422:   function beginMergePhase() {
  423:     if (!allGroupsSingle) {
  424:       recordMistake(
  425:         "Merge Sort cannot begin merging until every group contains one item.",
  426:       );
  427:       return;
  428:     }
  429: 
  430:     const round = groups.map((group) => ({
  431:       id: group.id,
  432:       values: [...group.values],
  433:     }));
  434: 
  435:     setMergeRoundGroups(round);
  436:     setNextRoundGroups([]);
  437:     setMergeQueue([]);
  438: 
  439:     setPhase("merge");
  440:     setSelectedGroupId(null);
  441: 
  442:     recordSuccess(
  443:       "Correct. Every sublist now contains one item, so the merge phase can begin.",
  444:     );
  445: 
  446:     startNextMergePair(round, []);
  447:   }
  448: 
  449:   function startNextMergePair(
  450:     roundGroups: MergeGroup[],
  451:     builtNextRound: MergeGroup[],
  452:   ) {
  453:     if (roundGroups.length === 0) {
  454:       finishMergeRound(builtNextRound);
  455:       return;
  456:     }
  457: 
  458:     if (roundGroups.length === 1) {
  459:       finishMergeRound([...builtNextRound, roundGroups[0]]);
  460:       return;
  461:     }
  462: 
  463:     const left = roundGroups[0];
  464:     const right = roundGroups[1];
  465: 
  466:     setMergeRoundGroups(roundGroups.slice(2));
  467:     setNextRoundGroups(builtNextRound);
  468: 
  469:     setCurrentMerge({
  470:       left: [...left.values],
  471:       right: [...right.values],
  472:       merged: [],
  473:     });
  474: 
  475:     setCurrentMergePairIds([left.id, right.id]);
  476: 
  477:     setProcedureFeedback(
  478:       `Merge [${left.values.join(", ")}] with [${right.values.join(
  479:         ", ",
  480:       )}]. Choose the smaller front value.`,
  481:     );
  482: 
  483:     setProcedureFeedbackType("info");
  484:   }
  485: 
  486:   function finishMergeRound(completedGroups: MergeGroup[]) {
  487:     if (completedGroups.length === 1) {
  488:       setGroups(completedGroups);
  489:       setCurrentMerge(null);
  490:       setPhase("complete");
  491: 
  492:       setProcedureFeedback(
  493:         "Excellent. All sublists have been merged into one sorted list.",
  494:       );
  495: 
  496:       setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  497:       return;
  498:     }
  499: 
  500:     setGroups(completedGroups);
  501: 
  502:     setMergeRoundGroups(completedGroups);
  503:     setNextRoundGroups([]);
  504: 
  505:     setCurrentMerge(null);
  506: 
  507:     setProcedureFeedback(
  508:       "Merge round complete. Begin merging the newly formed sorted sublists.",
  509:     );
  510: 
  511:     setProcedureFeedbackType("success");
  512: 
  513:     startNextMergePair(completedGroups, []);
  514:   }
  515: 
  516:   /*
  517:    * =========================================================
  518:    * MERGE INTERACTION
  519:    * =========================================================
  520:    */
  521: 
  522:   function takeFromSide(side: "left" | "right") {
  523:     if (phase !== "merge" || !currentMerge) {
  524:       return;
  525:     }
  526: 
  527:     const leftFront = currentMerge.left[0];
  528:     const rightFront = currentMerge.right[0];
  529: 
  530:     if (leftFront === undefined && rightFront === undefined) {
  531:       return;
  532:     }
  533: 
  534:     let expectedSide: "left" | "right";
  535: 
  536:     if (leftFront === undefined) {
  537:       expectedSide = "right";
  538:     } else if (rightFront === undefined) {
  539:       expectedSide = "left";
  540:     } else {
  541:       expectedSide = leftFront <= rightFront ? "left" : "right";
  542:     }
  543: 
  544:     if (side !== expectedSide) {
  545:       const chosenValue = side === "left" ? leftFront : rightFront;
  546: 
  547:       const correctValue = expectedSide === "left" ? leftFront : rightFront;
  548: 
  549:       recordMistake(
  550:         `Not quite. ${correctValue} is the smaller front value, so it must be taken before ${chosenValue}.`,
  551:       );
  552: 
  553:       return;
  554:     }
  555: 
  556:     const nextLeft = [...currentMerge.left];
  557:     const nextRight = [...currentMerge.right];
  558: 
  559:     const value = side === "left" ? nextLeft.shift() : nextRight.shift();
  560: 
  561:     if (value === undefined) {
  562:       return;
  563:     }
  564: 
  565:     const nextMerged = [...currentMerge.merged, value];
  566: 
  567:     recordSuccess(`Correct. ${value} is the next smallest available value.`);
  568: 
  569:     const mergeFinished = nextLeft.length === 0 && nextRight.length === 0;
  570: 
  571:     if (!mergeFinished) {
  572:       setCurrentMerge({
  573:         left: nextLeft,
  574:         right: nextRight,
  575:         merged: nextMerged,
  576:       });
  577: 
  578:       return;
  579:     }
  580: 
  581:     const mergedGroup: MergeGroup = {
  582:       id: currentMergePairIds
  583:         ? `${currentMergePairIds[0]}+${currentMergePairIds[1]}`
  584:         : `merged-${mergesCompleted + 1}`,
  585:       values: nextMerged,
  586:     };
  587: 
  588:     const updatedNextRound = [...nextRoundGroups, mergedGroup];
  589: 
  590:     setMergesCompleted((current) => current + 1);
  591: 
  592:     setCurrentMerge(null);
  593:     setCurrentMergePairIds(null);
  594: 
  595:     startNextMergePair(mergeRoundGroups, updatedNextRound);
  596:   }
  597: 
  598:   /*
  599:    * Higher difficulty allows the learner to select the value itself.
  600:    */
  601: 
  602:   const [selectedMergeSide, setSelectedMergeSide] = useState<
  603:     "left" | "right" | null
  604:   >(null);
  605: 
  606:   function selectMergeSide(side: "left" | "right") {
  607:     if (difficulty !== "higher" || phase !== "merge" || !currentMerge) {
  608:       return;
  609:     }
  610: 
  611:     setSelectedMergeSide(side);
  612:     setProcedureFeedback("");
  613:     setProcedureFeedbackType(null);
  614:   }
  615: 
  616:   function confirmMergeSelection() {
  617:     if (!selectedMergeSide) {
  618:       recordMistake("Select one of the two front values first.");
  619:       return;
  620:     }
  621: 
  622:     takeFromSide(selectedMergeSide);
  623:     setSelectedMergeSide(null);
  624:   }
  625: 
  626:   const procedureGroups =
  627:     groups.length > 0
  628:       ? groups
  629:       : [
  630:           {
  631:             id: "root",
  632:             values: [...question.values],
  633:           },
  634:         ];
  635: 
  636:   return (
  637:     <section className="space-y-8 rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm md:p-8">
  638:       <header>
  639:         <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  640:           Algorithm laboratory
  641:         </p>
  642: 
  643:         <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">
  644:           Merge Sort Challenge
  645:         </h2>
  646: 
  647:         <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  648:           Predict the final sorted list, then perform Merge Sort yourself by
  649:           splitting the data into smaller lists and merging them back together
  650:           in order.
  651:         </p>
  652:       </header>
  653: 
  654:       <SimulatorDifficulty
  655:         value={difficulty}
  656:         onChange={handleDifficultyChange}
  657:       />
  658: 
  659:       <SimulatorStats
  660:         attempts={attempts}
  661:         correct={correctAnswers}
  662:         accuracy={accuracy}
  663:         xp={xp}
  664:         streak={streak}
  665:       />
  666: 
  667:       {/* ==================================================== */}
  668:       {/* SCORED CHALLENGE                                     */}
  669:       {/* ==================================================== */}
  670: 
  671:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6">
  672:         <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  673:           Final-order challenge
  674:         </p>
  675: 
  676:         <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black">
  677:           Sort this list into ascending order.
  678:         </h3>
  679: 
  680:         <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  681:           {question.values.map((value, index) => (
  682:             <div
  683:               key={`${value}-${index}`}
  684:               className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white text-xl font-black"
  685:             >
  686:               {value}
  687:             </div>
  688:           ))}
  689:         </div>
  690:       </section>
  691: 
  692:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6">
  693:         <label htmlFor="merge-sort-answer" className="font-black">
  694:           Enter the final sorted list
  695:         </label>
  696: 
  697:         <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
  698:           Separate values using commas or spaces.
  699:         </p>
  700: 
  701:         <input
  702:           id="merge-sort-answer"
  703:           value={answer}
  704:           disabled={checked}
  705:           onChange={(event) => setAnswer(event.target.value)}
  706:           placeholder="Example: 2, 3, 6, 8"
  707:           className="mt-3 w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-300 px-5 py-4 font-mono text-xl font-black outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100 disabled:bg-slate-100"
  708:         />
  709: 
  710:         <div className="mt-5">
  711:           <SimulatorControls
  712:             canCheck={canCheck}
  713:             checked={checked}
  714:             hintVisible={hintVisible}
  715:             workingVisible={workingVisible}
  716:             resetLabel="Try again"
  717:             newExampleLabel="New question"
  718:             onCheck={handleCheck}
  719:             onHint={toggleHint}
  720:             onToggleWorking={toggleWorking}
  721:             onReset={handleTryAgain}
  722:             onNewExample={handleNewQuestion}
  723:           />
  724:         </div>
  725:       </section>
  726: 
  727:       <SimulatorFeedback
  728:         checked={checked}
  729:         correct={correct}
  730:         successMessage={`Correct. The sorted list is ${sortedValues.join(", ")}.`}
  731:         errorMessage={`Not quite. The correct sorted order is ${sortedValues.join(", ")}.`}
  732:         hintVisible={hintVisible}
  733:         hint="Merge Sort repeatedly splits the list into smaller sublists, then merges those sublists back together in sorted order."
  734:         workingVisible={workingVisible}
  735:         working={working}
  736:         examinerTip="Merge sort is a divide-and-conquer algorithm. It repeatedly divides the list and then merges sorted sublists until one sorted list remains."
  737:       />
  738: 
  739:       {/* ==================================================== */}
  740:       {/* HANDS-ON PROCEDURAL PRACTICE                         */}
  741:       {/* ==================================================== */}
  742:

  829:                     ? "Select a group containing more than one item, then split it."

  840:                     difficulty === "foundation" &&

   23:   InterventionEffectivenessItem,
   24:   InterventionEffectivenessStatus,
   25: } from "@/types/interventionEffectiveness";

   27: function badgeStyle(status: InterventionEffectivenessStatus): string {
   28:   switch (status) {
   29:     case "improving":
   30:       return "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800";
   31:     case "declining":
   32:       return "bg-red-100 text-red-800";
   33:     case "awaiting_evidence":
   34:       return "bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800";
   35:     case "baseline_needed":
   36:       return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800";
   37:     default:
   38:       return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";
   39:   }
   40: }
   41: 
   42: function changeText(value: number | null, suffix = ""): string {
   43:   if (value === null) return "â€”";
   44:   if (value > 0) return `+${value}${suffix}`;
   45:   return `${value}${suffix}`;
   46: }

   48: export default function InterventionEffectivenessOverview({
   49:   teacherId,
   50:   interventions,
   51: }: {
   52:   teacherId: string;
   53:   interventions: InterventionEffectivenessSource[];
   54: }) {
   55:   const [items, setItems] = useState<InterventionEffectivenessItem[]>([]);
   56:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   57:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   58: 
   59:   async function load() {
   60:     if (!teacherId.trim() || interventions.length === 0) {
   61:       setItems([]);
   62:       setLoading(false);
   63:       return;
   64:     }
   65: 
   66:     try {
   67:       setLoading(true);
   68:       setError("");
   69: 
   70:       const results = await Promise.all(
   71:         interventions.map(async (intervention) => {
   72:           try {
   73:             const impact = await getInterventionImpact({
   74:               interventionId: intervention.id,
   75:               teacherId,
   76:             });
   77: 
   78:             if (!impact) return null;
   79: 
   80:             return buildInterventionEffectivenessItem({
   81:               source: intervention,
   82:               impact,
   83:             });
   84:           } catch (caughtError) {
   85:             console.error(
   86:               `Unable to load intervention effectiveness for ${intervention.id}:`,
   87:               caughtError,
   88:             );
   89: 
   90:             return null;
   91:           }
   92:         }),
   93:       );
   94: 
   95:       setItems(
   96:         results.filter(
   97:           (item): item is InterventionEffectivenessItem => Boolean(item),
   98:         ),
   99:       );
  100:     } catch (caughtError) {
  101:       setError(
  102:         caughtError instanceof Error
  103:           ? caughtError.message
  104:           : "Intervention effectiveness could not be loaded.",
  105:       );
  106:     } finally {
  107:       setLoading(false);
  108:     }
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   useEffect(() => {
  112:     void load();
  113:   }, [teacherId, interventions]);
  114: 
  115:   const summary = useMemo(
  116:     () => buildInterventionEffectivenessSummary(items),
  117:     [items],
  118:   );
  119: 
  120:   return (
  121:     <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-teal-200 p-0">
  122:       <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-teal-950 to-emerald-900 p-6 text-white">
  123:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
  124:           <div>
  125:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-teal-200">
  126:               Intervention effectiveness intelligence
  127:             </p>
  128: 
  129:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">
  130:               Which interventions are producing improvement?
  131:             </h2>
  132: 
  133:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl text-sm leading-6 text-white/70">
  134:               Compare each intervention baseline with subsequent graded
  135:               evidence. Statuses are decision-support signals and should be
  136:               reviewed alongside the underlying assessment evidence.
  137:             </p>
  138:           </div>
  139: 
  140:           <button
  141:             type="button"
  142:             onClick={() => void load()}
  143:             className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-4 text-sm font-black text-teal-950"
  144:           >
  145:             <RefreshCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  146:             Refresh effectiveness
  147:           </button>
  148:         </div>
  149:       </div>
  150: 
  151:       {loading ? (
  152:         <div className="grid gap-4 p-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
  153:           {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, index) => (
  154:             <div
  155:               key={index}
  156:               className="h-24 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-100"
  157:             />
  158:           ))}
  159:         </div>
  160:       ) : error ? (
  161:         <div className="p-6">
  162:           <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5 text-sm font-bold text-amber-900">
  163:             {error}
  164:           </div>
  165:         </div>
  166:       ) : interventions.length === 0 ? (
  167:         <div className="p-6">
  168:           <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
  169:             No interventions available yet
  170:           </p>
  171:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
  172:             Create an intervention and capture a baseline before effectiveness
  173:             can be evaluated.
  174:           </p>
  175:         </div>
  176:       ) : (
  177:         <>
  178:           <div className="grid gap-4 p-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
  179:             <Summary
  180:               label="Improving"
  181:               value={summary.improving}
  182:               icon={<CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
  183:               tone="emerald"
  184:             />
  185:             <Summary
  186:               label="Limited change"
  187:               value={summary.limitedChange}
  188:               icon={<Activity className="h-5 w-5" />}
  189:               tone="slate"
  190:             />
  191:             <Summary
  192:               label="Declining"
  193:               value={summary.declining}
  194:               icon={<AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5" />}
  195:               tone="red"
  196:             />
  197:             <Summary
  198:               label="Awaiting evidence"
  199:               value={summary.awaitingEvidence}
  200:               icon={<Clock3 className="h-5 w-5" />}
  201:               tone="indigo"
  202:             />
  203:             <Summary
  204:               label="Success rate"
  205:               value={
  206:                 summary.successfulRate === null
  207:                   ? "â€”"
  208:                   : `${summary.successfulRate}%`
  209:               }
  210:               icon={<Target className="h-5 w-5" />}
  211:               tone="teal"
  212:             />
  213:           </div>

  219:                 {summary.baselineNeeded === 1 ? "" : "s"} need a baseline.
  220:               </span>{" "}
  221:               Effectiveness cannot be judged reliably until a before-intervention
  222:               comparison point exists.
  223:             </div>

  296: 
  297: function Summary({
  298:   label,
  299:   value,
  300:   icon,
  301:   tone,
  302: }: {
  303:   label: string;
  304:   value: number | string;
  305:   icon: React.ReactNode;
  306:   tone: "emerald" | "slate" | "red" | "indigo" | "teal";
  307: }) {
  308:   const styles = {
  309:     emerald: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700",
  310:     slate: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700",
  311:     red: "bg-red-50 text-red-700",
  312:     indigo: "bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700",
  313:     teal: "bg-teal-50 text-teal-700",
  314:   };
  315: 
  316:   return (
  317:     <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-4">
  318:       <div
  319:         className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${styles[tone]}`}
  320:       >
  321:         {icon}
  322:       </div>
  323:       <p className="mt-4 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
  324:         {label}
  325:       </p>
  326:       <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  327:     </div>
  328:   );
  329: }

  344:             <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl text-sm leading-6 text-white/75">
  345:               {review.explanation}
  346:             </p>
  347:           </div>
  348: 
  349:           <div className="grid gap-5 p-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
  350:             <div className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 p-5">
  351:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-700">
  352:                 Positive signals
  353:               </p>
  354: 
  355:               {review.successSignals.length ? (
  356:                 <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-emerald-950">
  357:                   {review.successSignals.map((item) => (
  358:                     <li
  359:                       key={item}
  360:                       className="flex items-start gap-2"
  361:                     >
  362:                       <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
  363:                       {item}
  364:                     </li>
  365:                   ))}
  366:                 </ul>
  367:               ) : (
  368:                 <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-emerald-900/70">
  369:                   No material positive change is recorded yet.
  370:                 </p>
  371:               )}
  372:             </div>
  373: 
  374:             <div className="rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 p-5">
  375:               <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-amber-700">
  376:                 Concerns
  377:               </p>
  378: 
  379:               {review.concernSignals.length ? (
  380:                 <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-sm text-amber-950">
  381:                   {review.concernSignals.map((item) => (
  382:                     <li
  383:                       key={item}
  384:                       className="flex items-start gap-2"
  385:                     >
  386:                       <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-600" />
  387:                       {item}
  388:                     </li>
  389:                   ))}
  390:                 </ul>
  391:               ) : (
  392:                 <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-amber-900/70">
  393:                   No material decline is recorded.
  394:                 </p>
  395:               )}
  396:             </div>
  397:           </div>
  398: 
  399:           <div className="border-t border-slate-100 p-6">
  400:             {review.reassessmentRecommended && (
  401:               <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  402:                 <div>
  403:                   <p className="font-black text-indigo-950">
  404:                     Focused reassessment recommended
  405:                   </p>
  406: 
  407:                   <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-indigo-800">
  408:                     Assign a new task on {impact.topic}, then return here once it is graded to review the change.
  409:                   </p>
  410:                 </div>
  411: 
  412:                 <Link
  413:                   href={reassessmentHref}
  414:                   className="inline-flex min-h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white"
  415:                 >
  416:                   <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" />
  417:                   Assign reassessment
  418:                 </Link>
  419:               </div>
  420:             )}
  421: 
  422:             <label className="block">
  423:               <span className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900">
  424:                 Teacher review note
  425:               </span>
  426: 
  427:               <textarea
  428:                 rows={3}
  429:                 value={reviewNote}
  430:                 onChange={(event) =>
  431:                   setReviewNote(event.target.value)
  432:                 }
  433:                 placeholder="Record why you are continuing, retargeting, closing or escalating the intervention."
  434:                 className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
  435:               />
  436:             </label>
  437: 
  438:             <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  439:               <button
  440:                 type="button"
  441:                 disabled={savingDecision}
  442:                 onClick={() =>
  443:                   void saveDecision(
  444:                     review.recommendedDecision,
  445:                   )
  446:                 }
  447:                 className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white disabled:opacity-60"
  448:               >
  449:                 <ClipboardCheck className="h-4 w-4" />
  450:                 {savingDecision
  451:                   ? "Saving..."
  452:                   : decisionLabel(
  453:                       review.recommendedDecision,
  454:                     )}
  455:               </button>
  456: 
  457:               {review.recommendedDecision !== "continue" && (
  458:                 <button
  459:                   type="button"
  460:                   disabled={savingDecision}
  461:                   onClick={() =>
  462:                     void saveDecision("continue")
  463:                   }
  464:                   className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 text-xs font-black text-slate-700 disabled:opacity-60"
  465:                 >
  466:                   Continue instead
  467:                 </button>
  468:               )}
  469: 
  470:               {review.canCloseSuccessful &&
  471:                 review.recommendedDecision !==
  472:                   "close_successful" && (
  473:                   <button
  474:                     type="button"
  475:                     disabled={savingDecision}
  476:                     onClick={() =>
  477:                       void saveDecision(
  478:                         "close_successful",
  479:                       )
  480:                     }
  481:                     className="inline-flex min-h-10 items-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white disabled:opacity-60"
  482:                   >
  483:                     Close as successful
  484:                   </button>
  485:                 )}
  486:             </div>
  487:           </div>
  488:         </Card>
  489:       )}
  490:     </div>
  491:   );
  492: }
  493: 
  494: function Metric({
  495:   label,
  496:   before,
  497:   after,
  498:   change,
  499: }: {
  500:   label: string;
  501:   before: string;
  502:   after: string;
  503:   change: string;
  504: }) {
  505:   return (
  506:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  507:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
  508:         {label}
  509:       </p>
  510: 
  511:       <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
  512:         {before} â†’
  513:       </p>
  514: 
  515:       <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-black text-slate-950">
  516:         {after}
  517:       </p>
  518: 
  519:       <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-black text-teal-700">
  520:         Change {change}
  521:       </p>
  522:     </div>
  523:   );
  524: }

FILE: hooks\useAdaptiveLearning.ts
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    5: import { getAdaptiveLearningPlan } from "@/services/adaptiveLearningService";
    6: import type { AdaptiveLearningPlan } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    7: 
    8: export function useAdaptiveLearning() {
    9:   const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
   10:   const [plan, setPlan] = useState<AdaptiveLearningPlan | null>(null);
   11:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   12:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   13: 
   14:   async function refresh() {
   15:     if (!user?.uid) {
   16:       setPlan(null);
   17:       setLoading(false);
   18:       return;
   19:     }
   20: 
   21:     setLoading(true);
   22:     setError("");
   23: 
   24:     try {
   25:       setPlan(await getAdaptiveLearningPlan(user.uid));
   26:     } catch (caughtError) {
   27:       console.error("Adaptive learning error:", caughtError);
   28:       setError(
   29:         caughtError instanceof Error
   30:           ? caughtError.message
   31:           : "The adaptive learning plan could not be loaded.",
   32:       );
   33:     } finally {
   34:       setLoading(false);
   35:     }
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   useEffect(() => {
   39:     if (!authLoading) void refresh();
   40:   }, [authLoading, user?.uid]);
   41: 
   42:   return {
   43:     plan,
   44:     loading: authLoading || loading,
   45:     error,
   46:     refresh,
   47:   };
   48: }

FILE: hooks\useStudentAdaptiveAnalytics.ts

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import {
    6:   emptyStudentAdaptiveAnalytics,
    7:   getStudentAdaptiveAnalytics,
    8:   type StudentAdaptiveAnalytics,
    9: } from "@/services/studentAdaptiveAnalyticsService";
   10: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   11: 
   12: export function useStudentAdaptiveAnalytics() {
   13:   const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
   14: 
   15:   const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState<StudentAdaptiveAnalytics>(
   16:     emptyStudentAdaptiveAnalytics,
   17:   );
   18: 
   19:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   20: 
   21:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   22: 
   23:   useEffect(() => {
   24:     if (authLoading) {
   25:       return;
   26:     }
   27: 
   28:     if (!user?.uid) {
   29:       setAnalytics(emptyStudentAdaptiveAnalytics);
   30:       setLoading(false);
   31:       return;
   32:     }
   33: 
   34:     let cancelled = false;
   35: 
   36:     async function load() {
   37:       try {
   38:         setLoading(true);
   39:         setError("");
   40: 
   41:         const loaded = await getStudentAdaptiveAnalytics(user!.uid);
   42: 
   43:         if (!cancelled) {
   44:           setAnalytics(loaded);
   45:         }
   46:       } catch (caughtError) {
   47:         console.error("Student adaptive analytics error:", caughtError);
   48: 
   49:         if (!cancelled) {
   50:           setAnalytics(emptyStudentAdaptiveAnalytics);
   51: 
   52:           setError(
   53:             caughtError instanceof Error
   54:               ? caughtError.message
   55:               : "Assessment analytics could not be loaded.",
   56:           );
   57:         }
   58:       } finally {
   59:         if (!cancelled) {
   60:           setLoading(false);
   61:         }
   62:       }
   63:     }
   64: 
   65:     void load();
   66: 
   67:     return () => {
   68:       cancelled = true;
   69:     };
   70:   }, [authLoading, user?.uid]);
   71: 
   72:   return {
   73:     analytics,
   74:     loading: authLoading || loading,
   75:     error,
   76:   };
   77: }

====================================================================================================
FILE: hooks\useStudents.ts
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: import { collection, getDocs } from "firebase/firestore";
    5: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    6: 
    7: export type StudentSummary = {
    8:   id: string;
    9:   name: string;
   10:   email: string;
   11:   xp: number;
   12:   streak: number;
   13:   badges: number;
   14:   completedLessons: number;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export function useStudents() {
   18:   const [students, setStudents] = useState<StudentSummary[]>([]);
   19:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   20: 
   21:   useEffect(() => {
   22:     async function loadStudents() {
   23:       try {
   24:         const snapshot = await getDocs(collection(db, "users"));
   25: 
   26:         const studentList: StudentSummary[] = snapshot.docs
   27:           .map((studentDoc) => {
   28:             const data = studentDoc.data();
   29: 
   30:             return {
   31:               id: studentDoc.id,
   32:               name: data.name || "Student",
   33:               email: data.email || "No email available",
   34:               xp: data.xp || 0,
   35:               streak: data.streak || 0,
   36:               badges: Array.isArray(data.badges) ? data.badges.length : 0,
   37:               completedLessons: Array.isArray(data.completedLessons)
   38:                 ? data.completedLessons.length
   39:                 : 0,
   40:               role: data.role,
   41:             };
   42:           })
   43:           .filter((student) => student.role === "student")
   44:           .map(({ role, ...student }) => student);
   45: 
   46:         studentList.sort((a, b) => b.xp - a.xp);
   47: 
   48:         setStudents(studentList);
   49:       } catch (error) {
   50:         console.error("Failed to load students:", error);
   51:       } finally {
   52:         setLoading(false);
   53:       }
   54:     }
   55: 
   56:     loadStudents();
   57:   }, []);
   58: 
   59:   return { students, loading };
   60: }

    1: "use client";

   20: export function useTeacherDashboard() {
   21:   const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
   22: 
   23:   const [data, setData] = useState<TeacherDashboardData>(
   24:     emptyTeacherDashboardData,
   25:   );
   26: 
   27:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   28: 
   29:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   30: 
   31:   useEffect(() => {
   32:     if (authLoading) {
   33:       return;
   34:     }
   35: 
   36:     if (!user?.uid) {
   37:       setData(emptyTeacherDashboardData);
   38: 
   39:       setError("");
   40:       setLoading(false);
   41: 
   42:       return;
   43:     }

   48:       try {
   49:         setLoading(true);
   50:         setError("");

   52:         const dashboardData = await getTeacherDashboardData(user!.uid);
   53: 
   54:         if (cancelled) {
   55:           return;
   56:         }
   57: 
   58:         setData(dashboardData);
   59:       } catch (caughtError) {
   60:         console.error("Teacher dashboard load error:", caughtError);
   61: 
   62:         if (cancelled) {
   63:           return;
   64:         }
   65: 
   66:         setData(emptyTeacherDashboardData);
   67: 
   68:         setError(
   69:           caughtError instanceof Error
   70:             ? caughtError.message
   71:             : "The teacher dashboard could not be loaded.",
   72:         );
   73:       } finally {
   74:         if (!cancelled) {
   75:           setLoading(false);
   76:         }
   77:       }
   78:     }
   79: 
   80:     void loadDashboard();
   81: 
   82:     return () => {
   83:       cancelled = true;
   84:     };
   85:   }, [authLoading, user?.uid]);
   86: 
   87:   return {
   88:     ...data,
   89:     loading: authLoading || loading,
   90:     error,
   91:   };
   92: }

FILE: hooks\useTeacherIntelligence.ts
====================================================================================================

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    6: import { getTeacherAnalyticsPortfolio } from "@/services/analytics/teacherAnalyticsService";
    7: import type { TeacherAnalyticsPortfolio } from "@/types/teacherAnalytics";
    8: 
    9: export function useTeacherIntelligence() {
   10:   const { user, profile, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
   11: 
   12:   const [portfolio, setPortfolio] =
   13:     useState<TeacherAnalyticsPortfolio | null>(null);
   14: 
   15:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   16:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   17: 
   18:   const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
   19:     if (
   20:       !user?.uid ||
   21:       (profile?.role !== "teacher" && profile?.role !== "admin")
   22:     ) {
   23:       setPortfolio(null);
   24:       setLoading(false);
   25:       return;
   26:     }
   27: 
   28:     try {
   29:       setLoading(true);
   30:       setError("");
   31: 
   32:       const result = await getTeacherAnalyticsPortfolio(user.uid);
   33: 
   34:       setPortfolio(result);
   35:     } catch (caughtError: unknown) {
   36:       const firebaseCode =
   37:         typeof caughtError === "object" &&
   38:         caughtError !== null &&
   39:         "code" in caughtError &&
   40:         typeof (caughtError as { code?: unknown }).code === "string"
   41:           ? (caughtError as { code: string }).code
   42:           : "";
   43: 
   44:       setPortfolio(null);
   45: 
   46:       if (
   47:         firebaseCode === "permission-denied" ||
   48:         firebaseCode === "firestore/permission-denied"
   49:       ) {
   50:         setError(
   51:           "Teacher intelligence is unavailable for this account.",
   52:         );
   53:       } else {
   54:         setError(
   55:           caughtError instanceof Error
   56:             ? caughtError.message
   57:             : "Teacher intelligence could not be loaded.",
   58:         );
   59:       }
   60:     } finally {
   61:       setLoading(false);
   62:     }
   63:   }, [profile?.role, user?.uid]);
   64: 
   65:   useEffect(() => {
   66:     if (authLoading) return;
   67: 
   68:     void refresh();
   69:   }, [authLoading, refresh]);
   70: 
   71:   return {
   72:     portfolio,
   73:     loading: authLoading || loading,
   74:     error,
   75:     refresh,
   76:   };
   77: }

   25: 
   26: type FirestoreIntegrityIncident = Omit<
   27:   ExamIntegrityIncident,
   28:   "occurredAt"
   29: > & {
   30:   occurredAt?: Timestamp;
   31: };
   32: 
   33: type FirestoreExamSubmission = Omit<
   34:   ExamSubmission,
   35:   | "id"
   36:   | "startedAt"
   37:   | "submittedAt"
   38:   | "markedAt"
   39:   | "updatedAt"
   40:   | "integritySessionStartedAt"
   41:   | "integrityIncidents"
   42: > & {
   43:   startedAt?: Timestamp;
   44:   submittedAt?: Timestamp;
   45:   markedAt?: Timestamp;
   46:   updatedAt?: Timestamp;
   47:   integritySessionStartedAt?: Timestamp;
   48:   integrityIncidents?: FirestoreIntegrityIncident[];
   49: };
   50: 
   51: function toDate(
   52:   value?: Timestamp | null,
   53: ): Date | null {
   54:   return value?.toDate
   55:     ? value.toDate()
   56:     : null;
   57: }
   58: 
   59: function convertIncident(
   60:   value: FirestoreIntegrityIncident,
   61: ): ExamIntegrityIncident {
   62:   return {
   63:     id: value.id,
   64:     type: value.type,
   65:     occurredAt: toDate(value.occurredAt),
   66:     questionNumber:
   67:       typeof value.questionNumber ===
   68:       "number"
   69:         ? value.questionNumber
   70:         : null,
   71:     detail: value.detail || "",
   72:   };
   73: }
   74: 
   75: export function createSubmissionId(
   76:   assignmentId: string,
   77:   studentId: string,
   78: ): string {
   79:   return `${assignmentId}_${studentId}`;
   80: }
   81: 
   82: function createAnswers(
   83:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
   84: ): StudentExamAnswer[] {
   85:   return assignment.questionSetSnapshot.questions.map(
   86:     (question) => ({
   87:       questionId: question.id,
   88:       questionNumber:
   89:         question.questionNumber,
   90:       response: "",
   91:       awardedMarks: null,
   92:       teacherFeedback: "",
   93:     }),
   94:   );
   95: }
   96: 
   97: function convertSubmission(
   98:   id: string,
   99:   data: FirestoreExamSubmission,
  100: ): ExamSubmission {
  101:   return {
  102:     id,
  103:     assignmentId: data.assignmentId,
  104:     studentId: data.studentId,
  105:     studentName:
  106:       data.studentName || "Student",
  107:     studentEmail:
  108:       data.studentEmail || "",
  109:     teacherId: data.teacherId,
  110:     classId: data.classId,
  111:     status:
  112:       data.status || "not_started",
  113:     answers: data.answers || [],
  114:     totalAwardedMarks:
  115:       data.totalAwardedMarks || 0,
  116:     totalAvailableMarks:
  117:       data.totalAvailableMarks || 0,
  118:     percentage: data.percentage || 0,
  119:     overallFeedback:
  120:       data.overallFeedback || "",
  121: 
  122:     integrityPolicySnapshot:
  123:       data.integrityPolicySnapshot || null,
  124: 
  125:     integrityIncidents:
  126:       Array.isArray(
  127:         data.integrityIncidents,
  128:       )
  129:         ? data.integrityIncidents.map(
  130:             convertIncident,
  131:           )
  132:         : [],
  133: 
  134:     integrityTerminated:
  135:       data.integrityTerminated === true,
  136: 
  137:     integrityTerminationReason:
  138:       data.integrityTerminationReason || "",
  139: 
  140:     integritySessionStartedAt:
  141:       toDate(
  142:         data.integritySessionStartedAt,
  143:       ),
  144: 
  145:     integrityLastQuestionNumber:
  146:       typeof data.integrityLastQuestionNumber ===
  147:       "number"
  148:         ? data.integrityLastQuestionNumber
  149:         : null,
  150: 
  151:     startedAt: toDate(data.startedAt),
  152:     submittedAt:
  153:       toDate(data.submittedAt),
  154:     markedAt: toDate(data.markedAt),
  155:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
  156:   };
  157: }
  158: 
  159: export async function getOrCreateExamSubmission({
  160:   assignment,

  163:   studentEmail,

  231:     studentEmail:
  232:       studentEmail?.trim() || "",

  234:     classId: assignment.classId,
  235:     status: "in_progress",
  236:     answers,
  237:     totalAwardedMarks: 0,
  238:     totalAvailableMarks:
  239:       assignment.totalMarks,
  240:     percentage: 0,
  241:     overallFeedback: "",
  242: 
  243:     integrityPolicySnapshot: null,
  244:     integrityIncidents: [],
  245:     integrityTerminated: false,
  246:     integrityTerminationReason: "",
  247:     integritySessionStartedAt: null,
  248:     integrityLastQuestionNumber: null,
  249: 
  250:     startedAt: new Date(),
  251:     submittedAt: null,
  252:     markedAt: null,
  253:     updatedAt: new Date(),
  254:   };

  282: export async function startExamIntegritySession({
  283:   assignment,
  284:   studentId,
  285: }: {
  286:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
  287:   studentId: string;
  288: }): Promise<void> {
  289:   const reference = doc(
  290:     db,
  291:     "examSubmissions",
  292:     createSubmissionId(
  293:       assignment.id,
  294:       studentId,
  295:     ),
  296:   );
  297: 
  298:   const snapshot =
  299:     await getDoc(reference);
  300: 
  301:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  302:     throw new Error(
  303:       "Submission not found.",
  304:     );
  305:   }
  306: 
  307:   const data = snapshot.data();
  308: 
  309:   if (
  310:     ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(
  311:       data.status,
  312:     )
  313:   ) {
  314:     return;
  315:   }
  316: 
  317:   const updates: Record<
  318:     string,
  319:     unknown
  320:   > = {
  321:     integrityPolicySnapshot:
  322:       assignment.integrityPolicy,
  323:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  324:   };
  325: 
  326:   if (
  327:     !data.integritySessionStartedAt
  328:   ) {
  329:     updates.integritySessionStartedAt =
  330:       serverTimestamp();
  331:   }
  332: 
  333:   await updateDoc(
  334:     reference,
  335:     updates,
  336:   );
  337: }

  339: function incidentId(
  340:   type: ExamIntegrityIncidentType,
  341: ): string {
  342:   return `${type}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
  343:     .toString(36)
  344:     .slice(2, 8)}`;

  347: export async function recordExamIntegrityIncident({
  348:   assignmentId,
  349:   studentId,
  350:   type,
  351:   questionNumber,
  352:   detail,
  353: }: {
  354:   assignmentId: string;
  355:   studentId: string;
  356:   type: ExamIntegrityIncidentType;
  357:   questionNumber: number | null;
  358:   detail: string;
  359: }): Promise<void> {
  360:   const reference = doc(
  361:     db,
  362:     "examSubmissions",
  363:     createSubmissionId(
  364:       assignmentId,
  365:       studentId,
  366:     ),
  367:   );
  368: 
  369:   const incident = {
  370:     id: incidentId(type),
  371:     type,
  372:     occurredAt: Timestamp.now(),
  373:     questionNumber:
  374:       questionNumber ?? null,
  375:     detail: detail.trim(),
  376:   };
  377: 
  378:   await updateDoc(reference, {
  379:     integrityIncidents:
  380:       arrayUnion(incident),
  381:     integrityLastQuestionNumber:
  382:       questionNumber ?? null,
  383:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  384:   });

  413: export async function terminateExamForIntegrity({
  414:   assignmentId,
  415:   studentId,
  416:   answers,
  417:   questionNumber,
  418:   reason,
  419: }: {
  420:   assignmentId: string;
  421:   studentId: string;
  422:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[];
  423:   questionNumber: number | null;
  424:   reason: string;
  425: }): Promise<void> {
  426:   const reference = doc(
  427:     db,
  428:     "examSubmissions",
  429:     createSubmissionId(
  430:       assignmentId,
  431:       studentId,
  432:     ),
  433:   );
  434: 
  435:   const terminationIncident = {
  436:     id: incidentId(
  437:       "integrity_termination",
  438:     ),
  439:     type:
  440:       "integrity_termination" as const,
  441:     occurredAt: Timestamp.now(),
  442:     questionNumber:
  443:       questionNumber ?? null,
  444:     detail: reason.trim(),
  445:   };
  446: 
  447:   await runTransaction(
  448:     db,
  449:     async (transaction) => {
  450:       const snapshot =
  451:         await transaction.get(reference);
  452: 
  453:       if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  454:         throw new Error(
  455:           "Submission not found.",
  456:         );
  457:       }
  458: 
  459:       const status =
  460:         snapshot.data().status;
  461: 
  462:       if (
  463:         ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(
  464:           status,
  465:         )
  466:       ) {
  467:         return;
  468:       }
  469: 
  470:       const currentIncidents =
  471:         Array.isArray(
  472:           snapshot.data()
  473:             .integrityIncidents,
  474:         )
  475:           ? snapshot.data()
  476:               .integrityIncidents
  477:           : [];
  478: 
  479:       transaction.update(
  480:         reference,
  481:         {
  482:           answers,
  483:           status: "submitted",
  484:           submittedAt:
  485:             serverTimestamp(),
  486: 
  487:           integrityTerminated: true,
  488:           integrityTerminationReason:
  489:             reason.trim(),
  490: 
  491:           integrityLastQuestionNumber:
  492:             questionNumber ?? null,
  493: 
  494:           integrityIncidents: [
  495:             ...currentIncidents,
  496:             terminationIncident,
  497:           ],
  498: 
  499:           updatedAt:
  500:             serverTimestamp(),
  501:         },
  502:       );
  503:     },
  504:   );
  505: }

  507: export async function autosaveExamAnswers(
  508:   assignmentId: string,
  509:   studentId: string,
  510:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[],
  511: ): Promise<void> {
  512:   const reference = doc(
  513:     db,
  514:     "examSubmissions",
  515:     createSubmissionId(
  516:       assignmentId,
  517:       studentId,
  518:     ),
  519:   );
  520: 
  521:   const snapshot =
  522:     await getDoc(reference);
  523: 
  524:   if (
  525:     snapshot.exists() &&
  526:     ["submitted", "marking", "marked"].includes(
  527:       snapshot.data().status,
  528:     )
  529:   ) {
  530:     throw new Error(
  531:       "This submission is locked.",
  532:     );
  533:   }
  534: 
  535:   await updateDoc(reference, {
  536:     answers,
  537:     status: "in_progress",
  538:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  539:   });
  540: }

  542: export async function submitExamSubmission(
  543:   assignmentId: string,
  544:   studentId: string,
  545: ): Promise<void> {
  546:   const submissionReference = doc(
  547:     db,
  548:     "examSubmissions",
  549:     createSubmissionId(
  550:       assignmentId,
  551:       studentId,
  552:     ),
  553:   );
  554: 
  555:   const snapshot =
  556:     await getDoc(
  557:       submissionReference,
  558:     );
  559: 
  560:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  561:     throw new Error(
  562:       "Submission not found.",
  563:     );
  564:   }
  565: 
  566:   const status =
  567:     snapshot.data().status;
  568: 
  569:   if (
  570:     status === "submitted" ||
  571:     status === "marking" ||
  572:     status === "marked"
  573:   ) {
  574:     return;
  575:   }
  576: 
  577:   await updateDoc(
  578:     submissionReference,
  579:     {
  580:       status: "submitted",
  581:       submittedAt:
  582:         serverTimestamp(),
  583:       updatedAt:
  584:         serverTimestamp(),
  585:     },
  586:   );
  587: }
  588: 
  589: export async function getAssignmentSubmissions(
  590:   assignmentId: string,
  591:   teacherId: string,
  592: ): Promise<ExamSubmission[]> {
  593:   const cleanedAssignmentId =
  594:     assignmentId.trim();
  595: 
  596:   const cleanedTeacherId =
  597:     teacherId.trim();
  598: 
  599:   if (
  600:     !cleanedAssignmentId ||
  601:     !cleanedTeacherId
  602:   ) {
  603:     return [];
  604:   }
  605: 
  606:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  607:     query(
  608:       collection(
  609:         db,
  610:         "examSubmissions",
  611:       ),
  612:       where(
  613:         "assignmentId",
  614:         "==",
  615:         cleanedAssignmentId,
  616:       ),
  617:       where(
  618:         "teacherId",
  619:         "==",
  620:         cleanedTeacherId,
  621:       ),
  622:     ),
  623:   );
  624: 
  625:   return snapshot.docs
  626:     .map((document) =>
  627:       convertSubmission(
  628:         document.id,
  629:         document.data() as FirestoreExamSubmission,
  630:       ),
  631:     )
  632:     .sort((first, second) =>
  633:       first.studentName.localeCompare(
  634:         second.studentName,
  635:         "en-GB",
  636:         {
  637:           sensitivity: "base",
  638:         },
  639:       ),
  640:     );
  641: }
  642: 
  643: export async function saveDraftMarking({
  644:   assignmentId,
  645:   studentId,
  646:   answers,
  647:   overallFeedback,
  648: }: {
  649:   assignmentId: string;
  650:   studentId: string;
  651:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[];
  652:   overallFeedback: string;
  653: }): Promise<void> {
  654:   const totalAwardedMarks =
  655:     answers.reduce(
  656:       (sum, answer) =>
  657:         sum +
  658:         (answer.awardedMarks || 0),
  659:       0,
  660:     );
  661: 
  662:   await updateDoc(
  663:     doc(
  664:       db,
  665:       "examSubmissions",
  666:       createSubmissionId(
  667:         assignmentId,
  668:         studentId,
  669:       ),
  670:     ),
  671:     {
  672:       answers,
  673:       overallFeedback:
  674:         overallFeedback.trim(),
  675:       totalAwardedMarks,
  676:       status: "marking",
  677:       updatedAt:
  678:         serverTimestamp(),
  679:     },
  680:   );
  681: }
  682: 
  683: export async function finaliseExamMarking({
  684:   assignmentId,
  685:   studentId,
  686:   answers,
  687:   overallFeedback,
  688:   totalAvailableMarks,
  689: }: {
  690:   assignmentId: string;
  691:   studentId: string;
  692:   answers: StudentExamAnswer[];
  693:   overallFeedback: string;
  694:   totalAvailableMarks: number;
  695: }): Promise<void> {
  696:   const assignmentReference = doc(
  697:     db,
  698:     "examAssignments",
  699:     assignmentId,
  700:   );
  701: 
  702:   const submissionReference = doc(
  703:     db,
  704:     "examSubmissions",
  705:     createSubmissionId(
  706:       assignmentId,
  707:       studentId,
  708:     ),
  709:   );
  710: 
  711:   const totalAwardedMarks =
  712:     answers.reduce(
  713:       (sum, answer) =>
  714:         sum +
  715:         (answer.awardedMarks ?? 0),
  716:       0,
  717:     );
  718: 
  719:   const safeAwardedMarks =
  720:     Math.min(
  721:       totalAvailableMarks,
  722:       Math.max(
  723:         0,
  724:         totalAwardedMarks,
  725:       ),
  726:     );
  727: 
  728:   const percentage =
  729:     totalAvailableMarks > 0
  730:       ? Math.round(
  731:           (safeAwardedMarks /
  732:             totalAvailableMarks) *
  733:             100,
  734:         )
  735:       : 0;
  736: 
  737:   await runTransaction(
  738:     db,
  739:     async (transaction) => {
  740:       const [
  741:         submissionSnapshot,
  742:         assignmentSnapshot,
  743:       ] = await Promise.all([
  744:         transaction.get(
  745:           submissionReference,
  746:         ),
  747:         transaction.get(
  748:           assignmentReference,
  749:         ),
  750:       ]);
  751: 
  752:       if (
  753:         !submissionSnapshot.exists()
  754:       ) {
  755:         throw new Error(
  756:           "Submission not found.",
  757:         );
  758:       }
  759: 
  760:       if (
  761:         !assignmentSnapshot.exists()
  762:       ) {
  763:         throw new Error(
  764:           "Assignment not found.",
  765:         );
  766:       }
  767: 
  768:       const submissionData =
  769:         submissionSnapshot.data();
  770: 
  771:       const assignmentData =
  772:         assignmentSnapshot.data();
  773: 
  774:       const alreadyMarked =
  775:         submissionData.status ===
  776:         "marked";
  777: 
  778:       transaction.update(
  779:         submissionReference,
  780:         {
  781:           answers,
  782:           overallFeedback:
  783:             overallFeedback.trim(),
  784:           totalAwardedMarks:
  785:             safeAwardedMarks,
  786:           totalAvailableMarks,
  787:           percentage,
  788:           status: "marked",
  789:           markedAt:
  790:             serverTimestamp(),
  791:           updatedAt:
  792:             serverTimestamp(),
  793:         },
  794:       );
  795: 
  796:       if (!alreadyMarked) {
  797:         const currentMarkedCount =
  798:           typeof assignmentData.markedCount ===
  799:           "number"
  800:             ? assignmentData.markedCount
  801:             : 0;
  802: 
  803:         const studentCount =
  804:           Array.isArray(
  805:             assignmentData.studentIds,
  806:           )
  807:             ? assignmentData
  808:                 .studentIds.length
  809:             : 0;
  810: 
  811:         transaction.update(
  812:           assignmentReference,
  813:           {
  814:             markedCount:
  815:               Math.min(
  816:                 currentMarkedCount +
  817:                   1,
  818:                 studentCount,
  819:               ),
  820:             updatedAt:
  821:               serverTimestamp(),
  822:           },
  823:         );
  824:       }
  825:     },
  826:   );
  827: }

FILE: services\quizService.ts
====================================================================================================

    1: import {
    2:   doc,
    3:   increment,
    4:   serverTimestamp,
    5:   setDoc,
    6:   writeBatch,
    7: } from "firebase/firestore";
    8: 
    9: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   10: 
   11: type SaveQuizResultParams = {
   12:   uid: string;
   13:   quizId: string;
   14:   topicId: string;
   15:   title: string;
   16:   scorePercent: number;
   17:   correctCount: number;
   18:   totalQuestions: number;
   19:   earnedXP: number;
   20: };
   21: 
   22: function createQuizResultId(quizId: string, uid: string): string {
   23:   const safeQuizId = quizId.trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "-");
   24: 
   25:   return `${safeQuizId}_${uid}`;
   26: }
   27: 
   28: export async function saveQuizResult({
   29:   uid,
   30:   quizId,
   31:   topicId,
   32:   title,
   33:   scorePercent,
   34:   correctCount,
   35:   totalQuestions,
   36:   earnedXP,
   37: }: SaveQuizResultParams): Promise<string> {
   38:   const cleanedUid = uid.trim();
   39:   const cleanedQuizId = quizId.trim();
   40: 
   41:   if (!cleanedUid) {
   42:     throw new Error("A valid student account is required.");
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   if (!cleanedQuizId) {
   46:     throw new Error("A valid quiz is required.");
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   const safeScorePercent = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(scorePercent)));
   50: 
   51:   const safeCorrectCount = Math.max(
   52:     0,
   53:     Math.min(Math.round(correctCount), Math.round(totalQuestions)),
   54:   );
   55: 
   56:   const safeTotalQuestions = Math.max(0, Math.round(totalQuestions));
   57: 
   58:   const safeEarnedXP = Math.max(0, Math.round(earnedXP));
   59: 
   60:   const resultId = createQuizResultId(cleanedQuizId, cleanedUid);
   61: 
   62:   const resultReference = doc(db, "users", cleanedUid, "quizResults", resultId);
   63: 
   64:   const userReference = doc(db, "users", cleanedUid);
   65: 
   66:   const batch = writeBatch(db);
   67: 
   68:   /*
   69:    * The uid field is required by the current
   70:    * Firestore security rules.
   71:    *
   72:    * A deterministic result ID prevents repeated
   73:    * page renders from creating duplicate quiz results.
   74:    */
   75:   batch.set(
   76:     resultReference,
   77:     {
   78:       uid: cleanedUid,
   79:       quizId: cleanedQuizId,
   80:       topicId: topicId.trim(),
   81:       title: title.trim() || "Untitled Quiz",
   82:       scorePercent: safeScorePercent,
   83:       correctCount: safeCorrectCount,
   84:       totalQuestions: safeTotalQuestions,
   85:       earnedXP: safeEarnedXP,
   86:       status: "completed",
   87:       completedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   88:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   89:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   90:     },
   91:     {
   92:       merge: true,
   93:     },
   94:   );
   95: 
   96:   /*
   97:    * Award XP once for this save operation.
   98:    */
   99:   batch.update(userReference, {
  100:     xp: increment(safeEarnedXP),
  101:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  102:   });
  103: 
  104:   await batch.commit();
  105: 
  106:   return resultId;
  107: }

  131: export const emptyTeacherDashboardData: TeacherDashboardData = {
  132:   studentCount: 0,
  133:   classCount: 0,
  134:   assignmentCount: 0,
  135:   activeAssignmentCount: 0,
  136:   examAssignmentCount: 0,
  137:   awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  138:   averageScore: 0,
  139:   quizAverage: 0,
  140:   examAverage: 0,
  141:   completionRate: 0,
  142:   lessonsCompleted: 0,
  143:   completedToday: 0,
  144:   recentActivities: [],
  145:   atRiskStudents: [],
  146:   topStudents: [],
