  149: 
  150: function safeNumber(value: unknown): number {
  151:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
  152: }
  153: 
  154: function safeString(value: unknown, fallback = ""): string {
  155:   return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : fallback;
  156: }
  157: 
  158: function safeStringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
  159:   return Array.isArray(value)
  160:     ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
  161:     : [];
  162: }
  163: 
  164: function formatActivityTime(timestamp?: Timestamp): string {
  165:   if (!timestamp) {
  166:     return "Recently";
  167:   }
  168: 
  169:   const activityDate = timestamp.toDate();
  170: 
  171:   const difference = Date.now() - activityDate.getTime();
  172: 
  173:   const minutes = Math.floor(difference / 60000);
  174: 
  175:   if (minutes < 1) {
  176:     return "Just now";
  177:   }
  178: 
  179:   if (minutes < 60) {
  180:     return `${minutes} minute${minutes === 1 ? "" : "s"} ago`;
  181:   }
  182: 
  183:   const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  184: 
  185:   if (hours < 24) {
  186:     return `${hours} hour${hours === 1 ? "" : "s"} ago`;
  187:   }
  188: 
  189:   const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
  190: 
  191:   return `${days} day${days === 1 ? "" : "s"} ago`;
  192: }
  193: 
  194: function isToday(timestamp?: Timestamp): boolean {
  195:   if (!timestamp) {
  196:     return false;
  197:   }
  198: 
  199:   const date = timestamp.toDate();
  200: 
  201:   const today = new Date();
  202: 
  203:   return (
  204:     date.getFullYear() === today.getFullYear() &&
  205:     date.getMonth() === today.getMonth() &&
  206:     date.getDate() === today.getDate()
  207:   );
  208: }
  209: 
  210: function calculateAverage(values: number[]): number {
  211:   if (values.length === 0) {
  212:     return 0;
  213:   }
  214: 
  215:   return Math.round(
  216:     values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length,
  217:   );
  218: }
  219: 
  220: export async function getTeacherDashboardData(
  221:   teacherId: string,
  222: ): Promise<TeacherDashboardData> {
  223:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  224: 
  225:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  226:     return emptyTeacherDashboardData;
  227:   }
  228: 
  229:   const [
  230:     classesSnapshot,
  231:     assignmentsSnapshot,
  232:     assignmentResultsSnapshot,
  233:     resourceAssignmentsSnapshot,
  234:     examAssignmentsSnapshot,
  235:     examSubmissionsSnapshot,
  236:   ] = await Promise.all([
  237:     getDocs(
  238:       query(
  239:         collection(db, "classes"),
  240:         where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  241:       ),
  242:     ),
  243: 
  244:     getDocs(
  245:       query(
  246:         collection(db, "assignments"),
  247:         where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  248:       ),
  249:     ),
  250: 
  251:     getDocs(
  252:       query(
  253:         collection(db, "assignmentResults"),
  254:         where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  255:       ),
  256:     ),
  257: 
  258:     getDocs(
  259:       query(
  260:         collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  261:         where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  262:       ),
  263:     ),
  264: 
  265:     getDocs(
  266:       query(
  267:         collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  268:         where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  269:       ),
  270:     ),
  271: 
  272:     getDocs(
  273:       query(
  274:         collection(db, "examSubmissions"),
  275:         where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  276:       ),
  277:     ),
  278:   ]);
  279: 
  280:   /*
  281:    * DATA ISOLATION:
  282:    *
  283:    * A teacher's dashboard must be built only from students explicitly
  284:    * enrolled in classes owned by that teacher.
  285:    *
  286:    * Never fall back to every platform student when a teacher has no classes.
  287:    * That would expose another teacher's learners and evidence.
  288:    */
  289:   const teacherStudentIds = new Set<string>();
  290: 
  291:   classesSnapshot.docs.forEach((classDocument) => {
  292:     const data = classDocument.data();
  293: 
  294:     safeStringArray(data.studentIds).forEach((studentId) => {
  295:       teacherStudentIds.add(studentId);
  296:     });
  297:   });
  298: 
  299:   const studentSnapshots = await Promise.all(
  300:     Array.from(teacherStudentIds).map((studentId) =>
  301:       getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId)),
  302:     ),
  303:   );
  304: 
  305:   const relevantStudents: UserRecord[] = studentSnapshots
  306:     .filter((studentSnapshot) => studentSnapshot.exists())
  307:     .map((studentSnapshot) => {
  308:       const data = studentSnapshot.data();
  309: 
  310:       return {
  311:         id: studentSnapshot.id,
  312:         name: safeString(data.name, "Student"),
  313:         role: safeString(data.role, "student"),
  314:         xp: safeNumber(data.xp),
  315:         streak: safeNumber(data.streak),
  316:         badges: safeStringArray(data.badges),
  317:         completedLessons: safeStringArray(data.completedLessons),
  318:       };
  319:     })
  320:     .filter((student) => student.role === "student");
  321: 
  322:   const relevantStudentIds = new Set(
  323:     relevantStudents.map((student) => student.id),
  324:   );
  325: 
  326:   /*
  327:    * Fetch quiz evidence only from students who belong to this teacher's
  328:    * classes. This avoids downloading platform-wide quiz results and then
  329:    * filtering them in the browser.
  330:    */
  331:   const quizResultSnapshots = await Promise.all(
  332:     Array.from(relevantStudentIds).map((studentId) =>
  333:       getDocs(collection(db, "users", studentId, "quizResults")),
  334:     ),
  335:   );
  336: 
  337:   const quizResultDocuments = quizResultSnapshots.flatMap(
  338:     (snapshot) => snapshot.docs,
  339:   );
  340: 
  341:   const assignments: AssignmentRecord[] = assignmentsSnapshot.docs.map(
  342:     (assignmentDocument) => {
  343:       const data = assignmentDocument.data();
  344: 
  345:       return {
  346:         id: assignmentDocument.id,
  347:         status: safeString(data.status, "active"),
  348:       };
  349:     },
  350:   );
  351: 
  352:   const resourceAssignments: ResourceAssignmentRecord[] =
  353:     resourceAssignmentsSnapshot.docs.map((assignmentDocument) => {
  354:       const data = assignmentDocument.data();
  355:       const studentIds = safeStringArray(data.studentIds).filter((studentId) =>
  356:         relevantStudentIds.has(studentId),
  357:       );
  358: 
  359:       const storedStudentCount = safeNumber(data.studentCount);
  360:       const storedCompletedCount = safeNumber(data.completedCount);
  361: 
  362:       return {
  363:         id: assignmentDocument.id,
  364:         status: safeString(data.status, "active"),
  365:         studentIds,
  366:         studentCount:
  367:           storedStudentCount > 0 ? storedStudentCount : studentIds.length,
  368:         completedCount: Math.min(
  369:           storedCompletedCount,
  370:           storedStudentCount > 0 ? storedStudentCount : studentIds.length,
  371:         ),
  372:       };
  373:     });
  374: 
  375:   /*
  376:    * Resource/lesson assignments store per-student completion in
  377:    * assignmentProgress/{assignmentId}_{studentId}.
  378:    *
  379:    * Read only the expected progress documents for this teacher's own
  380:    * assignments and currently enrolled learners. This keeps the dashboard
  381:    * isolated and lets Completed Today include lesson/resource completions.
  382:    */
  383:   const resourceProgressSnapshots = await Promise.all(
  384:     resourceAssignments.flatMap((assignment) =>
  385:       assignment.studentIds.map((studentId) =>
  386:         getDoc(
  387:           doc(
  388:             db,
  389:             "assignmentProgress",
  390:             `${assignment.id}_${studentId}`,
  391:           ),
  392:         ),
  393:       ),
  394:     ),
  395:   );
  396: 
  397:   const resourceAssignmentProgress: ResourceAssignmentProgressRecord[] =
  398:     resourceProgressSnapshots
  399:       .filter((progressSnapshot) => progressSnapshot.exists())
  400:       .map((progressSnapshot) => {
  401:         const data = progressSnapshot.data();
  402: 
  403:         return {
  404:           id: progressSnapshot.id,
  405:           assignmentId: safeString(data.assignmentId),
  406:           studentId: safeString(data.studentId),
  407:           status: safeString(data.status, "not_started"),
  408:           completedAt:
  409:             data.completedAt instanceof Timestamp
  410:               ? data.completedAt
  411:               : undefined,
  412:         };
  413:       })
  414:       .filter(
  415:         (progress) =>
  416:           relevantStudentIds.has(progress.studentId) &&
  417:           resourceAssignments.some(
  418:             (assignment) => assignment.id === progress.assignmentId,
  419:           ),
  420:       );
  421: 
  422:   const assignmentResults: AssignmentResultRecord[] =
  423:     assignmentResultsSnapshot.docs
  424:       .map((resultDocument) => {
  425:         const data = resultDocument.data();
  426: 
  427:         return {
  428:           id: resultDocument.id,
  429:           studentId: safeString(data.studentId),
  430:           assignmentId: safeString(data.assignmentId),
  431:           percentage: safeNumber(data.percentage),
  432:           status: safeString(data.status, "completed"),
  433:           completedAt:
  434:             data.completedAt instanceof Timestamp
  435:               ? data.completedAt
  436:               : undefined,
  437:         };
  438:       })
  439:       .filter((result) => relevantStudentIds.has(result.studentId));
  440: 
  441:   const quizResults: QuizResultRecord[] = quizResultDocuments
  442:     .map((resultDocument) => {
  443:       const data = resultDocument.data();
  444: 
  445:       return {
  446:         id: resultDocument.id,
  447:         uid: safeString(data.uid) || resultDocument.ref.parent.parent?.id || "",
  448:         studentName: safeString(data.studentName),
  449:         title: safeString(data.title, "Quiz"),
  450:         topicId: safeString(data.topicId, "Other"),
  451:         scorePercent: safeNumber(data.scorePercent),
  452:         createdAt:
  453:           data.createdAt instanceof Timestamp ? data.createdAt : undefined,
  454:       };
  455:     })
  456:     .filter(
  457:       (result) =>
  458:         Boolean(result.uid) &&
  459:         relevantStudentIds.has(result.uid),
  460:     );
  461: 
  462:   const examAssignments: ExamAssignmentRecord[] =
  463:     examAssignmentsSnapshot.docs.map((assignmentDocument) => {
  464:       const data = assignmentDocument.data();
  465: 
  466:       return {
  467:         id: assignmentDocument.id,
  468:         status: safeString(data.status, "active"),
  469:         studentIds: safeStringArray(data.studentIds),
  470:       };
  471:     });
  472: 
  473:   const examAssignmentIdSet = new Set(
  474:     examAssignments.map((assignment) => assignment.id),
  475:   );
  476: 
  477:   const examSubmissions: ExamSubmissionRecord[] = examSubmissionsSnapshot.docs
  478:     .map((submissionDocument) => {
  479:       const data = submissionDocument.data();
  480: 
  481:       return {
  482:         id: submissionDocument.id,
  483:         studentId: safeString(data.studentId),
  484:         studentName: safeString(data.studentName),
  485:         assignmentId: safeString(data.assignmentId),
  486:         status: safeString(data.status, "not_started"),
  487:         percentage: safeNumber(data.percentage),
  488:         topic: safeString(data.topic, "Written assessment"),
  489:         markedAt:
  490:           data.markedAt instanceof Timestamp ? data.markedAt : undefined,
  491:         submittedAt:
  492:           data.submittedAt instanceof Timestamp ? data.submittedAt : undefined,
  493:       };
  494:     })
  495:     .filter(
  496:       (submission) =>
  497:         relevantStudentIds.has(submission.studentId) &&
  498:         examAssignmentIdSet.has(submission.assignmentId),
  499:     );
  500: 
  501:   const markedExamSubmissions = examSubmissions.filter(
  502:     (submission) => submission.status === "marked",
  503:   );
  504: 
  505:   const awaitingMarkingCount = examSubmissions.filter(
  506:     (submission) =>
  507:       submission.status === "submitted" || submission.status === "marking",
  508:   ).length;
  509: 
  510:   const quizScores = quizResults.map((result) => result.scorePercent);
  511: 
  512:   const examScores = markedExamSubmissions.map(
  513:     (submission) => submission.percentage,
  514:   );
  515: 
  516:   const quizAverage = calculateAverage(quizScores);
  517: 
  518:   const examAverage = calculateAverage(examScores);
  519: 
  520:   const averageScore = calculateAverage([...quizScores, ...examScores]);
  521: 
  522:   const lessonsCompleted = relevantStudents.reduce(
  523:     (total, student) => total + student.completedLessons.length,
  524:     0,
  525:   );
  526: 
  527:   const expectedQuizSubmissions = assignments.length * relevantStudents.length;
  528: 
  529:   const expectedResourceCompletions = resourceAssignments.reduce(
  530:     (total, assignment) => total + assignment.studentCount,
  531:     0,
  532:   );
  533: 
  534:   const completedResourceCompletions = resourceAssignments.reduce(
  535:     (total, assignment) => total + assignment.completedCount,
  536:     0,
  537:   );
  538: 
  539:   const expectedExamSubmissions = examAssignments.reduce(
  540:     (total, assignment) =>
  541:       total +
  542:       assignment.studentIds.filter((studentId) =>
  543:         relevantStudentIds.has(studentId),
  544:       ).length,
  545:     0,
  546:   );
  547: 
  548:   const completedAssignmentResults = assignmentResults.filter(
  549:     (result) => result.status === "completed",
  550:   );
  551: 
  552:   const completedExamSubmissions = examSubmissions.filter(
  553:     (submission) => submission.status === "marked",
  554:   );
  555: 
  556:   const expectedSubmissions =
  557:     expectedQuizSubmissions +
  558:     expectedResourceCompletions +
  559:     expectedExamSubmissions;
  560: 
  561:   const completedSubmissions =
  562:     completedAssignmentResults.length +
  563:     completedResourceCompletions +
  564:     completedExamSubmissions.length;
  565: 
  566:   const completionRate =
  567:     expectedSubmissions > 0
  568:       ? Math.min(
  569:           100,
  570:           Math.round((completedSubmissions / expectedSubmissions) * 100),
  571:         )
  572:       : 0;
  573: 
  574:   const completedResourceProgressToday = resourceAssignmentProgress.filter(
  575:     (progress) =>
  576:       progress.status === "completed" &&
  577:       isToday(progress.completedAt),
  578:   ).length;
  579: 
  580:   const completedToday =
  581:     completedAssignmentResults.filter((result) => isToday(result.completedAt))
  582:       .length +
  583:     completedResourceProgressToday +
  584:     completedExamSubmissions.filter((submission) =>
  585:       isToday(submission.markedAt),
  586:     ).length;
  587: 
  588:   const topStudents: TopStudent[] = [...relevantStudents]
  589:     .sort((first, second) => second.xp - first.xp)
  590:     .slice(0, 5)
  591:     .map((student) => ({
  592:       id: student.id,
  593:       name: student.name,
  594:       xp: student.xp,
  595:       streak: student.streak,
  596:       badges: student.badges.length,
  597:     }));
  598: 
  599:   const quizActivities: RecentTeacherActivity[] = quizResults
  600:     .slice(0, 8)
  601:     .map((result) => {
  602:       const matchedStudent = relevantStudents.find(
  603:         (student) => student.id === result.uid,
  604:       );
  605: 
  606:       return {
  607:         id: `quiz-${result.id}`,
  608:         studentName: result.studentName || matchedStudent?.name || "Student",
  609:         activity: `Completed ${result.title}`,
  610:         result: `${result.scorePercent}%`,
  611:         time: formatActivityTime(result.createdAt),
  612:         type: "quiz" as const,
  613:       };
  614:     });
  615: 
  616:   const examActivities: RecentTeacherActivity[] = markedExamSubmissions.map(
  617:     (submission) => {
  618:       const matchedStudent = relevantStudents.find(
  619:         (student) => student.id === submission.studentId,
  620:       );
  621: 
  622:       return {
  623:         id: `exam-${submission.id}`,
  624:         studentName:
  625:           submission.studentName || matchedStudent?.name || "Student",
  626:         activity: "Completed written assessment",
  627:         result: `${submission.percentage}%`,
  628:         time: formatActivityTime(submission.markedAt || submission.submittedAt),
  629:         type: "exam" as const,
  630:       };
  631:     },
  632:   );
  633: 
  634:   const recentActivities = [...quizActivities, ...examActivities].slice(0, 8);
  635: 
  636:   const scoresByStudent = new Map<string, number[]>();
  637: 
  638:   quizResults.forEach((result) => {
  639:     if (!result.uid) {
  640:       return;
  641:     }
  642: 
  643:     const scores = scoresByStudent.get(result.uid) || [];
  644: 
  645:     scores.push(result.scorePercent);
  646: 
  647:     scoresByStudent.set(result.uid, scores);
  648:   });
  649: 
  650:   markedExamSubmissions.forEach((submission) => {
  651:     const scores = scoresByStudent.get(submission.studentId) || [];
  652: 
  653:     scores.push(submission.percentage);
  654: 
  655:     scoresByStudent.set(submission.studentId, scores);
  656:   });
  657: 
  658:   const atRiskStudents: AtRiskStudent[] = relevantStudents
  659:     .map((student) => {
  660:       const scores = scoresByStudent.get(student.id) || [];
  661: 
  662:       return {
  663:         id: student.id,
  664:         name: student.name,
  665:         weakTopic: "Combined Assessment Performance",
  666:         averageScore: calculateAverage(scores),
  667:         recommendedAction:
  668:           "Review quiz and written exam evidence, then assign targeted revision.",
  669:         hasResults: scores.length > 0,
  670:       };
  671:     })
  672:     .filter((student) => student.hasResults && student.averageScore < 50)
  673:     .sort((first, second) => first.averageScore - second.averageScore)
  674:     .slice(0, 5)
  675:     .map(({ hasResults: _hasResults, ...student }) => student);
  676: 
  677:   const topicGroups = new Map<string, number[]>();
  678: 
  679:   quizResults.forEach((result) => {
  680:     const topic = result.title || result.topicId || "Other";
  681: 
  682:     const scores = topicGroups.get(topic) || [];
  683: 
  684:     scores.push(result.scorePercent);
  685: 
  686:     topicGroups.set(topic, scores);
  687:   });
  688: 
  689:   markedExamSubmissions.forEach((submission) => {
  690:     const topic = submission.topic || "Written assessment";
  691: 
  692:     const scores = topicGroups.get(topic) || [];
  693: 
  694:     scores.push(submission.percentage);
  695: 
  696:     topicGroups.set(topic, scores);
  697:   });
  698: 
  699:   const classPerformance: TopicPerformance[] = Array.from(topicGroups.entries())
  700:     .map(([topic, scores], index) => ({
  701:       id: `topic-${index}`,
  702:       topic,
  703:       averageScore: calculateAverage(scores),
  704:     }))
  705:     .sort((first, second) => second.averageScore - first.averageScore);
  706: 
  707:   return {
  708:     studentCount: relevantStudents.length,
  709:     classCount: classesSnapshot.size,
  710:     assignmentCount:
  711:       assignments.length + resourceAssignments.length + examAssignments.length,
  712:     activeAssignmentCount:
  713:       assignments.filter((assignment) => assignment.status === "active")
  714:         .length +
  715:       resourceAssignments.filter((assignment) => assignment.status === "active")
  716:         .length +
  717:       examAssignments.filter((assignment) => assignment.status === "active")
  718:         .length,
  719:     examAssignmentCount: examAssignments.length,
  720:     awaitingMarkingCount,
  721:     averageScore,
  722:     quizAverage,
  723:     examAverage,
  724:     completionRate,
  725:     lessonsCompleted,
  726:     completedToday,
  727:     recentActivities,
  728:     atRiskStudents,
  729:     topStudents,
  730:     classPerformance,
  731:   };
  732: }
